Chapter 2651: The Punch of the Most Powerful Soundwaves!

Li Yao's brain-penetrating singing flooded into Wuying Qi's remaining soul.

Blackstar the Great had been through tens of thousands of battles in the past thousand years. He even managed to negotiate with and trick the Blood God, who used to be the greatest devil in the universe. However, he had never seen anything as outrageous as this!

Two things that happened today were beyond his wildest imagination.

The first thing was that his almost faultless Next Sun Plan was disrupted by Li Yao with the most disgusting pictures.

The second thing was that Li Yao was such a terrible singer. He had never heard such horrible singing!

"Stop! Stop now!"

Wuying Qi felt that abnormal bubbles were popping up from the depths of his brain, making his soul explode. He held his head and could not control the Colossus at all. He could only roar, "What song is this? It's torturing! Stop! I told you to stop!"

"That's impossible. Even the darkness and coldness of the whole universe cannot stop my fiery song!"

Li Yao kept singing with a low voice, "Mom is the best in the world. Where can you find happiness except in mom's arms?"

The singing was louder and louder, and it was more and more earsplitting and unpleasant. Bright gold blood flowed out of Wuying Qi's eyes, nose, ears, and mouth. His long hair that just turned crimson was showing gold too. Even his bloodshot eyes were emitting gold. A certain power was waking up inside the body and trying to break free.

"That's impossible…"

Wuying Qi roared in pain, "How… How can my cause be defeated by an insignificant song? It's impossible!"

"You still haven't realized what's going on? What defeats you is not the song, but the feelings of mankind. The thing that you and the Covenant Alliance ignored is the most precious and powerful weapon. It's what makes a human a human, and what makes a civilization a civilization!"

Moved by his own song, Li Yao shed hot tears too. He roared at Li Jialing who was suppressed by Wuying Qi's soul. "Jialing, do you hear my song? I'm singing this to you on behalf of your mother. She just told me a lot of things that she wanted you to hear!

"She said that she was wrong. She regretted many things, especially the bad things that Wuying Qi forced her to do, but there were two things that she didn't regret; to fall in love with your father and to give birth to you!

"She said that she loved you very much. She never met you because she feared that Wuying Qi would notice you. She said that she would 'dispose of' you because she wanted you to flee and live a peaceful life elsewhere!

"She said that she was deceived and threatened by Wuying Qi with all her children and that she had no choice except to work with Wuying Qi. However, not a day went by that she did not regret and fear. She felt that she did not deserve to be your mother. That's why she covered herself with a cold shell after she met you so that her passionate love could be disguised!

"She said that the biggest wish of her life was to hear you call her 'mother' and to hold you in her arms so that she could rub your hair!

"I'm definitely not lying to you. Although those were not her exact words, it was absolutely her meaning between the lines. I've merely restated it in my own way.

"If you want to hear her exact words, if you want to listen to her in person, just wake up, get your body back, and kill this scumbag!"

Wuying Qi and Li Jialing's body cramped violently.

Ten thousand gold, red, and black electric arcs were wandering crazily on the body, making him jump up and down. The struggle was a hundred times more intense than the battle between Wuying Qi and Li Linghai.

His fur was now red and now black, until a gold fire arose and burned up all the hair. He then recovered the appearance of a human being.

Tears of blood with gold glimmers flowed from his eyes. Screaming black air was spurting out of all his pores, as if an evil power was being pressed out of his body.

"That's impossible! You're in the Nascent Soul Stage at best. How can you defeat a soul in the Divinity Branching Stage?"

Wuying Qi and Li Jialing's face was now resolute, now angry, now confused, and now panicked. "How can you possibly get rid of my suppression?"

"Idiot, souls might have different levels, but feelings, the noblest of all things, do not. Even if you can control all the stars in the universe, you cannot completely deprive a son of his love for his mother!"

Li Yao took another deep breath. Exploiting the spiritual energy in every cell, he gathered it in his chest and his throat. His neck was three times thicker as he sang like a crystal bomb, "Jialing, let me upgrade my battle song and give you another hand!

"Under the silent stars in the sky, the kid is missing his mom. The blinking stars remind him of his mother's tears…"

"Pu!"

In Blackhole's Heart, Wuying Qi spewed out a mouthful of blood and black gas, and his chest collapsed at a visible speed.

"Stop singing. I'm begging you, please stop…"

He had lost the domination and madness of Blackstar the Great, and he was screaming like a dying old man.

"Li… Li Yao, I really think you should stop singing!"

Long Yangjun could barely keep Heavenly Crystal steady not far away. "Your mental attack is seriously interfering with me too. I find it impossible to lock onto the target. I-I'm about to throw up too. Do you really not know what a bad singer you are? You're causing as much damage to enemies as you do to allies!"

Li Linghai, who was meditating and recovering, slightly rolled her eyes. Her eyelashes were shivering, and her brainwaves were in a mess under Li Yao's song. There was no telling whether she was moved or she was simply disgusted.

"I… I… Cough, cough, cough, cough!"

His vocal cord was almost ruined by the two songs. Li Yao, failing to catch his breath, coughed hard. He could barely stand his song too.

"I told you to stop!"

Wuying Qi was already dying, but he reignited a dark fire when Li Yao vomited blood and blew up the super awesome stereo around Arsonist with the last bit of strength in Blackhole's Heart!

However, the broken stereo and the pieces of the broadcasting rune arrays were gathered on Arsonist's broken arms. Centered at the elbows, they turned into two clusters of high-energy metal torrents!

Boom!

Li Yao activated the power that was tearing his chest apart and closed his arms, combining the two torrents. His spiritual energy, his blood, his strength, his willpower, his soul, and his life were all sealed in. The torrents were immediately heated to thousands of degrees, and the emotions in them were even hotter.

"Mom!"

Li Yao's song burst out to a whole new level. The whole Gold Crystal Pyramid was shaking under his song and his emotions, causing infinite rune arrays to echo.

The song and the echo allowed the torrential emotions to soar to the ground, to the sky, and to the universe through the Gold Crystal Pyramid, before they burst out in the ears and the minds of all the people in the Empyreal Terminus Sector who had been maddened.

"Mom!"

Both the cruel and heartless Immortal Cultivators and the Demonic Cultivators who were killing crazily had mothers who gave birth to them and raised them.

Even if they became notorious and unforgivable villains later, they all had beautiful and precious memories with their mothers when they were crying toddlers and innocent kids.

The memories might be what was known as humanity.

The sun, another mother who raised lives and civilizations, was still releasing light and heat selflessly. She also brought the radiance of humanity to everybody on the capital planet and in the space battlefield who were on the verge of collapse.

For a moment, all the frantic people stopped slaughtering and sabotaging and listened to the song in the deepest part of their hearts.

"Oh, mother!"

Li Yao poured the loudest song into the metal torrents and bombarded it at the center of Blackhole's Heart's chest. "Mother in candlelight!"

Boom! Crack!

The breastplate of the legendary Blackhole's Heart was finally pierced through by Li Yao's punch of soundwaves.

The destructive mental power immediately filled the spiritual residence and cleansed Li Jialing's body… and eardrums.

Wuying Qi and Li Jialing burst out a devastating scream. His fingers were buried in his hair, and his body bent into a bow that was about to break. Infinite black fire and bloody fog squirted out.

His eyes turned into stubborn gold again, filled with the determination and fury of a young man.

The black fire and bloody fog, however, circled around him and formed a blurry gigantic shadow that was still struggling.

The shadow was exactly Wuying Qi's remaining soul.

He had been pushed out by Li Yao's song and Li Jialing's willpower again!

Chapter 2652: The Palace of Memories Near Death

"Stop… Stop singing!"

Li Jialing waved his hand at Li Yao, with pain and weakness all over his face. "It's me, Brother Yao. I'm perfectly fine now and have completely taken my body back. Please stop singing!"

"Jialing!"

Overjoyed, Li Yao hurried to stop. "Alright, alright, I'll stop singing. To be honest, I can't tolerate my singing anymore either…"

The twisted shadow made of blood and black fog, however, struggled to crawl back into Li Jialing's body.

However, Li Jialing's willpower was gathered into a gold fire that prevented Wuying Qi's second invasion. He was even swallowing Wuying Qi's remaining soul.

Possession was always dangerous. Once the owner of the body was awakened, they would likely swallow the power of the possessor. Li Yao had exactly absorbed Ou Yezi's memories and power in such a way.

"You want to go back again? Not a chance!"

Li Yao had gone through all the trouble to push Wuying Qi's soul out. He certainly would not allow Wuying Qi to return to Li Jialing's body. He simply gathered his mental power into a gigantic hammer and smashed Wuying Qi's remaining soul. "Get this!"

Boom!

As if a gigantic bell had been knocked, Li Yao's soul and Wuying Qi's had such an intense collision that visible ripples spread out.

There was a gap between their soul power, but Li Yao's soul was protected by his original body, whereas Wuying Qi's soul was exposed to the air. Their collision was like an armored train crashing a common shuttle. It was not hard to imagine which of them was more wounded.

Li Yao's soul was knocked back into his brain, but Wuying Qi's soul was shattered like a cloud under the wind and could not regather at all.

Li Jialing's brainwaves that were released nonstop were also cutting the cloud like sunlight.

"Ah! Ah! Ah!"

Wuying Qi had never been as desperate. His broken soul twisted and struggled. He tried to regather but was cut by Li Jialing. He tried to flee but was stopped by Li Yao.

Long Yangjun also seized the opportunity to launch the last strike. A hundred tiny crystal needles were darted into his remaining soul.

"I…"

The insane man of the time had finally reached his end. "I" echoed for a long time in the remaining soul, but he could not continue at all. It seemed that he would soon be dead for good.

However, the floating cloud suddenly constricted into a hundred round and deep drops, exactly like what Li Yao had seen in his illusion.

"Not good!"

Li Yao's face changed greatly, as he thought of the most horrifying possibility.

Since the Next Sun Plan failed, Wuying Qi had suffered consecutive setbacks and lost all chances of a comeback now.

However, he still had one last option, which was to detonate his last mental power and to die together with Li Yao and Li Jialing.

Back in the Seven Seas Grand Market, he had remotely controlled Wuying Qinxin to blow up her head, which nearly killed Li Yao. Also, he did not have a real head right now, but his remaining soul was much more powerful than Wuying Qinxin.

"Watch out!"

Li Yao reacted quickly and stood before Li Jialing. His soul was far more powerful than Li Jialing's. There was no reason why he would let his little brother take the blow of Wuying Qi's suicide attack.

Boom!

Before Li Yao was able to cover Li Jialing, the hundred black water drops where the power of Wuying Qi's remaining soul was focused had exploded into a black storm that swept across everything!

Li Yao, in the lead, was involved in the turbulence of Wuying Qi's violent consciousness. He sensed Wuying Qi's soul, belief, and memories like a tiny boat floating amid the surging tides.

Reality disappeared again. He was embraced by the memory pieces that were even more real than reality, as if he were visiting different worlds.

They were Wuying Qi's memories.

Wuying Qi's memories pierced through Li Yao's head like scorching bullets, allowing Li Yao to experience his life from his perspective. It was exactly like when he accepted Ou Yezi's memories.

He saw that he was inspecting a boundless fleet from a gargantuan space fortress. At the head of every starship in the fleet was the lightning trident emblem of the Imperium of True Human Beings, which was as new and vigorous as the rising sun.

It should be the first grandeur parade when the Imperium of True Human Beings was just established. It was also one of the pictures that Wuying Qi remembered most deeply.

Then, the fleet and the fortress were gone, and the memories were rewound to a long, long time ago into the infinite darkness. He was drifting like a broken boat, but he chanced upon the remains of a horrifying ship. From the remains, a fire of blood rose and smiled at him creepily.

"This must be the Blood God."

Li Yao read Wuying Qi's memories and thought, "This was when Wuying Qi encountered the Blood God in space. It was a life-changing moment for him. He certainly can't forget that."

Then, Wuying Qi's memories went all the way back through infinite colorful or mottled pictures, until it was frozen on a gloomy, rainy day.

It was a pouring storm outside, but thousands of mobsters who were as frantic as beasts were holding torches high in the rain.

The mobsters, as if possessed by extraterrestrial devils, lunged at him while howling.

The next picture was rather blurry, because Wuying Qi's eyes were full of tears and were covered by a pair of warm but rough hands.

"Mom, I was wrong. I was really wrong!"

Li Yao heard Wuying Qi scream regretfully, "I shouldn't have trusted the ordinary people. They were kind because they did not have power. Once they have received uncontrollable power, they can be even more brutal than us!

"I was wrong, Mom. I've destroyed this world. What should I do, Mom?

"If I can escape, I won't make the same mistake again. I won't trust them anymore. Mom, I won't be a Cultivator anymore. I'll be an avenger and seek revenge for you! Mom, I swear that I will kill all the people who hurt you!"

"S-Silly…"

Then, Li Yao heard the owner of the hands speak intermittently, "It's true that you made a lot of mistakes, but don't go from one extremity to the other. Cultivators and ordinary people are human beings alike, and they make as many mistakes as we do.

"The most important thing is not to lose faith in mankind and not to hate them. This world can be saved. Don't give up the path of the Cultivators however difficult it is.

"Remember, Ah Qi, there… there are no shortcuts. Anything that looks like a shortcut will lead you astray. They will…"

The warm voice came to an abrupt halt, and the rough hands slipped off from his face. Tears, mixed with the rain, made the ordinary people that were charging at him look like black tides, which swallowed him and shattered him.

"Ohhhhhhhhhhhh!"

The young Wuying Qi screamed in pain.

Li Yao quivered and realized the meaning of the memory.

It must've been when Wuying Qi was still in the Martial Meritocrats Sector before it was destroyed by a civil war.

The owner of the warm voice who died in his arms must be his mother.

Yes, what had escaped Li Yao's attention was that Wuying Qi also had a mother.

Considering what Wuying Qi did when he was young, his mother must've been a standard Cultivator, if not an overly pedantic and moralistic one.

So, had Li Yao's song raised Li Jialing's feelings for his mother, or had it awakened Wuying Qi's memories for his mother that had been buried for hundreds of years, leading to the collapse of his belief as an Immortal Cultivator?

It was truly a lucky shot.

This memory piece was particularly long and particularly blurry. It started from when Wuying Qi was caught by mobsters in a stormy night, to when he was interrogated for the Cultivators' last secrets, and ended with Wuying Qi being tied up and thrown into a burning volcano. The picture was frozen in his panic, desperation, fury, and regret when he fell from the crater.

Then, Li Yao realized something.

His consciousness had been stuck in this frozen memory palace, and he couldn't go out!

He was like an invisible shadow that flew above the crater, but there was no way he could get rid of the memory.

The mobsters around the crater and the young Wuying Qi who had fallen were all unmoving statues that couldn't offer any help.

He barely encountered such a creepy thing even when he absorbed Ou Yezi's memory pieces. Wherever he sprinted at, he would be bounced back as if he had run into cotton.

"Seriously?"

Li Yao's head was dizzy. "Have I been killed by Wuying Qi's self-detonation near his death, and we're now going to hell hand in hand?

"No wonder I'm viewing Wuying Qi's lifelong memories. You're exactly expected to reflect on your life in the moment of your death!

"Please don't! A smart and awesome superhero is dying after just saving the world? This is so unfair and unreasonable!

"I must get away from Wuying Qi's near-death reflections!"

Right when Li Yao was anxiously gritting his teeth to run out, he heard a gloomy voice from his back, which had been utterly silent until now.

"Thank you, Li Yao."

The voice made Li Yao's pupils constrict. The hair all over his body was standing up.

It was Wuying Qi's voice!

Chapter 2653: Wuying Qi 3.0

Crack! Crack! Crack! Crack!

Li Yao's neck uttered rusty noises as he turned back inch by inch. He immediately saw another transparent person floating not far away. Who else could it have been if not the haunting Wuying Qi?

However, Wuying Qi felt like an entirely different person from a moment ago.

He was wearing a white robe, and the cruelty and domination on his face were gone. He stared at his younger self and the mobsters who were throwing him into the volcano, but his eyes were calm and clear, as if he had come to new realizations.

He was floating in the middle of the smoke above the crater. Yet he seemed to be beyond this world and in full possession of this weird memory palace.

He was giving Li Yao the same feeling as Li Linghai did when Li Yao and Li Linghai met for the first time. They were both tranquil and untainted.

It did make sense. Wuying Qi was a half teacher for Li Linghai, so Li Linghai must've learned after him. This image should always be a facet of his mind.

For some reason, Li Yao was not wary of such a Wuying Qi at all, nor did he sense any danger from him.

Even so, Li Yao still freaked out. "Are you kidding me, bro? Why can't you just die? How many times have you transformed? Which between us is the undying cockroach here?"

Faced with Li Yao's aggression, Wuying Qi simply chuckled and said casually, "Li Yao, listen to my—"

Before he said "explanation", his shadow had been torn apart by Li Yao's.

"I won't! I won't! I won't!"

Li Yao cackled and said, "You're still stalling me at such a point? You can beat me and even kill me, but you can't insult my wisdom like this!"

However, however many times he tore Wuying Qi's shadow into shreds, it did not cause any substantial damage to Wuying Qi, who simply regathered again and again and still gazed at Li Yao calmly.

Nobody could win a fight between two shadows in a memory palace.

"This is impossible!"

Li Yao was dumbfounded. "What is this place? What is such a technique? What am I doing? What are you going to do, jacka*s?"

"Stop wasting your precious time and strength. You can't kill me in here, because I'm already dead, or rather, I'm in the middle of dying. Even someone as strong as Blackstar the Great can't die twice."

Wuying Qi said casually, "We're in my—no, in your brain. I'll inject some of my memories into your head with my last soul power. What you're perceiving is nothing but illusionary data. How can you expect to kill data?

"I've temporarily sealed this space, which is the last thing I could do by burning up my Divinity Branching Stage soul. How can it be broken easily? But don't worry. This seal will automatically perish when my soul is gone for good.

"I mean you no harm. I've truly lost the competition. More struggles will only mean more jokes. Dragging you to hell with me is pointless. I'm burning my soul to do this because I want to say something to you without being interrupted and to offer you my final legacy."

"Well…"

Li Yao blinked his eyes quickly and scanned Wuying Qi who was almost transparent. "Has your IQ suddenly plummeted, or has mine? Is this really not some kind of delay strategy?

"You say that you're dying. If I recall correctly, you were killed by my singing. But you're thanking me and giving me some legacies of Blackstar the Great? That's highly illogical, bro!"

"When you learn the truth in the morning, you can die in peace at night. I've understood the real truth, and my life is completed by the momentary realization. Does it really matter if I'm going to die 0.1 seconds later?"

Wuying Qi smiled at Li Yao. "My realization couldn't have been achieved without you. You also helped me get rid of Wuying Qi. I certainly have to thank you.

"Besides, my life and death are not important compared to my destiny. I need someone to pass on my belief. Is there a better candidate than you right now?"

"Hold on a second!"

Li Yao was rather stunned. No longer wondering whether or not it was another of Wuying Qi's tricks, he simply asked, "What did you say just now? I helped you get rid of Wuying Qi? Aren't you Wuying Qi?"

"Yes."

Wuying Qi nodded. "Of course I am Wuying Qi."

"You are Wuying Qi, but I helped you get rid of Wuying Qi, and you're thanking me for that?"

Li Yao was utterly confused. "How many Wuying Qis are out there?"

"At least three."

Wuying Qi pointed at the young man in the volcano and said, "The first Wuying Qi was the person who grew up in the Martial Meritocrats Sector, who crazily believed in the path of Cultivators, and who eventually caused a civil war between Cultivators and ordinary people because he thought that Cultivators carried some sort of 'original sin'. Let's call him 'Wuying Qi 1.0'. Can you understand it?"

Li Yao rolled his eyes and nodded.

"The second one is the Wuying Qi who witnessed the destruction of the Martial Meritocrats civilization, who luckily found the Blood God's heritage in space, and who usurped the highest power of the Star Ocean Republic as Blackstar the Great."

Wuying Qi said, "He's also the Wuying Qi who intended to reign mankind forever with the Next Sun Plan and who tried to destroy the whole Empyreal Terminus Sector when he couldn't achieve his purpose. Let's call this dark and evil man 'Wuying Qi 2.0', shall we?"

"Well, of course."

Although it was not easy to understand, Li Yao grasped what he meant.

With the destruction of the Martial Meritocrats Sector and the rebirth in the volcano as the boundary, Wuying Qi's life could be divided into two personalities that were the polar opposite of each other.

The first personality was an absolute fundamentalist Cultivator, and the second personality was a super Immortal Cultivator who was consumed by the darkness.

However, what was this third Wuying Qi, or Wuying Qi's third personality?

"There's also a 'Wuying Qi 3.0', who believes in the 'Next Sun Plan' and that he's fighting for mankind, and who will stop in time and even try to stop Wuying Qi 2.0 when he realizes that his crazy action will jeopardize the interest of mankind."

Wuying Qi smiled. "That will be me, a Wuying Qi that was just born but is the purest, noblest, and most selfless one."

Li Yao stared at the man for a long time, dumbfounded.

"Well, not that I'm unwilling to believe you."

Li Yao said with disbelief, "But isn't such a show of 'regret and change' too cliche? How stupid would I be if I am to believe that someone as shameless, unforgivable, and vindictive as Wuying Qi could generate a kind and noble personality? Sorry, I'm not insulting you. Well, fine, I am, because it's impossible after all!"

"That's fine. Nobody would've believed in my third personality, because Wuying Qi 2.0 is truly an unpardonable and incorrigible beast."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said peacefully, "When he escaped from the Martial Meritocrats Sector, his mind was swallowed by hatred, which turned him into a selfish demon who loathed mankind.

"Then, he met the Blood God who shared his attitude but was even eviler. How could the two demons produce a savior who fought for the civilization?

"Both 'renewing the Imperium' and 'Next Sun Plan' were excuses for him to reclaim power and reign the universe forever. He had never believed in them for one second.

"So, this third personality was not created naturally by his conscience, but created on purpose."

"Huh?"

Li Yao's mouth widened. "What do you mean by 'created on purpose'?"

"Wuying Qi 2.0 mastered the method of massive brainwashing, but to brainwash other people, he had to convince other people of his genuineness first. So, he had to brainwash himself and implant his faith for the 'Next Sun Plan' and his love for mankind and things like that in his head."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "Just like you said earlier, the best liars have to deceive themselves first. If they can't convince themselves, how can they convince other people? So, Wuying Qi specifically implanted an indestructible mark in his soul, ready to trigger the Next Sun Plan with the mark.

"He thought that he had the mark under control and that he could wipe it out after his plan worked. But he was wrong. He underestimated the power of his technique. A new personality was created by him unintentionally, which is me, Wuying Qi 3.0."

Dumbfounded, Li Yao thought for a long time and finally figured it out. "Wait, wait, I think it's too much for me to understand. Let me rephrase it in a plainer way.

"You're saying that the evil Wuying Qi intended to fool everybody in the world, but to fool them, he had to fool himself into thinking that he was truly a world savior. However, he worked too hard when he fooled himself, and he actually convinced himself of the idea. So, you were created?"

"That's a rather vulgar statement, but yes."

Wuying Qi 3.0 nodded solemnly. "That's exactly what happened. I'm not the unpardonable, selfish, and unfaithful demon. I'm the ultimate Wuying Qi who has seen the most extreme kindness and who has created the most extreme evilness in the past thousand years. I have understood everything, and my belief is unbreakable!"

Chapter 2654: The War Between 2.0 and 3.0!

"Wow, incredible!"

Greatly enlightened, Li Yao applauded and said, "So, apart from Wuying Qi 1.0, Blackstar the Great actually has double personalities just like me, one righteous and the other evil. Or rather, one mild and the other extreme.

"All the unforgivable bad things, like detonating the sun to make the Empyreal Terminus Sector die with him, were done by the evil, extreme Wuying Qi and had nothing to do with the righteous, mild Wuying Qi. So, you do not need to be held responsible at all… Could you be more shameless?"

However tranquil Wuying Qi 3.0 was, there was still embarrassment on his face after Li Yao said that. He snorted and said, "Believe it or not, the truth is right before your eyes. Do you really think a minor cockroach and a random warrior from the primeval age could've resisted Blackstar the Great? If Wuying Qi 3.0 hadn't fought Wuying Qi 2.0 determinedly and distracted 90% of his strength and computational abilities, do you think you could've won so easily?"

"Well…"

Li Yao was rather suspicious. "Are you serious?"

"Of course. Your scheme couldn't have fooled Wuying Qi 2.0 if he were undisturbed."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said casually, "It seemed that you confessed everything honestly, including your plan and the rough coordinates of the Star Glory Federation. You even submitted the half key to the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum. However, I could've obtained them even if you did not volunteer to confess.

"The trap under the disguise of another trap on Li Jialing was the simplest trick. It was absolutely illogical that you brought Li Jialing to the capital planet. Something was definitely wrong. As long as you keep that in mind, you wouldn't be fooled at all.

"If Wuying Qi 2.0 were alone, he would've reached a reasonable conclusion in half a second and killed you and Long Yangjun mercilessly without giving you any time to talk.

"But you were lucky. Really, too lucky. The critical moment of the Next Sun Plan was also the moment when the war between Wuying Qi 2.0 and Wuying Qi 3.0 was most heated, which occupied 90% of my computational abilities. That was why I misjudged again and again and eventually got killed by you."

Li Yao was stunned again for a long time.

"You're suggesting that—"

Frowning, he said, "The Blackstar the Great we resisted had actually been in serious mental dissociation all the time, and his two split personalities were fighting. As a result, he, or you, could only wield 10% of his strength and computation ability. But even so, we were still almost defeated by you?"

"That's right."

Wuying Qi 3.0 admitted frankly, "You only managed to defeat 10% of Wuying Qi 2.0 because I distracted 90% of him."

"…"

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao said to Wuying Qi 3.0 in amazement, "I've crushed too many supervillains, and many of them made nasty remarks after their failure. But I have to say that I've never seen anyone bragging as refreshingly and creatively as you!"

"I understand your meaning, but I'm only speaking the truth without any exaggeration."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled. "Also, I'm not claiming all the credit. I admit that you were the key to the battle and even my lifesaver."

"Huh?"

Li Yao was baffled. "If you defeated 90% of Wuying Qi 2.0, why was I the key and your lifesaver?"

"It was thanks to your 'tricks' that I finally defeated Wuying Qi 2.0. It's even safe to say that I wasn't really born, transforming from a manually-made 'pseudo personality' into a real personality and even a dominating main personality, until you played your tricks!"

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "I had been subjected to Wuying Qi 2.0 and was only an ignorant existence. Although I knew his evilness and the truth of the Next Sun Plan, there was nothing I could do except harassing him now and then. It's like the occasional pain in the bad guys' hearts when they do bad things.

"However, when you tossed the two Cosmos Rings that stored the Colossi to Wuying Qi 2.0, and he clenched them to damage them, his belief completely collapsed. No, it made me realize that he was just a weakling without any belief and that I was the real Wuying Qi with pure faith. That's how I was upgraded!

"I had to say that your plan was a complete success. Although Wuying Qi 2.0 destroyed the retrieval rune arrays of your Cosmos Rings, making it impossible for you to summon your Colossi, it also exposed his weakness and his lack of confidence in his charisma and appeal. How could he have defeated me if he did not trust himself?

"So, during that moment, I completely got rid of Wuying Qi 2.0's control and gained powerful self-willing, raising Wuying Qi's soul to an unprecedented level, which is Wuying Qi 3.0!

"To some extent, you're my creator, or at the very least, you are the pusher for my birth. How can I not thank you and give part of Wuying Qi's legacy to you?"

Li Yao was rendered speechless for a long time.

"So… mysterious?" he murmured.

"Not at all."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "In the jargon of the ancient Cultivators, Wuying Qi had a new understanding during the battle against you and reached a higher level, and his soul went through drastic changes. Is it easier for you to understand now?"

"Well, maybe."

Li Yao finally closed his mouth that had been opened for a long time. He thought for a moment and said, "Fine, if this is really your delay strategy, I'll admit my failure. What you said is truly interesting.

"So, the evil Wuying Qi 2.0 has been replaced by you and is completely gone?"

"Not exactly. I only said that I got rid of him, not that I destroyed him."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "I'm his mental devil, and he is mine. How can you destroy your mental devil so easily?"

"What?"

Li Yao grew nervous again. He glanced around warily and asked, "Where is he? He's not going to suddenly jump on my back, is he?"

"Rest assured. We're alone here. I've already kicked the annoyance away."

Wuying Qi 3.0 paused for a moment and said, "To be more exact, he left voluntarily. No matter how strong he used to be, he is always a coward without belief. He's scared of you, who is shameless and sleazy, but determined. He dare not break into your brain at all, so he chose Li Jialing.

"During the moment of the self-detonation, we completely split apart. I entered your head, and he entered Li Jialing's.

"The three… The four of us have all absorbed the power of the Imperial Fire Pearl. Yes, the Imperial Fire Pearl was complete at the beginning, but I knocked down a piece and gave the rest to you so that our souls could be subconsciously coordinated when we trained with the Imperial Fire Pearl.

"People of different blood types cannot transmit blood to each other. Souls have their 'type' too, which is much more complicated than blood type. However, the Imperial Fire Pearl could reduce the differences of our souls and increase the odds of a successful possession."

"Are you kidding me?"

Greatly shocked, Li Yao meant to grab Wuying Qi 3.0's collar and shake him, but he caused nothing but ripples between the two shadows. "You waited such a long time to tell me such an important thing? What are you going to do to Jialing? Just leave him alone already!"

"It has nothing to do with me. It's only about him, the idiotic Wuying Qi 2.0."

Wuying Qi 3.0 looked at Li Yao, undisturbed. "Why so anxious? Do you have no confidence in Li Jialing at all? Do you not believe that he can resist and even swallow the remains of Wuying Qi 2.0 with his own power now that he's awakened?

"Rest assured, Li Jialing will be fine. When he was possessed, I had a close contact with his soul. His potential is greater than anyone's imagination. His soul is so brilliant and perfect, and it's nothing like your messy, jumbled, and abnormal soul.

"What about Wuying Qi 2.0? Weakling. From the beginning to the end, he has always been a hilarious and pitiful weakling. He got the Blood God's heritage by luck, which granted him enormous strength but not a powerful mind. Under the seemingly invulnerable shell, he's but a soft caterpillar deep down in his soul.

"How can such a Wuying Qi 2.0 possess Li Jialing again when he only has broken pieces of his soul? In my opinion, he will only humiliate himself again when he struggles to survive. In the end, he will still die in the most shameful way. Even his memories, experience, and strength might be swallowed by Li Jialing!"

Chapter 2655: Ultimate Benevolence Is Not Right!

"Cut the crap!"

Li Yao was too concerned about Li Jialing to be convinced by Wuying Qi 3.0 easily. The man looked agreeable a moment ago, but Li Yao felt that his white robe was even more dreadful than the evil version with the black fog. "What are you doing exactly? Set me free!"

"Don't be hasty. Like I said, this memory palace will collapse when the moment comes."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said calmly, "Besides, what can you do even if you go out? The competition of beliefs is too dangerous. Nobody can help Li Jialing right now. Your belief is not his. He can only weather through it with his own willpower.

"As to what we're trying to do… I estimate that Wuying Qi 2.0 is only looking for an heir for himself at the end of his life, just like I am.

"So, why don't you hear me out before you make a choice? You don't strike me as such an impatient man!"

"Enough of the bulls*it. I can listen to you, but you have to answer my question first!"

Staring at Wuying Qi 3.0, Li Yao thought for a moment and asked, "What status are you in right now? Are you a Cultivator or an Immortal Cultivator?"

"While I do not agree with such an oversimplified way of classification, if I have to choose between the two…"

Wuying Qi 3.0 said with a smile, "I think I've finally returned to where I started after all the things I've been through, and I'm now a Cultivator again."

"Oh? Is that so?"

Li Yao looked at him suspiciously. "Are you sure?"

"Very positive. Probably nobody in the whole universe is as certain as me."

Wuying Qi 3.0 heaved a long sigh. "The path of the Cultivators sometimes results in the waste of resources, but the path of the Immortal Cultivators is even more severely flawed, because my purpose when I created it was impure. It can only serve as an expedience but cannot guide a civilization to sail against the incessant tides in the universe.

"I was wrong, Li Yao. My experience in the past thousand years has made me realize how selfish and naive I was. I am truly unpardonable!"

"Well…"

Li Yao scratched his chin suspiciously. "You sound rather sincere. That's the right attitude!"

"Of course I'm sincere. This is the ultimate answer that I've reached after I dissected and analyzed my thoughts and my mind."

Wuying Qi 3.0's eyes were so clear that they were almost invisible. "Rest assured. I'm totally different from Wuying Qi 2.0. He intends to disseminate the true path of immortality to every corner of the universe with the Gold Crystal Pyramid and the enhancement of the star, thereby reigning mankind forever. Even I abhor him!

"I've truly repented and changed! Now, I'm sincere, selfless, and righteous, and I consider things for the overall interest of mankind. When I was awakened, I immediately realized how hilarious and shameless I used to be!

"The Gold Crystal Pyramid was a piece of precious stellar-level magic equipment, and the true path of immortality is a wrong, inferior thought! To launch the true path of immortality with the Gold Crystal Pyramid is a crime to mankind and to the future!"

"That's right! That's right! Well said!"

Li Yao slapped his thigh and laughed. "Look, you have truly understood a lot of things. If you had realized it earlier, we wouldn't have needed to fight at all, and we could've created a beautiful tomorrow for mankind together, didn't we?"

"You don't say! Thankfully, you and I managed to stop Wuying Qi 2.0's evil 'Next Sun Plan'."

Wuying Qi 3.0 also smiled sincerely. "Now, it's time to optimize the Next Sun Plan so that it can play a better role for mankind. The Gold Crystal Pyramid is too precious to send the evil and wrong ideas of the Immortal Cultivators to mankind. It should inoculate the righteous ideas of the Cultivators in everyone!"

Li Yao's smile was frozen, as if a heavy brick had just been smashed at his face.

"What did you say?"

Li Yao's face was twisting. "What do you want?"

"It's not about me, but you."

Wuying Qi 3.0 blinked at Li Yao and smiled. "It's now or never! You won't have a second chance! Li Yao, haven't you always wished to spread the ideas of the Cultivators to the whole universe so that mankind can share peace and prosperity under the light of Cultivators. Now is your best opportunity!

"Blackstar the Great is about to die, but the Gold Crystal Pyramid is not destroyed. If it could be used to send wrong thoughts, it can certainly be used to send correct ideas too. I'll teach you the ways to operate and even modify the Gold Crystal Pyramid. The precious knowledge will all be yours. You can benefit mankind with it and bring infinite brightness to them!"

Sacred and pure brightness beamed out of Wuying Qi's white robe.

Yet such a glorious image was even creepier than the Wuying Qi who was shrouded in blood and fog for Li Yao.

Li Yao couldn't help but step back away, and he was still terrorized. He finally realized something. Why was he so stupid enough to believe that Wuying Qi could change? This Wuying Qi 3.0 was the evilest Wuying Qi of all!

"Are you indicating that I should inherit your cause and continue brainwashing mankind?"

Li Yao murmured, "That's impossible. I would never do that. If I really do that, would I be any different from you?"

"Of course you are different. How can this be brainwashing?"

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled. "The true path of immortality is wrong and evil. Wuying Qi was atrocious and heinous when he tried to inculcate evil thoughts in everyone.

"However, the path of the Cultivators is correct, righteous, bright, and kind, isn't it? You admit that, don't you? You're willing to defend it with your life, aren't you?

"If so, how can sharing your correct, righteous, bright, kind, pure, great, and benevolent ideas with the public to improve the moral level of the whole civilization, finish the Immortal Cultivators with the minimal cost, and reilluminate the universe with the light of Cultivators be called brainwashing?

"Don't be nervous, and there's no need to be so vigilant. Like I said, technology and tools are neutral. A blade can both be used to kill and to save people, depending on who's holding it. Isn't it the same for the Gold Crystal Pyramid? When it's in the hands of Wuying Qi, it would destroy a civilization; but if it's in the hands of a great hero like you, it will be a marvelous tool to save the civilization, isn't it?" said Wuying Qi 3.0 casually with a smile.

Li Yao, however, felt that he had been frozen. He could not say anything.

"Fine. I know that the precious 'legacy' is too unexpected. It's totally understandable that you can't accept it for the time being."

Wuying Qi 3.0 floated toward Li Yao, and Li Yao could not keep the distance between them no matter how he stepped back. Eventually, he haunted Li Yao's shadow and whispered to his ears, "Do you want to hear my life story?"

"No! Never!"

Though he was only a shadow, Li Yao was still creeped out. "Would you please go away? Two men shouldn't be so intimate, alright?

"I do not want to hear your life story. Nor am I interested in your worthless legacy. If you really consider yourself a 'righteous and kindhearted Wuying Qi', why don't you just die in peace and stop annoying me?"

"Hehe…"

Wuying Qi 3.0 did slightly separate from him. With the same casual but vicious smile on his face, he seemed to have seen through everything. "How can you learn the history between the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God, the secrets of the origin of the Covenant Alliance, the location of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum, and the way to become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid if you don't want to listen to my life story?"

"Wait, I'm truly interested in the first few things, but why would I become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid?"

His eyes bloodshot, Li Yao breathed heavily and gnashed his teeth as he said, "The Gold Crystal Pyramid is too evil to be controlled by a human. The first thing I'm going to do after I wake up is to demolish it!

"So, just drop it! No matter how you deceive me or make deals with me with your secrets, I won't become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Never!"

"Deceive? Deals? You're wrong, Li Yao."

Wuying Qi 3.0 looked down at him, with the domination of Blackstar the Great again. "I do not need to deceive or make deals with you. In fact, if you calm down and think about it, you will volunteer to become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. You'll even beg me to teach you how to control the Gold Crystal Pyramid and to give you the remainder of the Book of Revelation. But rest assured, we're both fellow Cultivators. I will tell you everything without you begging. After all, you've done me a favor, and this is for the civilization of mankind."

"It's true that old habits die hard."

Li Yao snorted and said, "You're trying to brainwash me even when you're dying? I'm rather interested in finding out how I would voluntarily become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Come on, whatever trick you may have, just bring it on!"

Chapter 2656: The Demon Within the Gold Crystal Pyramid

"No, I'm not 'brainwashing' anybody. I'm just helping you see the fact."

Wuying Qi 3.0's lips curled. His face and his robe were full of ash, making him seem rather dirty. "Oh, maybe I do not need to help you at all. With your wisdom and your insight on the darkness of humanity, you should've foreseen your destiny, right?"

"You made use of such shameless methods, so you must have a clear understanding about the vileness of mankind. You know that you have no choice except to become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. But you're unwilling to face the pathetic fact. Am I right, Li Yao?"

Li Yao's shadow was obviously shaking, like a reflection in a pond that had been just broken by a pebble.

"I have no idea what you're talking about!"

Li Yao gnashed his teeth and said, "I won't become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. I will destroy it. I will… destroy it."

"No, you won't. You will seize it, study it, upgrade it, and turn it into the last defense line between the Imperium and the federation, between the Immortal Cultivators and the Cultivators, to defend your motherland and your belief."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled more and more brilliantly, with the volcanic ash dancing around him like butterflies. "It's your only chance, as well as your only leverage to bargain with people like Lei Chenghu. You know it. You always know it.

"You don't have a choice. You are different from Lei Chenghu, Li Linghai, Jin Yuyan, and the thousands of Immortal Cultivators at the center of the cosmos right now. When Blackstar the Great exists, you may hold back your disagreement and work together, but all of you know that your cooperation won't last. The moment Blackstar the Great collapses, the alliance of the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators will fall apart. You're destined to fight among yourselves!

"Even if you never mean to kill Lei Chenghu, Li Linghai, or Jin Yuyan, and they will never turn against you because you've done them many favors, what about the other Immortal Cultivators?

"Now, it is no longer a secret that you are a Cultivator from a Cultivators' nation called Star Glory Federation at the border of the cosmos. No matter how you try to cover it up, more and more Immortal Cultivators will know it. How can you guarantee that those Immortal Cultivators won't invade your motherland?

"Even if people like Lei Chenghu, Li Linghai, and Jin Yuyan are rational and friendly enough, will they never change? Will they control the Imperium forever? If they change or die, how will the new-generation Immortal Cultivators view the Star Glory Federation? Will they feel that it might turn into a threat to the Imperium?

"Hehe, you know very well that your motherland can't expect to resist the Imperium of True Human Beings in a head-on clash in the next five hundred years. The resources at the border of the cosmos are limited anyway. I don't think you can keep your speed of development, can you?

"So, faced with the Imperium of True Human Beings which is gargantuan and unpredictable, what else can serve as the Star Glory Federation's first and last defense line except the Gold Crystal Pyramid, a dagger stabbed in the heart of the Imperium?

"So, only by threatening the Imperium with the Gold Crystal Pyramid to reach a certain 'balance of mutual deterrence', will peace be realized between the Imperium and the federation. If you destroy the Gold Crystal Pyramid, how are you going to 'persuade' the ferocious and cold-hearted Immortal Cultivators? With your sincerity, your kindness, or your nasty singing?"

Li Yao was lost for words and thought hard.

"Also, it's not just about the Star Glory Federation; it's also about the path of the Cultivators that is of paramount importance for you."

Wuying Qi 3.0 extended his hand and gathered a mouthful of ash before he blew the gray butterflies at Li Yao. He smiled and said, "You are a real Cultivator, a firm and determined warrior. So, you couldn't have cooperated with Lei Chenghu and Li Linghai purely to destroy Blackstar the Great, could you? That would've been one demon replacing another.

"Li Linghai's ideas to renew the Imperium and to bring justice and fairness to everyone and Lei Chenghu's faith in the honor of soldiers were the keys that motivated you. You were convinced that, under the leadership of Li Linghai and Lei Chenghu, the rotten Imperium will be reborn and move to a brighter future, where most ordinary people will live a better life, and the Cultivators like you can subtly change the nature of the Imperium in the process.

"That's the reason why you could keep your belief as a Cultivator while cooperating with Lei Chenghu and rescuing Li Linghai.

"It's a nice plan, but how are you going to carry it out?

"How can you guarantee that Li Linghai will fulfill her promise? How can you guarantee that Lei Chenghu will be a soldier who fights for the civilization? And how can you guarantee that their 'reform' will go as you wish?

"What if they regret later? What if they are reluctant to liberate the hominoids living below the ground on the capital planet? What if Lei Chenghu is going to sacrifice thousands of ordinary people for a certain purpose? How are you going to supervise and stop them?

"The Gold Crystal Pyramid is the only way, isn't it? You know very well that, only by controlling the Gold Crystal Pyramid, a piece of magic equipment that can destroy the capital, will you and other Cultivators be able to participate in the renewal of the Imperium as a supervisor with the highest authority.

"You can certainly destroy the Gold Crystal Pyramid under the spur of the moment, but if so, the Cultivators will lose the only weapon to contain the Immortal Cultivators. What are you going to do if the Immortal Cultivators slaughter hominoids again? Rescue them one by one?

"Just wake up, Li Yao. Stop fooling yourself and escaping from the dilemma. This is your responsibility. This is your destiny. This is the most glorious power that belongs to you! You have to let all the Immortal Cultivators know that you can destroy the whole Empyreal Terminus Sector and that you will unhesitatingly do so with a heart of iron if need be.

"Only by doing so can you intimidate all the Immortal Cultivators, secure your tiny federation under the pressure of the Imperium, ensure that the reform of the Imperium goes according to your plan, and rescue the hominoids who are still suffering!"

"Do you really think that I'm such a demon? Whatever happens, I will not destroy the Empyreal Terminus Sector!"

Li Yao bellowed in rage, "This is not just the capital of the Imperium of True Human Beings, but also the most prosperous Sector of mankind, a vital crossroad of the universe, and our precious home and wealth. I will never destroy the Empyreal Terminus Sector crazily like you would!"

"Your fury, just like your overly-passionate speech, is only a cover for the weakness and panic in your heart."

Wuying Qi 3.0 grabbed a mouthful of ash again, which turned into a gray flower. He blew at the flower, and the flower wobbled away, glimmering. He stared at the dance of the flower and smiled. "Maybe you won't, but you have to convince all the Immortal Cultivators that you will. If it makes you feel more comfortable, you may consider the whole thing to be a strategic deception, and to start this strategic deception, you have to become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid first.

"Also, there's one other thing that's quite ironic. Do you know what it is?

"Do you remember the standard for the alliance of the Pangu civilizations to accept new members? To boast the ability to destroy themselves, which is the sign of a real civilization.

"However, none of the self-destroying abilities of the thirteen carbon-based lives that made up the alliance were invented by 'demons'. The terrifying abilities to tear apart a planet, change the magnetic field, or even control a star were all the inventions of sages, geniuses, and peace-lovers, who created the abilities that were enough to destroy their civilization for the purpose of defending their civilizations, their homes, and their families against evil enemies or even ending wars for good.

"To protect their civilizations, intelligent lives created the abilities that could destroy their civilizations. It sounds ironic, but it was and will always be true.

"So, if one day you lose control and turn from a dragon-slayer into a demon in the Gold Crystal Pyramid, don't go too hard on yourself. You won't be the first, and you certainly won't be the last."

Stunned, Li Yao listened quietly and wiped the ash that had dropped to his face.

The ash melted in his palm like dirty snowflakes and leaked into his body.

"Rest assured, the ash is nothing but an illusion. It does not contain the slightest mental power."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled and said, "For people like you, mental corruption is useless and will only raise your wariness. Besides, I'm already too weakened to have another mental competition with you. I can only expect that you will realize your unique destiny eventually, and I have no doubt that you will."

Chapter 2657: The Dragon Slayer

"I-I won't!"

Li Yao tried to shake off the ash on him, but since they were right above the creator, the ash was dancing like snowflakes and could not be removed at all. "I won't do it whatever you say. There has to be a better choice and a more perfect solution!"

"Is there really such a thing as a perfect solution? I've been searching for it in my whole life, but I found nothing."

Wuying Qi 3.0 sighed with pity in his eyes. "Even if you want to destroy the Gold Crystal Pyramid, how exactly are you going to do it? Arsonist and Blackhole's Heart have been too seriously damaged to launch another destructive attack. Long Yangjun's Heavenly Crystal is on the verge of collapse too.

"This Gold Crystal Pyramid was one of the final trump cards of the Houyi Clan. The Pangu Clan's possible assault and sabotage were taken into consideration when it was designed, so it's extra solid. Just now, our Colossi had a long and fierce battle, but was the Gold Crystal Pyramid affected at all?

"So, if a fierce battle of three Colossi failed to destroy it, how are you planning to demolish it without a Colossus? The best you can do is to sabotage some components or tamper with the data, but such damage can be easily repaired.

"If you are too concerned to shoulder your responsibility, the Gold Crystal Pyramid will likely fall into someone else's hand—probably Lei Chenghu's.

"This is the capital where millions of Immortal Cultivators are gathered anyway, and it's impossible for a few Cultivators to occupy this place for long. Eventually, you need Lei Chenghu to clean up the mess, and most Immortal Cultivators will pledge loyalty to him.

"Think about it. As 'the last Immortal Cultivator of the Imperium', would Lei Chenghu destroy the Gold Crystal Pyramid, or would he want to be the new master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid?

"Things would be the same even if it isn't Lei Chenghu but Li Linghai, Jin Yuyan, or any other Immortal Cultivators. None of them can resist such a temptation, especially after witnessing how it destroyed the ace fleets of the four families easily. All the Immortal Cultivators will fight bloody battles to gain control over the Gold Crystal Pyramid, craving to be its master!

"Tell me, Li Yao, do you want Lei Chenghu, or Li Linghai, or any other Immortal Cultivators whom you do not know at all, to be the Gold Crystal Pyramid's master? Do you wish to transfer the power of destruction to anyone else?

"Very well, your eyes are telling me your answer. If you do not want the Gold Crystal Pyramid to be controlled by an Immortal Cultivator, the only thing you can do is to seize the opportunity while the capital planet is still in chaos to absorb my heritage with the Book of Revelation and inject your gene information and soul mark to the Gold Crystal Pyramid so that you will be its new master!

"Only by doing so will you intimidate all the ambitious Immortal Cultivators, prevent the Gold Crystal Pyramid from being controlled by malicious guys, and ensure everlasting peace in the Empyreal Terminus Sector."

Li Yao stepped back quickly with a pale face, shivering.

"It's fine. Just show your doubt and hesitation. It's really fine. I'm not mocking you or retaliating against you. I'm just talking to a new-generation dragon slayer as a senior dragon slayer about the past and about how we turn into dragons in the end ourselves."

Wuying Qi 3.0 floated forth and grabbed Li Yao's shoulder sympathetically. "Slaying the dragon is not the end of an adventure but the beginning of a long journey. However, you never really know it until scales, claws, and tusks are growing in you.

"Did you know that I was a confident dragon slayer just like you a thousand years ago? I once slew infinite dragons and demons, who were all inhumane and unforgivable like Wuying Qi 2.0.

"I killed infinite corrupt officials and generals of the Star Ocean Republic, who did all kinds of bad things though they looked like Cultivators. Trust me, they weren't any better than Wuying Qi 2.0 when they tried to preserve their lives and their properties toward the end of the Star Ocean Republic.

"I once led an army back to the Martial Meritocrats Sector, my hometown, and attacked the neighboring Sand Primitive Sector, partly for revenge and partly to eliminate the evil Chiyou Cult. When the Chiyou Cult was destroyed, its believers and priests summoned a monster named Chiyou. I hunted it for half a year in space before I executed all of its 72 incarnations!

"I even eradicated the remains of the Mad Armageddon so that the great devil ten thousand years ago could not cause trouble to our world anymore.

"Every time, every time I killed those villains and stopped them from destroying the world, I thought it was the end of the game and I was the final victor. After the final battle against the Mad Armageddon, my ecstasy was beyond description. I embraced an eternal victory. No insane villains can ever harm my world or my civilization anymore.

"However… Hehe… In the end…

"That's why I said that you're not the first. It's not humiliating, really. Embrace your destiny with courage, face the scales and claws that are growing under your skin, and wait for the next dragon slayer to kill you and pass on your destiny. This is not humiliating."

Wuying Qi 3.0 was silent for a long time as he stared at his younger self who was falling into the magma in the crater.

Li Yao, on the other hand, looked at his hands and arms.

The butterflies of ash drifted to his hands and his arms and melted into them. In a trance, he really felt that he had grown a vague, barely-visible layer of gray scales.

"Now, young dragon slayer, are you ready to listen to the lifelong story of this vicious dragon?" Wuying Qi 3.0 finally opened his mouth and asked casually.

He did not force Li Yao to listen, as if he would really bring everything, including the secrets of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, to the other world if Li Yao didn't want to listen to it.

But of course, it remained unknown whether or not he had left a copy of the ways to control the Gold Crystal Pyramid elsewhere.

"Just… speak," said Li Yao, struggling.

He felt that not just his skin, but his heart was also growing small and thick scales.

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled and did not question Li Yao's self-doubt, as if he were really just recalling his past before his death.

"Where should I start? You know the absurdities I did in the Martial Meritocrats Sector, do you?"

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "You must know that although the propaganda is exaggerated, it's not entirely false. The ordinary people of the Martial Meritocrats Sector were truly ignorant, shameless, and merciless."

"That was because your methodology was fundamentally wrong!"

Li Yao couldn't help but argue, "You had been spoiling the ordinary people like they were ignorant kids since the beginning, and you never taught them anything. Getting to the bottom of it, you do not believe that ordinary people can contribute to civilization at all!

"After hundreds of years of spoiling, the ordinary people of the Martial Meritocrats Sector naturally became mischievous and uncontrollable. Isn't it only natural that they ruined the civilization?

"In the Star Glory Federation, our path, which has been modified countless times and will be modified countless times more, is different. We won't repeat your mistake!"

"Fantastic!"

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled. "Isn't it all the more reason why you should claim the Gold Crystal Pyramid, disseminate the new methodology of the Cultivators to the whole universe, and awaken more people? Chances are that you can be the savior of the universe!"

Li Yao was rendered speechless. He decided to stop fighting with this guy since this guy was about to die.

"Let's start from the moment I was tossed into the crater."

Wuying Qi 3.0 did not mock Li Yao any further but continued his story.

The frozen palace of memories began to move again as he narrated. The young man in midair fell into the magma. Surrounded by a thin spiritual shield, he floated and floundered desperately in the magma. The spiritual shield soon melted, and the magma burned his body mercilessly.

"There's something that I've never told anyone."

Recalling his struggle in the magma, Wuying Qi 3.0 seemed to be still in pain. "It's true that I found a secret base in the volcano and escaped the Martial Meritocrats Sector with a shuttle inside. However, I did not discover the Mad Armageddon's heritage by luck later. It was destined.

"As a matter of fact, the secret base hidden in the volcano in the Martial Meritocrats Sector was part of the Blood God's plan ten thousand years ago when he rebelled."

Chapter 2658: The Blood God's Mission!

Deep inside the volcano, a gate blocked by magma was slowly opened, revealing a base that had been sealed for ten thousand years. Mechanical robots surfaced in the magma, hauling young Wuying Qi's incomplete body into the base, where they implanted tremendous artificial organs into his body and covered him with pieces of black armor.

The whole base was utterly silent, and the mechanical skeletons moved rigidly like fully-wound puppets.

"This is actually an enlisting base of the 'Human Expedition Army'. Even the weakest man can be modified into a well-trained warrior very quickly after they're sent into this place. If they're strong in the first place, they would be much stronger after the modifications."

Wuying Qi 3.0 explained, "The so-called 'Human Expedition Army' was a name that the Blood God gave his army. In the magnificent war ten thousand years ago, the Blood God's rebelling army and the army that was loyal to the Supreme Emperor fought in space and on every planet. They even thought that the war would linger on for thousands of years and that even if they died, their ideologies would be passed on and continue the fight.

"That was why this enlisting base was established. The Blood God thought that even if the Star Ocean Imperium and his Human Expedition Army were gone thousands of years later, anyone who discovered the enlisting base would be able to absorb his legacy and become a new-general warrior for the Human Expedition Army.

"So, a lot of automatic and half-permanent designs were adopted in this base. There were also plenty of mission modules. As long as one warrior was created, they could carry out the missions step by step and wake up more enlisting bases to raise more warriors so that the Human Expedition Army could be reestablished."

Li Yao nodded his head, deep in thought. The Blood God's idea was similar to Professor Mo Xuan's Tinder Plan and the Blood Stripe Virus' civilizational possession.

The war between the Blood God and the Supreme Emperor was not limited to one era but lingered on to infinity.

Even though they died thousands of years ago, as long as the Blood God's enlisting base raised new warriors for the expedition army again, the Blood God would be the final victor.

"I was quite lucky. This enlisting base was powered by the heat of the magma, so it was still as new as ever despite the passage of time. The puppet tutors inside were undamaged too."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "Also, nobody in the past ten thousand years had approached this enlisting base before me, so I was given a high permission and promoted as a colonel of the Human Expedition Army.

"The puppet tutors treated my wounds and trained me in the ways that the Blood God left behind. The fifty puppet tutors were supposed to teach ten thousand soldiers, but they only had me. They left quite a deep impression on me."

Behind Wuying Qi 3.0, young Wuying Qi, who had been utterly reshaped by black organs and armor, started his inhumane training under the supervision and torture of the fifty iron puppets.

Even Li Yao, who was known as a training maniac, felt that he was sweating after seeing how Wuying Qi trained himself years ago.

"I spent three years deep inside the volcano. Three unforgettable years!"

Wuying Qi 3.0 said coldly, "Three years later, I was completely reborn, and I was no longer a rookie.

"Naturally, I planned to seek revenge. But on one hand, I was alone, and it was impossible for me to fight two Sectors even though I was an elite warrior of the Human Expedition Army; on the other hand, I received a new mission.

"Having no other choices, I had to fly a small shuttle into space away from my home together with those iron skulls!"

The picture in the memory palace changed. The volcano, the enlisting base, and the Martial Meritocrats Sector were gone, replaced by the boundless cosmos. Wuying Qi 3.0 and Li Yao floated in the darkness as glimmers.

A shuttle slowly passed them and left a long helical trace behind them.

"I thought that I was carrying out a regular mission—to search for other enlisting bases, activate them, recruit more soldiers, and reestablish the Human Expedition Army, like I'd been trained countless times before."

Wuying Qi 3.0's voice was shivering, as the scene had left too deep an impression on him. "However, my mission turned out to be much more advanced. When I was woken up after a hundred years of hibernation, I found myself right before the remains of a gargantuan starship. It was Doom, the Blood God's flagship!"

Before Wuying Qi 3.0 and Li Yao, a decrepit starship slowly surfaced in the darkness.

It was more like a space fortress in the shape of a human head than a starship, except that it was mottled and riddled with holes, making it look like the skull of a screaming skeleton.

Even though it was a memory from thousands of years ago and even though they were in space, Li Yao could still hear the howling of the skeleton.

There was no telling how huge the space fortress was because of the lack of references, but Wuying Qi's shuttle was as insignificant as dust compared to it.

Walking in Wuying Qi's shoes, Li Yao imagined that he accidentally became a warrior of the Human Expedition Army when he thought he was going to die, before he crossed the cosmos to visit the commander of the Human Expedition Army… It was truly suffocating.

Guided by Wuying Qi 3.0, Li Yao slowly entered the space fortress through a hole that looked like the eye socket following young Wuying Qi.

This space fortress was utterly empty like a rotten head. The shuttle drifted in the darkness for a long time before it reached a vast palace. There was nothing on the obsidian floor except for a gigantic pool of blood.

The creepy thing was that, though the place had no heat, pressure, or air and was the same as the vacuum outside, and it had been thousands of years since the destruction of Doom, the freshest blood was still rolling in the pool!

The terrifying picture attracted young Wuying Qi and Li Yao, who did not think to flee even when the blood darted out from the pool and condensed into a threatening shadow.

"This is the remains of the Mad Armageddon."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "Rumor has it that the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God, his strongest clone, had a brutal fight on the capital planet ten thousand years ago. They were both heavily wounded. As a result, the Supreme Emperor had to sleep on the Gold Throne where time was almost frozen, and the Blood God fled into space and went missing.

"Actually, as the Supreme Emperor's clone, the Blood God was not as strong as the Supreme Emperor. He was more heavily wounded than the Supreme Emperor was. Not only was his body reduced to blood, but his soul was also sealed in it and could not escape. He only recovered slightly after thousands of years of training, so that he could send an instruction to the enlisting bases all over the universe, asking the new-generation warriors of the expedition army to meet their commander here.

"When I entered the enlisting base, it was the time that the Blood God woke up, so I was the only warrior that received the instruction and reported in."

Li Yao looked at the shivering, ghastly shadow of blood and imagined the fight between the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God, so fascinated that he almost wished that he could travel back in time and experience the battle in person!

"At that time, although I was disappointed by the ordinary people and determined to seek revenge, and I had been trained as a soldier of the Human Expedition Army for three years, I still remembered the dreadfulness of the Blood God. I was highly vigilant of him."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "At first, I thought I was going to die. The Blood God summoned me to this place certainly because he was going to enjoy my body and transform my body into a container for his soul."

Li Jialing couldn't help but ask, "Like what you plan to do to Li Jialing?"

"No, it's like what Wuying Qi 2.0 planned to do to Li Jialing."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled and said, "However, the Blood God couldn't do it. Naturally, it was not because he was merciful, but because his soul had been shattered by the Supreme Emperor and haunted for thousands of years. It was impossible for him to possess another person.

"So, he could only give up. But he asked me to carry out a new mission, hoping that I could continue the competition of beliefs between him and the Supreme Emperor!"

Chapter 2659: The Blood God's Mutation!

"The competition of beliefs?"

Interested, Li Yao asked, "So, the war between the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God was not for the highest power?"

"Not just for power, of course."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled and said, "The desire for the highest power alone was not enough to support the Blood God against the Supreme Emperor's pressure. He would've been crushed by the Supreme Emperor easily the moment he rebelled.

"No, it was not about power—at least not the insignificant power in this hopelessly depressing universe, but the power on the vast, unknown lands.

As Wuying Qi 3.0 explained, the blood that popped above the pool like fire suddenly expanded and enshrouded the whole universe, before it split into thousands of drops of blood that were in the shape of warships.

It was the Blood God's unprecedented fleet that was made of blood!

"When the Star Ocean Imperium had just been established, and the standard Type-I Humans were reclaiming the universe and crushing the Type-II Humans that were commonly known as demons, the Blood God never coveted the highest power or thought to betray the Supreme Emperor."

Wuying Qi said, "At that time, the surviving demons had indeed reached out to him, proposing that all the demons would listen to his command as long he betrayed the Supreme Emperor and initiated a civil war within the Star Ocean Imperium.

"But the Blood God was unmoved. As a clone made of the Supreme Emperor's desire for slaughter and conquest, he was already fulfilled by exploring new worlds and killing his enemies. He enjoyed slaughters and conquests and was not interested in political power at all. So, he did not plan to betray the Supreme Emperor at all.

"Just like that, the Blood God explored and destroyed worlds one after another like the most perfect slaughter machine. He found all the Type-II Humans hiding in the corners of the universe and killed them, including the newborn babies. He was satisfied and grew stronger in the carnage.

"As the demons fled, the expedition army was further and further away from the Supreme Emperor's radiance. They entered the darkest Sectors at the border of the cosmos. Due to the long supply lines and the occasional space storms that disrupted the communication of his branch fleets, the war was not as smooth as before.

"However, the Blood God was not bothered at all. Yet, something he looted from demons made him consider the significance of life, war, and civilization."

In the picture behind Wuying Qi 3.0, a human made of blood was deep in thought in the middle of countless jade chips.

Fascinated, Li Yao couldn't help but ask, "What was it exactly?"

"It's the origin and the destiny of demons."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "Who I am, where I'm from, and where I'm going to… Those Type-II Humans were interested in those questions too. Almost every monarch of the demon dynasties during the thirty thousand years of Great Darkness had been seeking the origin of the Chaos Water that created demons. Do you know that?"

"I know a thing or two."

Recalling the research products in Kunlun, Li Yao said, "The demons forty thousand years ago were created by an ancient Cultivator named Ba Yanzhi. It was Ba Yanzhi who discovered the long-sealed Chaos Water, a mysterious black liquid that could activate certain genes in the human body and turn humans into demons.

"However, Ba Yanzhi was only the discoverer of the Chaos Water, not its inventor. The Chaos Water can be traced back to the primeval war hundreds of thousands of years ago, when the Nuwa Clan created the first demons and enlisted them in a special force."

"That's correct. You're much more knowledgeable than I expected. It seems that you are destined to become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled and said, "In the primeval era, the thirteen carbon-based lives gathered the essence of their genes and created a brand-new species named 'human being' for various purposes—to make a perfect tool, to unify their appearance so that the alliance would be more solid, or to preserve the embers of their civilization so that they could live through the long periods when there were few resources.

"The main designer of human beings was the Nuwa Clan.

"The human beings at the beginning certainly did not look like us right now. At that time, the perfect gene strands of the thirteen carbon-based lives hadn't been found yet, so infinite 'experimental humans' were created. Many mystical creatures we know today are actually the abnormal, experimental humans, who were the failed versions before us.

"Eventually, the genetic engineers and life designers of the Nuwa Clan, after thousands of failures, finally created two successful models.

"Type-I Humans kept their primeval genes deep inside their gene strands. They did not have many abilities, but they also consumed minimal resources. They could even live for almost a hundred years and pass on their genes without any spiritual energy as long as there were air, food, and water.

"However, once Type-I Humans were placed in an environment of abundant spiritual energy, they would be able to activate their potential and become as strong as the primeval experts.

"They were like some sort of… spore, which could live with or without oxygen. Their ability to survive without spiritual energy was something that the primeval clans could never achieve. So, they're a very successful model.

"After designing the basic model, the Nuwa Clan went further and designed an upgraded model, which could survive and even attack fiercely even when there were few resources. They were Type-II Humans, known as demons.

"The Nuwa Clan's design was not a complete success this time. Type-II Humans focused too much on their physical abilities that their potential was suppressed and their gene strands became unstable, resulting in malign mutations during reproduction. Obviously, it was not the best model in the long run.

"However, the shortcoming of Type-II Humans was tolerable, especially on a battlefield. Since Type-II Humans did not need much training to fight, they were much more excellent soldiers than Type-I Humans were. When the war between the Nuwa Clan and the Pangu Clan burst out, the Nuwa Clan manufactured copious Type-II Humans as their warriors.

"What I'm trying to say is that demons, or Type-II Humans, are closer to the Nuwa Clan than we, the standard Type-I Humans, are. The leaders of the demons gathered too many secrets on the Nuwa Clan during the thirty thousand years of reign, but now the Blood God obtained them.

"The Blood God read the heritages of the Nuwa Clan, including the remains of the Book of Revelation. He finally learned of the truth of the universe, or rather, the 'multiverse', as you put it."

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao said prudently, "The Blood God learned that there were universes beyond our universe and that as long as we crossed the boundless black wall outside of the three thousand Sectors, we would see an unknown but brilliant place that was the destiny of our civilization?"

"That's right."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "It was also the reason why the Nuwa Clan fought the Pangu Clan. The Nuwa Clan wanted to go out, but the Pangu Clan wanted to stay back for at least several million years. The war destroyed the primeval civilizations but not the Nuwa Clan's ambition, which somehow ended up in the Blood God's heart.

"In the meantime, while unveiling the truth of the universe with the heritages of the Nuwa Clan, the Blood God also heard the calling of the extraterritorial devils.

"As we said before, the so-called 'extraterritorial devils' are actually the last words of the destroyed civilizations beyond the black wall, which contain their precious technologies, wildest ambitions, or infinite rage. Those things crept into the Blood God's head and allured him to go beyond the black wall and claim all the treasures of the bygone civilizations for mankind!

"Most Cultivators are too weak to understand the information that the extraterritorial devils carried. They either go mad or simply execute the extraterritorial devils.

"However, with the heritages of the Nuwa Clan, the Blood God was able to understand those last words, which informed him of the vastness and prosperity of the grand universe and made him eager to explore and conquer it!"

Chapter 2660: Born for Conquest!

"Then…"

Li Yao finally realized it. "The Blood God was lost and degenerated into the Mad Armageddon!"

"No, he was not lost. It was his nature and his setting when he was 'created'."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "You should know that the Blood God was not a natural-born human, but a clone that the Supreme Emperor made with 100% of his own genes. Then, the Supreme Emperor inculcated the need for exploration and conquest as well as the destructive part of his personality into the head of this clone.
