"The Blood God was born for war. War was his destiny. He was a commander, a general, a sergeant, and a most distinguished soldier. He was destined to fight until the end of the world!

"However, the Star Ocean Imperium had already been established, and Type-II Humans were being crushed and would be eliminated soon. Nothing could stop their final failure. So, the Blood God was faced with a cruel question.

"If all the enemies were destroyed and the whole universe was explored and conquered, mankind would embrace at least thousands of years of peace. In such a case, what was the point of his life? Why should he live?

"For a soldier who was addicted to war, adept at war, and had nothing but war in his life, peace was intolerable. Before he discovered the secrets of the Nuwa Clan and the extraterritorial devils murmured to him, he was ready to be killed by the last bullet in the last battle, which would be the best ending for him.

"However, the gate to the infinite universes beyond the black wall was opened. Treasures were calling him in the fog at the end of the road. For a war maniac like the Blood God, how could he not embark on the journey?"

Li Yao took a long breath. "Then, the Blood God betrayed the Supreme Emperor."

"No, not yet."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "Since the Supreme Emperor decided to create twenty clones and appoint the strongest of them as the commander of the expedition army, he certainly had his way to control the clones and ensure their loyalty. At that time, the Blood God's ambitions hadn't corrupted his loyalty yet. He reported all his findings to the Supreme Emperor and asked the Supreme Emperor to continue the expedition toward the outer lands after the remaining demons were wiped out.

"Civilization originated from war and cannot exist without war. Eternal peace will only lead to eternal corruption. Only continuous explorations and conquests can empower and reinvigorate a civilization so that it will live forever!

"That's how the Blood God pleaded with the Supreme Emperor. He requested to lead the expedition army forward after the remaining demons were eliminated. If the Star Ocean Imperium did not have enough wealth to support an enormous expedition army, he could explore the outer lands with half of the fleet, or one tenth of the fleet, or even only one starship by himself. He could sail to the splendid grand universe on a ship alone across the black wall and seek a way out for mankind!"

Even though it was the notorious Blackstar the Great telling a story of the Blood God who was even more notorious, Li Yao was still deeply shocked.

One ship to seek a way out for the civilization of mankind through the black wall!

Fight relentlessly even if doom was right ahead, until one was killed by the last bullet on the final battleground!

"Did the Supreme Emperor accept the Blood God's request?"

Li Yao asked a question whose answer he knew.

Back at the summit of the eccentrics of the Pangu Universe, he had discussed the problem with Boss Bai and Long Yangjun.

If a Flood that swallowed infinite civilizations were lurking beyond the black wall, one starship that went out was enough to expose all the information about the Pangu Universe, which would be the end for all the creatures in the Pangu Universe.

To prevent such a possibility, the Pangu Clan destroyed their advanced cyber technologies and wiped out their feelings, desires, and curiosity!

So, why would the Supreme Emperor permit the Blood God's reckless move?

"Of course not. The Blood God's demand could not be satisfied. You must consider the state of the Supreme Emperor back then."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "The Supreme Emperor is definitely a great hero. We, the Immortal Cultivators, acknowledge him as the founder of the modern civilization too. However, in his age, to overthrow the tyranny of demons, the Supreme Emperor adopted quite a lot of cruel and even extreme methods.

"The darkness in the Supreme Emperor's complicated personality corroded his heart on his way of assuming power, making him vacillate between a saint and a tyrant.

"To prevent himself from being swallowed by darkness, he split twenty different personalities with unparalleled techniques and injected the extreme and emotional ones into his clones. So, the main personality left in him was a perfect leader who was absolutely calm, rational, and considered for the big picture.

"Since the Supreme Emperor had injected all his desire for conquest and exploration into the Blood God, the Supreme Emperor certainly wouldn't be convinced by the Blood God or the extraterritorial devils so easily.

"Even if the multiverse did exist, exploring it right then was still too hasty. Hundreds if not thousands of years of preparation was necessary.

"The Supreme Emperor did not accept the Blood God's request. On the other hand, the Supreme Emperor realized that he made a great mistake. He underestimated his clone that was made of desire for conquest and slaughter. His clone was getting beyond his control and might lead a fleet to the outer lands disregarding his command.

"The Supreme Emperor would not allow it to happen. He decided to recall the Blood God and modify the Blood God again. He could even swallow the Blood God and melt with his strongest clone again."

"Huh?"

Li Yao found it hard to believe. "Swallow and melt with the Blood God? Is it really possible?"

"If personalities can be split, they can certainly be melted together. Why do you think they can't be?"

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "If you ever reach the Divinity Branching Stage someday, you will understand the marvelousness of life better.

"However, while the Supreme Emperor wanted to melt the Blood God, the Blood God was less enthusiastic.

"Maybe in the Supreme Emperor's eyes, it was natural for two personalities to melt together so that they could both be complete. However, due to the Nuwa Clan's secrets and the extraterritorial devils' whisper, the Blood God considered himself a unique, real, and complete life, who was nobody's clone or shadow. He was the Blood God, a real person!

"So, after receiving the Supreme Emperor's command asking him to return to the capital, the Blood God inherited the Nuwa Clan's legacy, listened to the extraterritorial devils, and accepted the surrender of the remaining demons, before he declared war on the Supreme Emperor!

"Even when I met him ten thousand years later, he still did not consider it a betrayal, nor was he interested in the supreme power at all. For the Blood God, our world was a dark and depressing cage, and reigning over such a cage was pointless if not absurd. His only wish had always been to break out and search for new hope!"

Li Yao had a lot of complicated feelings as he listened.

In all fairness, neither the Supreme Emperor nor the Blood God was wrong. While the Blood God had reasons to explore the outer lands, the Supreme Emperor's prudence was also understandable!

If the Blood God were less extreme, and the Supreme Emperor chose to talk to his clone instead of trying to swallow it in a hurry, the history in the following ten thousand years might have been different.

Then, Li Yao was amused by his childishness.

Talk. There were too many conflicts in this world that could've been resolved by a peaceful talk.

If only the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan had talked, if only the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God had talked, if only the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators had talked, and if only humans and demons had talked…

But they all chose to fight in the end!

"I don't need to bore you with the details of the war. I believe you've read the history books like Rise and Decline of the Star Ocean Imperium."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "Toward the end of the civil war, the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God had a fierce battle on the capital planet for the control of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. The Blood God learned of the Gold Crystal Pyramid from the secrets of the Nuwa Clan, so he was determined to conquer the capital planet regardless of the cost. The Supreme Emperor was unaware of the Gold Crystal Pyramid at first, but he certainly wouldn't let the Blood God seize the capital planet. So, the two of them had a huge fight and were both heavily wounded."

Chapter 2661: The Channeling Temple

Li Yao nodded his head. He had inferred it with Long Yangjun earlier and was not surprised at Wuying Qi's story.

In such a way, the mysterious legends from ten thousand years ago had a logical explanation. Though not as romantic as before, it made more sense now.

"You know the result of the battle. The Supreme Emperor and the Blood God repeated the tragedy in the primeval age like the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan. They perished together, and so did the troops that were loyal to them, disappearing into the cold universe. The civilization of human beings that had just been reconstructed was almost destroyed, embracing another ten thousand years of chaos."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "The Supreme Emperor escaped to the Gold Throne, and the remains of the Blood God hid in Doom, his flagship, where he rested for nine thousand years before he was finally able to send out a telepathic thought to the enlisting bases all over the universe to summon the new-generation warriors.

"However, because of the long time passed, most of the enlisting bases had been ruined or run out of energy, ending up as cold and silent graves.

"The Blood God was also too weak. His telepathic thought was seriously jammed during the transmission and might not have reached every enlisting base. In the end, perhaps only 1% of the bases heard his calling.

"In the end, I was the only warrior who showed up before him, and it seemed that nobody would come again for a long time.

"Having no other choices, the Blood God could only place all his power and hope in me. He taught me everything he knew, which included bizarre knowledge that human beings could not understand with their wisdom, hoping that I could accomplish the mission."

Li Yao pricked his ears, knowing that it was important. He asked in a hurry, "What mission?"

"First of all, to sneak into the Empyreal Terminus Sector, find the Gold Crystal Pyramid, modify it on a large scale, and start the Next Sun Plan."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said frankly, "But it's not so easy to sneak into the Empyreal Terminus Sector, and it's impossible to modify the Gold Crystal Pyramid even if I can sneak it. Besides, the energy of a whole planet is needed to power the Gold Crystal Pyramid!

"It had been more than nine thousand years since the collapse of the Star Ocean Imperium. With the unique astronomical features of the Empyreal Terminus Sector, it must be the capital of a powerful force. I cannot activate the Gold Crystal Pyramid until I get the place under control.

"To unearth, modify and use the Gold Crystal Pyramid, I had to control this planet; to control the planet, I had to defeat the powerful force that regarded this planet as their capital; and to defeat them, I would need a strong and loyal troop!

"The ways to modify the Gold Crystal Pyramid were based on the clues on the books left by the Nuwa Clan, and the books were actually from some members of the Houyi Clan who joined the Nuwa Clan later. It seemed that the zealots of the Houyi Clan were hunted when their scheme to threaten the Pangu Clan with the Gold Crystal Pyramid was discovered, so they had to join the Nuwa Clan's side. But the Nuwa Clan did not want to destroy the sun or brainwash anyone with the Gold Crystal Pyramid. They only wanted to release the extraterritorial devils with the facility so that every carbon-based life could hear the extraterritorial devils. So, they came up with the plans for modification.

"The modification was only theoretically possible. A lot of experiments were needed to figure out its feasibility in reality.

"All in all, I have to find more enlisting bases, or rather, 'Channeling Temples', of the Human Expedition Army first."

"Hold on a second—"

Li Yao raised his hand. "What are Channeling Temples? Is channeling a special technique of the Covenant Alliance to communicate via brainwaves of a special frequency?"

"Yes, but it's not the Covenant Alliance's special technique. It's a technique of the Pangu civilizations' alliance and its cornerstone."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said casually, "Like I said to you before, talking and writing are very ineffective means of communication that are only suitable for planetary civilizations. It's no different from the howling of beasts.

"Interstellar civilizations, particularly the diversified interstellar civilizations that have multiple races, have to count on energy waves, especially brainwaves, to communicate with each other.

"How could the Houyi Clan describe the magnificent solar storm that they witnessed on the orbit, how could the Zhurong Clan describe the ten thousand hues of a fire and the information that they read from burning matter, and how could the Gonggong Clan describe the feeling when they were gradually melted into the water in the boundless ocean with words?

"Those feelings cannot be expressed in any language, but they were the core of those carbon-based lives. If they could not understand one another, it was impossible for them to strike a stable alliance, and a war could consume all creatures.

"So, after the Pangu civilizations' alliance was established, plenty of Channeling Temples were set up on every planet. You can consider them to be certain… brainwave intensifying buildings. They're the centers of socialization, education, and administration. All the members of the alliance have to visit the Channeling Temple regularly to communicate with other carbon-based lives so that they could understand each other and the nature of life better.

"You can also consider the Channeling Temple as a special religious building, where the Zhurong Clan who played with furious fire every day could understand the Gonggong Clan who lived in water and could even transfer their souls to fish, and where the Kuafu Clan who were half-plants and half-animals could understand the Houyi Clan who were like a swarm of insects.

"In the meantime, the Pangu Clan, as the leader of the alliance, could send instructions regarding the future of the civilization into every citizen's head via the Channeling Temples. The orders such as wiping out feelings and destroying all the deep-space exploration starships were all issued and carried out through the Channeling Temples."

"Got it."

Li Yao snorted and said, "Channeling Temple is just a fancy name for 'brainwashing center'. It seems that the thirteen primeval clans each had different talent, and the Pangu Clan's talent was to mess with other people's heads."

"I wouldn't object if you insist that such an advanced means of communication is brainwashing."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said peacefully, "However, channeling is not the only way of brainwashing. I've seen too many corrupt officials of the Star Ocean Republic who tricked millions of people with their sophistry. The shameless Wuying Qi 2.0 was also an excellent speaker who could courage millions of people to die for his ambitions with only a few words. They did not need a Channeling Temple at all.

"Technology is just technology. There are many ways to brainwash people if you want, and channeling is just one of them.

"Of course, in the last years of the primeval age, the Channeling Temple did become a holy sanctuary for human beings, the new servant species. Both the human beings manufactured in factories and those who were naturally reproduced would be sent into the Channeling Temples regularly to be inculcated with knowledge and loyalty. Sometimes, the Channeling Temple was even part of every man-manufacturing factory, and they coexisted all the time."

Li Yao recalled the black city that was purely made of geometrical shapes on Kunlun. There were indeed some buildings that looked like sanctuaries. Back then, the researchers wondered what those religious buildings that boasted a unique style were for.

"Thanks to the legacy of the Nuwa Clan, the Blood God learned of the locations of many man-manufacturing factories and Channeling Temples. So, as long as he could find those places, he could create a troop that was absolutely loyal to me—or him. Although the Nuwa Clan worshiped absolute freedom and chaos and this did not befit their ideology, neither me nor the Blood God was really a follower of the Nuwa Clan. There was no time to consider too much in the emergency."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "After training with the Blood God for ten years, I left with all his knowledge and a new mission.

"At that time, the Star Ocean Republic, which regarded the Empyreal Terminus Sector as the capital, was declining, but it was a powerful country that had developed for thousands of years anyway. I knew that I could not defeat it by myself.

"So, instead of entering the territory of the Star Ocean Republic, I explored the edge of the cosmos, until I reached several Sectors that belong to the Covenant Alliance today. Of course, the Covenant Alliance wasn't established yet back then. Those Sectors were still far-flung places with few resources. Bloody battles took place all the time.

"However, I knew very well that several man-manufacturing factories and Channeling Temples were in those Sectors!"

Chapter 2662: Default Setting

Li Yao took a deep, deep breath and recalled the miserable Nepenthe believers deep below the ground.

Perhaps Long Yangjun was correct. The Covenant Alliance was not created by the Pangu Clan, but by human beings themselves.

"How could you have discovered the man-manufacturing factory or the Channeling Temple on your own?"

Li Yao asked in a cold voice, "Did the local warlords not realize your scheme?"

"Wrong. They noticed my scheme very early. Or rather, I volunteered to tell them part of my scheme."

With a mysterious and creepy smile on his face, Wuying Qi 3.0 said casually, "Don't you see? Thousands of years of warfare had twisted the minds of the local experts at the border of the cosmos. They called themselves Cultivators, but they were nothing like you. They were just bugs under the skin of Cultivators.

"Those bugs had been fighting each other so brutally that they were essentially crazy devils. They craved new weapons and new resources and would pay any price to kill their sworn enemies.

"Who would refuse it if I told them that there was a way to manufacture incessant super slaves and super warriors?

"I hid my strength that came from the Blood God and pretended to be a harmless archaeologist. I 'accidentally' discovered the remains of some Channeling Temples and man-manufacturing factories, and a local warlord 'accidentally' learned it. That idiot was tricked and threatened me to create an invincible army for him so that he could swallow the dozens of Sectors nearby.

"Of course, he also provided tremendous resources for me so that I could study further and repair the Channeling Temples and man-manufacturing factories.

"After a lot of setbacks and hard work, I repaired a man-manufacturing factor and the correspondent Channeling Temple several years later. Actually, the man-manufacturing factory was not important, because human beings had great reproductive capabilities themselves. It was too costly and time-consuming to develop new humans with the man-manufacturing factory. So, the better choice was to capture the ordinary people, wipe out their feelings, and control their souls in the Channeling Temples. Although the quality of the slaves and soldiers created in such a way was not guaranteed, their quantity was huge and their cost was low. It is still the regular way for the Covenant Alliance to create new blood even to this day.

"The warlord received a batch of slaves who were emotionless, uncomplaining, and would die before they stopped working, as well as an iron-blooded army who was fearless and relentless and would sacrifice themselves to accomplish a mission. So, he grew much more powerful and swallowed multiple Sectors.

"But good days did not last long. When he secured consecutive victories, his enemies discovered their own Channeling Temples and modified their subjects into emotionless, absolutely loyal slaves and soldiers with the same technology. You should be smart enough to know that it was me who secretly leaked the technology and even guided other warlords to building their own Channeling Temples under different identities."

"Got it. The slaves and soldiers brainwashed by the Channeling Temple were loyal to the local warlords on the surface, but there were some fundamental instructions deep inside their genes and souls that, once activated, would shift their loyalty to you!"

Li Yao said coldly, "It's a pity that your plan went wrong in the end. You thought that you could manipulate all the warlords, but you accidentally triggered the most original function of the Channeling Temples, making the brainwashed people pledge their loyalty to the Pangu Clan!"

"You're not wrong at all, but the question was not about the warlords, because I could easily deal with those idiots with 10% of my computational ability."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "The problem was about the Blood God. It was the conflict between me and the Blood God that got things out of control."

"The conflict between you and the Blood God?"

Li Yao found it surprising. "What was the conflict between you and the Blood God that made you kill him in the end?"

"It was all the fault of the evil, weak, vile, childish, and hilarious Wuying Qi 2.0."

Wuying Qi 3.0 mumbled, "You should know that, despite everything I had been through, I was essentially a weak, mortal man who was fascinated by the worldly entertainments.

"The Supreme Emperor and the Blood God might hold different beliefs, but they both considered for the civilization of mankind and the ultimate mysteries of the universe beyond. They pursued the answers to the questions that might not carry any answer at all.

"Those questions and those pursuits were incomprehensible for Wuying Qi 2.0 at that time.

"For Wuying Qi 2.0, the Blood God's goal was hilarious. Even if the multiverse did exist, it was still perilous to climb across the black wall. Besides, who knew what was lying in the universe beyond the wall? There were too many uncertainties. It was too far-fetched!

"Even if the Blood God was right that mankind would be starved in this tiny universe as the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and many other primordial civilizations did sooner or later, it would take at least hundreds of thousands of years before it became reality. Then, why should a weakling like Wuying Qi 2.0 worry for the people hundreds of thousands of years later?

"If Wuying Qi 2.0 mastered an invincible army through the Blood God's method, he would have many better things to do. He could seek his revenge, he could thud upon the Cultivators and the ordinary people, he could dominate this tiny universe, he could change everyone with his ideas, and he and his offspring could reign over this universe for at least ten thousand years!

"He had so many things to do. Why would he climb the black wall, search for the multiverse, and likely perish in space storms without leaving a single cell behind together with the crazy Blood God like an idiot?

"The future of mankind was not as important as his present. Wuying Qi 2.0 believed in that. So, after he realized the power at his disposal, he betrayed the Blood God without any hesitation.

"Supposedly, he should make the people who were being brainwashed in the Channeling Temples obey the Blood God's command and gain ambitions to explore the grand universe.

"However, he tampered with the Blood God's command and changed the target of their loyalty to himself. He intended to eradicate the brainwashed people's loyalty, reverence, and obedience to the Blood God.

"However, his command and the Blood God's had a serious conflict in the souls of the brainwashed. As a result, the minds of most of the brainwashed people were reset… As a refiner, you should know that a lot of magic equipment has default settings and that if things go wrong, the user can restore the default settings by pressing a button easily, right?

"The brainwashed people were the same. Because of the serious conflict between Wuying Qi 2.0's command and the Blood God's, which led to chaos on the genetic and soul level, another command that was at the bottom of their genes was activated. Their 'default settings' which were about the absolute loyalty to the Pangu Clan were restored.

"Eventually, things went out of control. The guys whose default settings were restored did not obey local warlords, Wuying Qi 2.0, or the Blood God. They acted on their own and controlled the man-manufacturing factories and the Channeling Temples in a mysterious pattern hundreds of thousands of years ago. Like viruses, they spread out their people and tried to resurrect the Pangu Clan.

"The local warlords could not resist those human-shaped puppets at all. I did not know how to stop the absurdities either.

"Thankfully, I had collected enough experiment data and gained a deeper understanding of the improvement of the inculcation technology. So, I simply left…"

"You simply left after causing such a huge disaster?"

Li Yao found it hard to believe. "You are truly an irresponsible jacka*s!"

"Yes, Wuying Qi 2.0 was indeed an out-and-out jacka*s, but in his defense…"

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "We did not know the power of the Channeling Temples as well as we do today. Nobody could foresee how much longer the chaos would last. Since it was in a destitute place at the border of the cosmos, those human-shaped puppets that did not have a lot of resources might die out soon.

"With that in mind, Wuying Qi 2.0 finally returned to the Star Ocean Republic with the experiment data and the wealth he looted from the warlords. In the following decades, he built up his influence in the Star Ocean Republic.

"But thanks to the previous lesson, he dare not touch the Channeling Temples again, which was too dangerous and might get himself killed.

"He lived in peace for many years in the Star Ocean Republic, until the news that the Alliance of the Holy Covenant spread out. He was greatly shocked.

"However, on second thought, he realized that the arrival of the Covenant Alliance also created the best opportunity for him to assume the supreme power of the sea of stars!"

Chapter 2663: The Sunspot Plan!

"You raised an enemy for your own sake!"

Li Yao couldn't help but snort as he said, "I knew that the Covenant Alliance and the Imperium were the two sides of the same coin. They're both venomous flowers that grew from the corpse of the Star Ocean Republic. But it still puzzles me how you defeated the Covenant Alliance again and again and became a famous general when you did not have a powerful troop."

"It's very simple. Not all the people of the Covenant Alliance were reset."

Wuying Qi 2.0 explained, "While more than 90% of the people of the Covenant Alliance were reset and had pledged their loyalty to the Pangu Clan, which was gone hundreds of thousands of years ago, there were still some people who received my soul mark and swore their loyalty to Wuying Qi 2.0.

"However, this soul mark had existed like some sort of recessive genes. They did not know it themselves until it was activated.

"But once activated, they would be my most loyal and secretive spies inside the Covenant Alliance.

"Though their population was not huge, they could expose the intelligence about the Covenant Alliance to me, including the Covenant Alliance's route of attack, details of their warships, and commanders, etc. They could also sabotage the Covenant Alliance from within.

"With those spies, as well as the war abilities that I inherited from the Blood God, I was naturally invincible!"

Li Yao was dumbfounded by the truth.

"Just like that, I built up my power and secretly weakened the Star Ocean Republic in the war against the Covenant Alliance. When the timing was ripe, I overthrew that corrupt regime of Cultivators and established a country of my own!"

Wuying Qi 3.0 said casually, "You may be wondering why I didn't destroy the Covenant Alliance after I established the Imperium of True Human Beings since it was no longer useful. After all, my attack should be unstoppable under the help of the spies after I claimed the most prosperous planet at the center of the cosmos, right?

"I thought exactly the same at that time. Even though the Covenant Alliance was thriving, I did not consider it a big deal, because I believed that the spies were enough to help me eliminate the Covenant Alliance any time I wanted.

"At that time, I had a greater enemy to deal with. Guess who he was?"

After a long silence, Li Yao said, "… The Blood God."

"That's right. The Imperium of True Human Beings was part of the deal between me and the Blood God. He helped me gain supreme power, and I would establish a glorious expedition army with this supreme power to explore the outer lands."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "However, Wuying Qi 2.0 did not want to fulfill his promise. He was scared that the Blood God would learn of the Covenant Alliance and realize his betrayal.

"The Blood God had been asleep during Wuying Qi 2.0's rise and couldn't have known what he did. However, nobody could tell what would happen if the Blood God woke up again or whether or not a second 'new-generation warrior' would be summoned to him.

"So, Wuying Qi 2.0 had no time to destroy the Covenant Alliance at all. He had to concentrate all his forces to finish his teacher, the Blood God!"

Behind Wuying Qi 3.0, the blood gradually fell off, and a new picture was revealed.

Infinite starships and Immortal Cultivators, led by Blackstar the Great, returned to the debris of Doom, found whatever was left of the Blood God, and woke up the sleeping devil.

Wuying Qi reported what he had done in the past decades to the Blood God and asked him to inspect his troop. At the Blood God's most vulnerable moment, he launched a war at the Blood God!

Li Yao genuinely felt the cruelty of the war even though he was only watching it based on pictures of blood.

"I had carefully prepared for decades. I gathered the strongest Immortal Cultivators of the Imperium and countless primeval treasures and super warships."

Wuying Qi 3.0's voice still carried fear as he said, "But the Blood God almost defeated us even though he barely had any strength left. Everybody died. Most starships were destroyed. Even I, his 'true disciple', was almost obliterated when I finally eliminated him.

"Li Yao, do you really think that I couldn't do anything about the noble families from the Star Ocean Republic, which expanded into the four Kurfürst families today?

"Hehe. Those families are nothing. Had it not been for the Blood God, I would've eradicated all of them. The four Kurfürst families wouldn't have appeared at all!

"Pitifully, the war against the Blood God consumed the best soldiers who had fought under my command for decades loyally and heavily wounded me, to the point that I was neither able to deal with the remains of the noble families nor to destroy the Covenant Alliance before it grew big.

"I knew that I was dying, but I was unwilling to die just like that. So, I dedicated all my attention and resources to the quest for the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum. My fatal wound was left by the Blood God, which couldn't be treated by any medication in the universe. Maybe, only the power of the Supreme Emperor could save me and even help me grow even stronger.

"If so, neither the remains of the noble families nor the rising Covenant Alliance would really be a problem.

"Just like that, toward the end of my life, I set off again with all the information I knew of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum in the shuttle that carried me to the Blood God years back. After a long search, I finally found the mausoleum.

"It's a pity that it was too late when I found it. My body had withered and was about to turn into a pool of blood like the Blood God did, and my soul was weaker than ever. Even though I had the key to the central part of the mausoleum, I could not open the gate.

"I could only wipe out all the traces of my existence and hide in eternal sleep, waiting for newcomers to arrive. A thousand years later, Li Linghai and the guy from the Covenant Alliance came… You know what happened next."

Li Yao had a lot of complicated feelings after listening to Wuying Qi's long and eventful life.

Wuying Qi was indeed an unforgivable fiend, but his life was full of astonishing tides like a magnificent river.

Despite knowing that he might not tell the truth, Li Yao couldn't help but ask, "You're going to die soon. Are you regretful about your life?"

After a long silence, Wuying Qi 3.0 shook his head. "You are only regretful if you made the wrong choice when you could've made a better one. But for Wuying Qi 2.0 a thousand years ago, he could not understand the Blood God who had the multiverse and the future of the civilization in mind. He would betray the Blood God and pursue power in the mortal world even if it were repeated a hundred times.

"However, a thousand years later, after I've tasted the supreme power, experienced the most shameless betrayal, stomped on all the rules in the world, and boasted the wealth that everybody dreamed about, I'm gradually evolving from 2.0 to 3.0. I can more or less understand and even agree with the Blood God right now.

"When the Next Sun Plan was implemented, and when 2.0 me was fighting you, 3.0 me was questioning myself incessantly. What am I doing? Is it really fun to compete with insignificant ants for insignificant power? Is it really not wasteful that the Next Sun Plan is regarded as an approach to seize the highest power instead of the beginning of a glorious civilization? Even if I can reign over the Imperium for another ten thousand years, so what? Can I really find the significance of my life?

"At that moment, I had an epiphany. I rose to a whole new level and fully understood the Blood God's legacy, which helped me realize the significance of my life. I upgraded from 2.0 to 3.0!

"Now, I'm dying quickly, but like I said before, when you learn the truth in the morning, you can die in peace at night. I've found the significance of my life. My journey can come to a full stop without any regret. But I still have to pass on the Blood God's legacy to someone else, and it's going to be you. It has to be you, Li Yao."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled, like a beloved teacher and father. He extended his gray arm to Li Yao, as if he were going to rub Li Yao's face.

Creeped out, Li Yao jumped back and shouted, "Hey! Let's focus on talking! Don't lay your hand on me! What exactly is this legacy that you're talking about?"

"First of all, the ways to modify and use the Gold Crystal Pyramid. I believe that you're going to need it in your future journey."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled. "Also, the Sunspot Plan. It's the key for you to defeat the Covenant Alliance, and it's my greatest trump card. Do you not want it?"

"The Sunspot Plan?"

Li Yao blinked his eyes. "You mean the literal spots on the sun?"

"You can understand it however you see fit."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "Do you remember that about 90% of the people of the Covenant Alliance were reset but the rest were actually loyal to me under the control of their recessive genes? Now, a thousand years have passed, and those spies have naturally died. However, the recessive genes are still passed on in their offspring. Their offspring are exactly the sunspots!"

Chapter 2664: Opportunity to the Divinity Branching Stage!

Li Yao found it hard to believe. "It's been a thousand years, but the 'sunspots' still obey you unconditionally because of the recessive genes?"

"That's exactly the mystery of genes and soul."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said casually, "Hundreds of thousands of years have passed, but human beings are still scared of spiders and snakes. Even the harmless snakes can still raise disgust and fear. It is because spiders and snakes were major threats to mankind when they still lived in the woods. Such fear and obedience on the genetic level are unreasonable.

"Of course, the 'intervention' that I imposed on those people a thousand years ago was on a shallow level, so the recessive genes are gradually losing effect after generations. A thousand years ago, maybe 5% of people carried such genes, but a thousand years later, only 1% or 0.5% of the people are still loyal to me.

"They might not be large in number, but 0.5% of spies are more than enough on a volatile and unpredictable battlefield, aren't they?

"At the very least, you can learn of the specific location and strategy of the Covenant Alliance's army via the sunspots. Even if only one of the commanders on the enemy's side happens to be a sunspot loyal to us, we won't lose a battle, will we?"

Li Yao thought for a moment and realized that it did make sense.

Sometimes, one or two spies at a critical position would be enough to change the outcome of a campaign.

If 0.5% to 1% of its total population were spies, how could the Covenant Alliance win this war?

No wonder Wuying Qi was so fearless.

On the surface, the reformists did not stand a chance under the dual attack of the four families and the Covenant Alliance. But actually, if the Next Sun Plan had worked out, he would've obtained the ace fleets of the four families and a ferocious army of extraterrestrial devils. With the spies within the Covenant Alliance, he was likely to win in the end.

However, the Sunspot Plan was definitely not as simple as Wuying Qi described. Pondering for a moment, Li Yao said, "Even if you're speaking the truth, how are you going to activate those sunspots? You don't know who or where they are!"

"Exactly. That's why you have to be the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, which is not only the key to the Next Sun Plan but also the key to the Sunspot Plan."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled. "I do not need to find those sunspots in person at all. All I have to do is to launch my brainwaves to the Sectors that the fleets of the Covenant Alliance are likely to be hiding in, and the sunspots will automatically receive it, like how crystal processors can automatically connect to the Spiritual Nexus."

"Are you serious?"

Li Yao frowned. "Does it mean infuriating the sun and destroying the Empyreal Terminus Sector again?"

"No. The Next Sun Plan requires the enhancement and magnification of the sun because it needs to brainwash a whole Sector, including a lot of Immortal Cultivators who are firm and strong."

Wuying Qi 3.0 explained, "But in the Sunspot Plan, you don't have to brainwash billions of people. All you need to do is to send your brainwaves into the heads of those spies via the Spiritual Nexus. Precision is more important, and not much energy is required. So, the crystals here should be enough. There's no need to get the sun involved.

"If you think carefully, you'll know that I'm not lying. The Sunspot Plan is a subsequent procedure of the Next Sun Plan, and the sun of the Empyreal Terminus Sector should've been infuriated according to the Next Sun Plan. I would have to control the solar storms, or the sun might expand and swallow the whole capital planet. In such a case, the Gold Crystal Pyramid would be destroyed. How could I carry out the Sunspot Plan then?"

Li Yao was convinced, but he had new questions. "Wait, I know a thing or two about the Spiritual Nexus. You can't fool me. The Imperium of True Human Beings and the Alliance of the Holy Covenant can't be using the same system of Spiritual Nexus, right? Even if your brainwaves can be transmitted to the border of the Imperium through the Gold Crystal Pyramid, how are they going to jump to the Covenant Alliance? Don't tell me that your mental power is so strong that you can penetrate into the Covenant Alliance's Spiritual Nexus!"

"Of course it's impossible."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "I tried launching my soul into the Spiritual Nexus via the Gold Crystal Pyramid and jumping into the Covenant Alliance, but like you said, the universe is too vast and the Spiritual Nexus is too unsteady. Also, the Covenant Alliance's network is highly encrypted and defended. It's barely possible for me to sneak in. My soul might even be heavily wounded when they detect me as a crystal processor virus.

"However, my brainwaves do not have to invade the Covenant Alliance at all. All I need to do is to wait for the main forces of the Covenant Alliance to come to the Imperium, don't I?"

Li Yao's eyes widened. "Are you talking about the invasion of the Covenant Alliance?"

"Exactly."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled and said, "The Covenant Alliance did not try its best but volunteered to retreat during the Imperium's counterattack to stretch out the Imperium's supply lines so that the Imperium would waste a lot of precious resources on the newly-conquered areas. Then, the Covenant Alliance would take advantage of the weakness and try to destroy the Imperium!

"How could I have neglected something that even Lei Chenghu had noticed?

"So, the main forces in the Covenant Alliance must be lurking in darkness while waiting for the best opportunity.

"At this moment, two great battles are going on in the Seven Seas Space Zone and the capital at the same time, and the reformists, the expedition army, and the four families have devoted their best troops to the battles. So, this is the best moment for the Covenant Alliance!

"So, I'm certain that the main forces of the Covenant Alliance have taken action since the moment they learned of the battles. They may be marching to the heart of the Imperium right now!

"Hehe. There are few things I could do about them when they were hiding in their nest, but now that they've entered the Imperium, they're within the Spiritual Nexus of the Imperium, and my telepathic thoughts can be sent into the heads of the sunspots through the Gold Crystal Pyramid and the Spiritual Nexus.

"Regular people of the Covenant Alliance won't have any reaction to my telepathic thoughts, but the sunspots are very sensitive to it and will be woken by me. Then, wouldn't this fleet of the Covenant Alliance be crushed easily?"

"Huh…"

Li Yao truly did not foresee Wuying Qi's plan.

The Covenant Alliance thought that they could take advantage of the civil war within the Imperium. Little did they know that they were jumping into Wuying Qi's trap.

"Now, the Sunspot Plan needs some minor revisions."

Wuying Qi 3.0 looked at Li Yao and said, "Supposedly, I'm the commander of Sunspot Plan, and the sunspots will obey all my commands unconditionally after their recessive genes are awakened.

"But you're not me. You'll have some features of my soul at best. In the eyes of those sunspots, I'm their true god, and you are a priest who masters some of the divine power at best.

"Therefore, it remains unknown whether or not you can carry out the Sunspot Plan, reach out to those sunspots, and convince them to follow your command.

"But in any case, to take care of the invasion of the Covenant Alliance with minimal cost, you can't ignore the Sunspot Plan, and to activate the Sunspot Plan, you have to become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid."

Li Yao frowned and thought for a long time.

Wuying Qi 3.0's statement sounded legitimate, but his suggestion was also highly risky.

"Are you still hesitating whether or not you should take the risk? I'll give you more reasons."

Wuying Qi 3.0 smiled. "Now that you've got the whole key, you are free to explore the secrets of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum. Li Linghai will tell you the coordinates of this mausoleum even if I don't. However, both you and Li Linghai are too weak for the exploration. As Divinity Transformation Stage experts, you won't be able to enter the final gate until hundreds of years later.

"Divinity Branching Stage. You need to reach at least the Divinity Branching Stage to open the gate in the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum and resist the ultimate power from ten thousand years ago!

"Do you want to taste the feeling of the Divinity Branching Stage? Do you want to evolve into a more marvelous life form? I can tell that you're beyond the peak of the Divinity Transformation Stage, and all you need is an opportunity. So, the Gold Crystal Pyramid and the Sunspot Plan will be your best training tools. Project your telepathic thoughts into the sunspots via the Gold Crystal Pyramid and experience the feeling of living as dozens of individuals simultaneously. That will be your opportunity to reach the Divinity Branching Stage!"

"Well…"

Li Yao gasped and swallowed hard.

"I can offer you the ways to use the Gold Crystal Pyramid as well as the lessons I learned when I tried to advance into the Divinity Branching Stage for free."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said enticingly, "I truly mean you no harm. But of course, you are free to consider that I'm dark and malicious. However, everything comes with a cost. How is it possible to defeat the Covenant Alliance easily and enter the Divinity Branching Stage without taking any risk? This is a choice and a challenge. Cultivator Li Yao, do you dare to accept this challenge?"

Chapter 2665: All The Same...

"I'm not a three-year-old. Stop bewildering me with such childish talks, alright?"

Furrowing his brows, Li Yao mocked, "Maybe you do have a point, but for some reason, your tone makes me unhappy, and I really want to refuse you! Even if someone has to control the Gold Crystal Pyramid, why does it have to be me? Some people are more suitable than me, like Li Jialing, right?"

"Li Jialing, the young lion, is indeed more suitable than you from a certain perspective."

Wuying Qi 3.0 said, "But he belongs to Wuying Qi 2.0. It's an agreement between me and Wuying Qi 2.0. He will receive Wuying Qi 2.0's legacies, including Wuying Qi 2.0's unparalleled power as well as Blackhole's Heart. That Colossus is not suitable for you, and your Arsonist is one of the best Colossi itself. But other than that, everything concerning the Gold Crystal Pyramid is yours.

"You are definitely free to give the Gold Crystal Pyramid to anyone including Li Jialing, but are you sure you're going to do that? Think about it carefully. If you give the Gold Crystal Pyramid, you will be at fault when he turns into the next dragon.

"Anybody else will be the same. Whoever receives the Gold Crystal Pyramid is likely to become a dragon. Then, who do you want to become a dragon?"

Li Yao was rendered speechless for a moment.

"Undoubtedly, you can be a dragon slayer for your life, whose hands are forever clean, killing one dragon after another. But someone has to be the dragon, right? Dragons are indispensable for the advancement of mankind."

Wuying Qi 3.0's smile was even creepier, and his voice crawled into Li Yao's heart like invisible bugs. "Stop hesitating. It's not the time for moral lessons. Yes, the technology of massive inoculation is deeply flawed, and I should've looked for a better solution.

"But in this pathetic reality, it's often the case that we have to choose among the deeply-flawed plans to take care of complicated problems.

"If we leave the future of the Star Glory Federation aside; if we ignore whether or not Li Linghai and Lei Chenghu will liberate all hominoids, and whether or not it will cause another turmoil in the Imperium; and if we leave the Covenant Alliance aside—yes, let's put everything aside for now…

"Then, what about the new-generation virtual lives—Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King?

"You know better than anybody else how powerful the information lives, virtual lives, crystal processor lives, or mechanical lives are. Since the day the crystal processor and the spiritual puppet were invented, countless people had dreaded that they would replace humans and even enslave and destroy the obsolete human beings.

"Such robots and cyborgs are so strong, swift, and elegant. Their very existence highlights the sluggishness, weakness, and insignificance of obsolete humans. Humans view them with helplessness, jealousy, fury, and fear, just like how the Pangu Clan viewed humans. One day, they will replace the obsolete humans as new humans, and this process won't be peaceful and painless. A war of blood and fire that is a hundred times more horrible than any wars in the past will be inevitable!

"Face the reality. It's impossible for mankind to gladly accept Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King. Now, Boss Bai and Long Yangjun have accepted Xiaoming and Wenwen partly because they are sort of eccentrics themselves, partly because they boasted unparalleled computation and analysis abilities, and most importantly, it's because Xiaoming and Wenwen are still too weak and do not seem dangerous at all.

"However, when the new-generation information lives thrive, and the billions of ordinary people—who are idiotic, weak, selfish, insecure, shortsighted and easily-provoked—learn of them, do you think that they will gladly accept the new lives like you, Boss Bai, and Long Yangjun did?

"That's impossible. There's bound to be a war, just like the war that destroyed the civilization in the Martial Meritocrats Sector. Nobody knows the consequence when billions of ordinary people are instigated better than I do. They will be completely unreasonable!

"If you want the world to accept virtual lives with no bloodshed, and if you want mankind to peacefully reach the new phase where reality and illusion coexist, use the Gold Crystal Pyramid!

"Yes, it sounds like brainwashing if you do that, but you will be able to stop the total war between mankind and information lives. Wouldn't it be in the best interest of your civilization?"

Li Yao's eyes were like boiling swamps.

"Hold on a second—"

He almost lost his calmness when he discovered something impossible. "How can you know about Xiaoming and Wenwen? I have never told you anything about them. I buried the memories of them so deeply in my soul that you couldn't have read it even when you brainwashed me. You're not supposed to know them!"

"Yes, Wuying Qi can't know Xiaoming and Wenwen, but he somehow knows. How are you going to explain this self-contradiction?"

Wuying Qi 3.0 chuckled. His face blurred, and his voice changed into a tone that Li Yao was very familiar with. "The reason is simple. Because I'm already dead. The real Wuying Qi died when he detonated his soul. What happened next was that his memories, his power, and his soul pieces entered your brain, and you digested and absorbed them.

"I'm not Wuying Qi, but you are. It was not Wuying Qi who tried to persuade you to use the Gold Crystal Pyramid; it was yourself. You know the value of the Gold Crystal Pyramid and that you have to get it under control. That's why you created me, to convince yourself with Wuying Qi's image.

"You knew that you were destined to be the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid from the very beginning, but you still needed an excuse, and I was your excuse. That can't be helped. Human beings are all hypocritical. Even someone at the peak of the Divinity Transformation Stage can barely cross the mental obstacle on his own.

"Now, you have completely overcome the obstacle. Do you realize who you are, and who I am?

Wuying Qi 3.0's shadow rippled out and changed into Li Yao's own appearance, who looked at Li Yao peacefully.

It looked like a gigantic mirror in the depths of the memory palace that was reflecting Li Yao's heart.

Li Yao saw that his heart was examining himself unemotionally.

Bam!

The enormous mirror fell apart, and its pieces reflected ten thousand different Li Yao from ten thousand different angles. Every Li Yao seemed to be caged in the mirror pieces, unable to break free. However, the cages were shattered in the next second, and the glittering powder gathered into infinite gray butterflies that replaced Li Yao above the volcano in the Martial Meritocrats Sector.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

The gray butterflies danced and fell on Li Yao like volcano ash and dirty snowflakes, leaking into his soul.

Wuying Qi's final legacy, including the ways to modify and use the Gold Crystal Pyramid, the details of the Sunspot Plan, the traps in the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum, his understandings when he reached the Divinity Branching Stage and upgraded to 3.1, were all melted into Li Yao's soul mysteriously and became part of Li Yao.

"I never figured out something that had bothered me for a thousand years until this moment."

Wuying Qi 3.0's voice echoed in Li Yao's head again, like Li Yao's own whisper. "How could I have slain an unparalleled man like the Blood God a thousand years ago when I grew strong only by accident and did not have any belief at all except for my addiction to power?

"The battle between me and the Blood God is like a nightmare etched to my soul. I thought that I was going to be killed by the Blood God. My final victory was uncanny and perplexing.

"But I've realized the answer at this moment.

"It's just like how a shameless cockroach like you could kill Blackstar the Great—as a matter of fact, I never really killed the Blood God; the Blood God has been living in my heart.

"I never received his full legacy, filled the last gap of his belief, and helped him finish the glorious journey of his life until the moment I killed him.

"Now, it's time for you to ask yourself the same question. Li Yao, have you really killed Wuying Qi? Can you really kill Wuying Qi? When this memory palace collapses and you wake up from unconsciousness, who will you be?

"Hehe. It's all the same. Perhaps everything is the same. As long as we can break out of this goddamn universe, the Blood God, Wuying Qi, Li Yao, Immortal Cultivators, Cultivators, and Demonic Cultivators will be no different…"

The memory palace trembled violently.

It was the sign of a collision.

Eventually, hundreds of gray butterflies constituted a gray statue, which vaguely looked like Wuying Qi or the Blood God or Li Yao himself. The statue extended its hand to Li Yao with a smile, but when Li Yao extended his hand to it, he realized that it was nothing but another mirror and a vivid reflection.

Boom!

Li Yao was suddenly shocked. The memory palace collapsed all of a sudden, and infinite memory pieces flooded into his head. As if he had slept for a thousand years, he woke up in excruciating pain!

Chapter 2666: Battle of Brothers!

He woke up from a thousand years of sleep. Like the bones that were rising from a black swamp as thick as oil, his memory pieces ran amok in his brain like glittering butterflies. The butterflies from a hundred years ago, a thousand years ago, and ten thousand years ago crashed, resulting in brilliant and hazy fire, which licked his brain cells and cleansed and reorganized his soul again and again.

He put on a mysterious smile. There were indescribable cunningness and unstoppable ambition in this smile.

He slowly opened his eyes, and the two swirls in his eyes stopped spinning, changing from the original black, white, and red into mysterious gray.

He moaned, like any person would after a good night's sleep. His immeasurable consciousness surfaced in the gray swirls; he was back. He was finally back!

Then, Li Yao saw a big hand that was turning larger and larger still before his hands, until it smacked his face brutally.

"Ouch!"

His facial organs were displaced, his teeth were dancing, and he was flung away in midair, but before he sensed the pain, he had been slapped again on the other side of his face.

The two slaps refreshed him and pulled him back from the lost abyss. He felt that he was not the Blackstar the Great who dominated the universe ten thousand years ago, nor Blackstar the Great who claimed the country a thousand years ago, but more like a big spinning gyro.

"Li Yao, wake up!"

He heard a determined voice, followed by another heavy blow on his face. The person shouted, "Don't be lost! Don't be deceived by Wuying Qi! Do you hear me? I'm your best friend, Long Yangjun! I have to wake you up today! I have to wake you up even if my soul and my life are burned out!"

A storm of fists, palms, knees, and feet landed on Li Yao's face and body mercilessly and incessantly.

"Ahhhhhhhhhhh!"

Li Yao meant to resist, but the previous battle against the remaining soul of Wuying Qi, or maybe the Blood God, had drained him. He was now exhausted and weaker than ever. How could he resist Long Yangjun's brutal savaging? He could only hold his head and scream like he was a pig being butchered.

"Go away! No matter whether you are Wuying Qi or anything even eviler, get away from my best friend, Li Yao!"

Long Yangjun roared deafeningly and kicked Li Yao crazily again, almost making Li Yao throw up. "Li Yao, hang in there. I'm trying to save you. You need to pull yourself together and fight side by side with me!"

"S-Stop kicking! It's me!"

Li Yao finally cried devastatingly, with tears running on his swollen face. "I'm Li Yao! I'm really Li Yao! What are you doing exactly?"

"Cut the crap! I don't believe you!"

Long Yangjun was still kicking him hard. "You must be Wuying Qi's remaining soul who has possessed Li Yao. I knew that you wouldn't be defeated so easily. Anyway, I'm going to kick you to death today!"

Li Yao felt that the whole planet had rolled over him hundreds of times.

Even the battle against Wuying Qi was not as painful as this.

"You're just getting back at me for what I did to you earlier!"

Li Yao shouted as loudly as possible, "It's me! It's really me! Stop beating! I've already taken care of Wuying Qi's remaining soul! It's really me! I'm going to fight back if you don't stop! I mean it!"

"Is it really… you, Li Yao?"

Long Yangjun kicked Li Yao brutally yet again before she finally stopped in satisfaction. But she still gazed at Li Yao vigilantly as if she would not hesitate to attack again if anything goes wrong.

"Of course! You know it!"

Grimacing in pain, Li Yao held his cheek and roared, "You just wanted to beat me up! You had always wanted to beat me up, hadn't you? You were only trying to get back at me! That's outrageous! Ouch. That's too outrageous!"

"Hehe, a scoundrel views all other people as scoundrels. It's true that I do not like you and often have the urge of punching you in the face, but I certainly wouldn't really do it without a good reason."

Long Yangjun rubbed her fists that had become swollen due to the beating. Appreciating her masterpiece, she said, "Just now, when you and Li Jialing fought Wuying Qi's remaining soul, Wuying Qi's remaining soul suddenly exploded into two streaks of light that dashed into each of you. Then, you became weird. You were like a rigid statue, with gray mist popping up. You were also murmuring to yourself nonstop, as if you were possessed.

"In order to save you, I summoned all my strength by burning out my soul and my life despite my exhaustion, determined to exterminate the evil power that possessed you even if I couldn't wake you up. But instead of thanking me, you're questioning my motive? That's too… Hahahahahaha. Sorry, I'm sorry. I didn't mean to beat you so hard. Hahahahahahahahahaha!"

The solemn expression on Long Yangjun's face collapsed. She laughed so hard that she could barely catch her breath.

"If you hadn't laughed in the end…"

Li Yao squinted and glared at Long Yangjun. "… I might have believed you!"

"I was not lying."

Long Yangjun took a deep breath and tried to stop laughing. She found a broken piece of a suit and gave it to Li Yao as a mirror. "Look at yourself right now."

"This is…"

Li Yao gasped hard as he looked at the mirror.

In the mirror, although his face had been deformed by Long Yangjun, there was grayness in his messy hair. Near his temples, the grayness looked like a flower or a mysterious crown!

"How did I turn into this?"

Li Yao stroked his hair and cried, "Why have I grown such mysterious gray hair? It makes me look elegant, unpredictable, and revered. This doesn't agree with me. I am so much more handsome right now that people will be too awed to talk to me!"

Long Yangjun found it hard to believe. "What are you talking about?"

"Nothing. I'm just testing my sense of humor."

Li Yao looked at himself in the mirror and took a long breath of relief. "Thankfully, I've maintained my basic sense of humor. I couldn't have said that if I were corrupted by Wuying Qi's soul, could I?"

"… Fine. You're right if you replace 'sense of humor' with shamelessness."

Long Yangjun thought for a moment and was not as wary as before. "Vulture Li Yao is the only person in the whole universe who can be so shameless. Wuying Qi's remaining soul couldn't have made such a statement. So, welcome back, Li Yao! By the way, have you really shattered Wuying Qi's remaining soul?"

"Well, it's rather mysterious. We'll talk about it later."

Li Yao asked in a hurry, "How long was I unconscious? Where is Jialing?"

"Five minutes. You've been unconscious for five minutes. Li Jialing is still lying there. His situation does not seem as bad as yours since he has been sound asleep, so I'm here to wake you up first," said Long Yangjun, clenching his fists.

"Only five minutes?"

Li Yao did not realize that such a long time in the memory palace was only a brief instant in reality.

However, just because Li Jialing was sleeping did not mean that his situation was not bad. On the contrary, the young lion might be in grave danger!

Right then, as Long Yangjun pointed at him, Li Jialing roared in agony.

Li Yao and Long Yangjun looked at each other, both feeling that it was not good.

By the time they rushed to him, Li Jialing had jerked like a lobster that fell into boiling oil.

His gold hair was wreathed in a black fog again, which corroded his hair and his skin like venom, as if it was going all the way into his flesh and his blood.

Under the excruciating pain, he transformed himself. His muscles were torn apart, and his bones expanded. He turned into a gargantuan monster like a standing lion!

"Hooooooooo!"

Li Jialing held his head tightly and spewed out plumes of black smoke. He writhed as if two armies were fighting in his head, leaving deep scratches on the floor that was as tough as iron.

"Jialing!"

Li Yao grew anxious. Nobody understood Li Jialing's situation better than him. Could this young man who was only in his twenties defeat Wuying Qi 2.0 with his own willpower?

Li Yao came close without caring about anything, hoping to wake Li Jialing up with brotherhood. "Listen to me! I'm Li Yao! I'm your big brother!"

"Hooooooo!"

Li Jialing, who had been caught by madness, heard nothing of it. He crashed Li Yao's chest like a black tornado and nearly ripped it apart. Li Yao was easily knocked away.

It was not Li Jialing's power at all, but the enormous legacy that Wuying Qi 2.0 bestowed upon him!

Pu!

Li Yao fell on the ground again and vomited blood crazily. When he sat up in dizziness, he found that Li Jialing was crouching not far away in a black fog and gazing at him bloodthirstily.

"Don't… Don't be rash! Let's just talk nicely!"

Creeped out, Li Yao hurried to open his hands to show that he was not malicious. He said sincerely, "We're brothers, aren't we? If you don't like the way I addressed you, I can call you big brother!"

Chapter 2667: True Dragon!

Li Jialing was briefly stunned, with amusement flashing in his eyes. But it only glowed for one moment before it was eclipsed by coldness and brutality.

His sharp talons that stuck out of his battle boots scratched the ground as he charged at Li Yao!

Li Yao had used up the last bit of strength in his muscles. There was nothing he could do when Li Jialing lunged at him.

Right before Li Jialing's claw cut his neck, a streak of brightness knocked him from one side to a hundred meters away.

The streak of brightness then gathered hundreds of broken pieces and darted them at Li Jialing.

It was exactly Long Yangjun, who had saved Li Yao again!

The broken pieces carried Long Yangjun's immense soul power. She quickly made a gesture and mumbled spells, with blood flowing out of her facial organs, while she directed the pieces within Li Jialing so that they could lock down the vehement power in him.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

Transparent electric arcs surged out and wandered all over Li Jialing, almost shocking his bones out.

"Hooooooo!"

Li Jialing roared in devastation and hopped up and down in the cage of electric arcs.

"What… What are you doing?" Li Yao shouted.

"I'm saving you, and killing him by the way!"

Long Yangjun said coldly, "Has it not occurred to you that he is too possessed to be saved? I have to take precautions like how I attacked you just now. If you hadn't woken up in time, I would have killed you mercilessly!"

"No, you can't do that!"

Li Yao pulled his hair; his heart in a mess.

"I know that you regard him as a blood brother. I like him very much too."

Long Yangjun gnashed her teeth. "But it's not the time for petty kindness. You know Wuying Qi's dreadfulness better than I do. There will be a greater tragedy if he's not killed! Also, didn't Li Jialing insist that we kill him if Wuying Qi controlled him before we set off?

"He would rather die than to let Wuying Qi bring disasters to this world in his appearance!"

"I know, I know, but—"

Li Yao breathed heavily and looked at Li Jialing who was struggling in the cage of electric arcs. He said in pain, "You can't kill him. You have no idea what he's going through. You can't kill Li Jialing! That will only activate Wuying Qi's power in him, making him trapped in darkness forever!"

"What?"

Long Yangjun's pupils constricted abruptly.

The cage of electric arcs that tied Li Jialing up was also shattered in deafening roars as Li Jialing's body expanded yet again. The black fog was both like a pair of wings and a suit of armor for him. He charged at Long Yangjun while waving his iron arms.

Boom!

Li Jialing and Long Yangjun had a head-on clash in midair. Both of them were consumed by the blast.

Li Jialing was only in the Nascent Soul Stage, whereas Long Yangjun boasted the power beyond the Divinity Transformation Stage and the mysterious legacies from the primeval age. Supposedly, Li Jialing would be no match for her.

Yet it was Long Yangjun who was pushed back by the blast.

Her right arm was twisted to a weird angle and dangled down. Like Li Yao, she vomited blood crazily and crashed on the ground!

"This is impossible!"

Long Yangjun was completely shocked as she held her broken arm, unable to believe her eyes. "How can Li Jialing's power be so dark and terrifying? He's no longer Li Jialing, but—"

"Hehehehe…"

The blast that just consumed Li Jialing and Long Yangjun was consumed by the intense black fog which turned into Wuying Qi's image. Like an armor, it enshrouded Li Jialing who was walking close.

Li Jialing stared at Li Yao and Long Yangjun in a cold and strange way, like a lion that was examining its prey.

"Jialing, stop and figure out what you want exactly!"

Holding back his pain, Li Yao stood up and roared desperately, "This is your war. We can't help you. You must rely on yourself to defeat Wuying Qi's soul! I trust that you can defeat him!"

"Humph!"

With a brutal smile on his face, Li Jialing waved his black claw, and a strong wind slashed Li Yao's chest like a sharp edge, blowing Li Yao backward again.

However, Li Yao did not fall this time. He gritted his teeth and persevered with a bloodstained smile.

"I already know Wuying Qi's plan. Is this the best you can do, Blackstar the Great?"

Li Yao burst into laughter. He grimaced in pain as his wounds were affected by his laughter, but he shouted again despite the pain, "Maybe, this is what Wuying Qi wished to see. He purposefully split himself into a 2.0 and a 3.0 so that we would fight each other!

"If we do fight each other like he wished, no matter who kills whom in the end, the survivor will become his puppet, his slave, and the successor of his pathetic destiny, never to escape!

"But I believe that you won't be tricked, Jialing. Everything is up to you now. My life, Long Yangjun's life, your mother's life, and the fate of the sea of stars is in your hands. You won't disappoint us or yourself. I trust you! I believe in you!"

"Hooooooo!"

Li Jialing's face became hideous and hesitating. He roared desperately and attacked Li Yao nonstop. Li Yao was knocked back and vomited blood. He barely looked like a human anymore.

But no matter how he was brutalized, Li Yao stood on the ground without falling like a nail.

"I… I won't fight back!"

Smiling miserably, Li Yao continued, "Because I believe that Wuying Qi 2.0 has always been a superficial weakling and the biggest loser. How can a loser like you defeat my most beloved brother? My brother won't kill me. He will only blow up your head with me!"

Li Jialing's eyes were bloodshot, and he breathed heavily. Black and gold flames of spiritual energy were burning from the cracks on his face. He staggered toward Li Yao, leaving burning footprints behind him.

"I… I…"

Murmuring in blurs, he slowly extended his claw to Li Yao. There was no telling whether he was going to tear Li Yao's heart apart, or if he was asking for his brother's help when he was sinking in the black swamp.

"Jialing, I'm always ready to help you, but not this time. You have to help me and everybody else."

Li Yao's head was as swollen as a ball, but his eyes were clearer than ever. He tried to put on a smile. "It seems that you made the right decision to sneak into the capital. Maybe you are the real hero of this war. Now, it's time to end it by blowing up Wuying Qi 2.0 and save us all!"

"I… I… I can't…"

Li Jialing's voice was shivering, and his face was in pain. The bones all over his body were cracking as he said, "Help… Help me… Brother… Brother Yao…"

He was still walking to Li Yao uncontrollably, and the claws where black venom was dripping were only one inch away from Li Yao's throat.

Li Yao, on the other hand, had backed off to a wall. He could not retreat any further.

"Fine. If brotherhood cannot wake you up, I guess we have to invite the holiest feeling in the world now—mother's love!"

Li Yao sighed and said, "There's nothing I can do now except to sing a few classic songs that praise mother's love for my good brother…"

Li Jialing quivered so hard as if a piton of ice had pierced into his skull through his whole body.

He was also woken up, like when Li Yao was beaten up by Long Yangjun!

Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

Infinite black gas was leaking from his face and his wounds, but before it escaped, it had been burnt up by the bright gold fire behind and purified into basic information and power.

Li Jialing's body was transformed and reborn again!

The tusks, claws, and abnormal muscles retreated, and his gold fur dropped one hair after another. He regained the appearance of a human, save his bulging canine teeth and the bright gold colors in his eyes and his hair.

He became the handsome young man that Li Yao saw at the very beginning again.

However, an ocean of ambitions and determination were hidden under his seemingly young appearance. Blackstar the Great's shadow and he had been perfectly melted and could not be separated anymore.

He gazed at his palm that was as smooth as jade.

He waved his hands, and a cluster of black fire was raised. He waved his hands again, and a gold fire consumed the black fire and turned into a raging dragon. On a closer look, the dragon even had a pair of purple eyes as if it were a living creature!

Boom!

Li Jialing rolled his hands and crumbled the purple-fire dragon. The broken fire spread out and gathered into a burning sword that ruled the universe in his hand. In the meantime, Li Yao and Long Yangjun both heard an earsplitting explosion that shouted, "True dragon is here!"

Chapter 2668: Life Is a Continuous Battle!

Only one technique could've demonstrated such a domineering fire: Dragon Over The Sky, which belonged to the Supreme Emperor ten thousand years ago!

The Blood God naturally knew the Supreme Emperor's technique. He taught it to Blackstar the Great ten thousand years later, and Blackstar the Great revealed it under Li Linghai's appearance when Li Yao met her in the Sand Primitive Sector.

Li Yao did not expect to sense the same power again in Li Jialing today.

It seemed that Li Jialing had completely absorbed Wuying Qi 2.0's power, or he couldn't have controlled his spiritual energy so deftly.

Li Yao looked at Li Jialing's hands and his own hands that were like chicken claws, before he suddenly grabbed his wrist and shook it hard.

Long Yangjun had finally suppressed the surging blood in herself. Noticing that Li Yao was shivering as if he suffered an electric shock, she asked curiously, "What are you doing?"

"I'm checking if I can create such a splendid fire too."

Li Yao shook his wrist for a long time, but nothing except for a mist that seemed to be sick was rising from his hand, and it dispersed the moment he got distracted. Li Yao was rather bummed. "Seriously? Wuying Qi left 99% of his power and battle experience to Li Jialing and an opportunity to become a vicious dragon to me? That's so unfair!"

"Ugh!"

Li Jialing watched the sword for a long time in fascination. Then, the blade suddenly fell apart into purple flames that died out soon. As if exhausted, Li Jialing became pale and fell on his knees, gagging. He vomited deep-purple blood that was mixed with black threads, as if countless parasites that had been luring in his body were being discharged.

As he vomited out the blood, Li Jialing was finally back to himself. But he seemed weak and exhausted.

He was a 20-year-old young man after all. He had fought Wuying Qi brutally earlier and inherited Wuying Qi's enormous power later. It was too much for him to absorb all at once.

Li Yao hurried to give him a hand and input feeble spiritual energy into his body.

Both of them had absorbed energy from the Imperial Fire Pearl and Wuying Qi's legacy. To some extent, their power was of the same origin, so it was easy for Li Jialing to digest Li Yao's spiritual energy.

Li Jialing moaned and murmured, "What—What happened to me? I feel that I just had a long dream. My head hurts so much."

"That's right. We both had a long nightmare. But it's over now."

Li Yao said solemnly, "Do you remember who you are and who I am?"

"I am… Li Jialing."

Looking at Li Linghai who was lying not far away, Li Jialing became firm again. "You're Brother Yao. We're fighting Wuying Qi. We've already defeated Wuying Qi!"

He jumped in delight, only to exclaim in pain as his wounds were affected. He couldn't help but touch his wounds, but what he felt was his new skin which was as smooth as that of a newborn baby.

"Has… Has my gold fur dropped?"

The young man was rather excited. "Not only have we defeated Wuying Qi, but I'm also back to normal! Wait, my hair is much longer, mixed with strands of gold hair. Is this the last trace of the Gold Lion? Huh. Brother Yao, your hair has turned partly gray!"

"That's right."

Li Yao scratched his hair and said, "Hair dyeing is popular this year."

"I'm still dizzy. There's a lot of new stuff in my head, stuff that is in a disorder and is unbelievable."

Li Jialing held his head and murmured, "I remember that I, Brother Yao, and Sister Long were tricking Wuying Qi when he knocked me out. Then, lots of things happened. I saw a violent black sun and a rising red star. After that…

"After that, the black sun fell apart, and its pieces gathered into a black river of magma which flooded into my brain and burnt up my soul!

"I sank into a place that felt like hell. In an abyss where a black fire raged, I saw how Wuying Qi changed from a Cultivator into an Immortal Cultivator. In the meantime, a persuading voice had been talking to me, trying to convince me that the true path of immortality was correct.

"I was almost convinced. I was about to be convinced. But at the most dangerous moment, I suddenly heard the most unpleasant singing of a man. His singing was so tormenting that I felt my life back in the City in the Sky was not as miserable as that."

Li Yao: "…Fine. Let's skip over the details. How do you feel right now?"

Li Jialing took a deep breath and extended his hand again.

Both Li Yao and Long Yangjun focused their eyes on his palm.

This time, no matter how Li Jialing concentrated, he could not unleash the purple fire anymore.

Yet, a stream of vague purple spiritual energy in the shape of a dragon was slowly flowing under the skin of his palm, like a fish that was swimming under the ice.

Suddenly, the purple spiritual energy glittered.

"Ah!"

As if his hand was burnt, Li Jialing cried and subconsciously retreated his hand.

When he opened his hand again, the purple dragon had been melted with his palm print, transforming it into a new pattern that was emitting a purple glow.

"That's weird…"

Li Jialing blinked his eyes and said in confusion, "I don't think my palm print was like this before. This is so majestic that I really want to grab something with my hand. Or rather, I have the urge to grab all the stars."

Recalling the unusual phenomenon that Li Jialing just caused and what Wuying Qi 3.0 told him before death, Li Yao did not know what to say.

What would come would come. It was impossible to avoid one's destiny. They had to embrace the challenge with courage!

"Brother Yao, what happened to me exactly?"

Li Jialing clenched his fists in confusion.

"I have the same question. What happened to you and Li Jialing when you passed out and struggled. Is Wuying Qi really dead?"

Long Yangjun was also puzzled.

"It's very hard to explain. Since our time is limited, let's just cut the long story short!"

Li Yao pondered for a moment and said, "Just now, when I, Jialing, and Wuying Qi were fighting, Wuying Qi detonated his soul and split into two different personalities. His righteous personality invaded my brain, and the dark and evil one invaded Jialing's, trying to corrupt us and turn us into his successors."

"Hold on a second—"

Long Yangjun raised her hand. "What did you just say? His righteous personality chose you as his successor and invaded your brain, and his dark and evil personality chose the young lion to inherit the sordid, shameless, and degenerate stuff? Are you sure you're not mistaken? Shouldn't it be the other way around?"

"…"

Li Yao glared at Long Yangjun and said angrily, "I'm certainly not mistaken, because his righteous personality was actually even eviler than his evil personality!

"In conclusion, Jialing and I have both swallowed part of Wuying Qi's personality and soul power. The good thing is that we've killed him and divided his legacy without paying any price. The world is back to peace again!"

Li Jialing thought for a moment and said, "What about the bad thing?"

"The bad thing is that, although we destroyed his soul, we might still be influenced by his personality without us realizing it until we sink into darkness and become another Wuying Qi, or even another Blood God."

Li Yao opened his hands and said, "Of course, there's another possibility that is even worse. Actually, we may have been swallowed by Wuying Qi or the Blood God, and they are just mimicking the way we talk to deceive other people. I might not be the real Li Yao, and you might not be the real Li Jialing. Or rather, maybe Li Yao and Li Jialing were just distractions, and the real Wuying Qi or Blood God had already escaped into Long Yangjun's body."

"Huh?"

Li Jialing was rather scared. "That's hard to believe. How are we going to confirm it?"

"Don't be scared. It's indeed hard to believe. I'm just putting it in perspective."

Li Yao smiled and said, "It's impossible to find the answer to the ultimate question, 'who am I?'"
