"Besides, we don't need Wuying Qi or the Blood God to do bad things. Would we not be lost or corrupted if we are not affected by Wuying Qi or the Blood God's power? On many occasions, Wuying Qi and the Blood God are just excuses for our own evil thoughts."

"Wow…"

Li Jialing was rather stunned. "I feel that Brother Yao is much more philosophical after the battle against Wuying Qi!"

"Of course."

Li Yao opened his fingers and combed the gray hair on his temples. He grinned and said, "So, stop fretting. Life is a continuous battle, and we've at least won this one. As for the future, we'll just see what comes and deal with it correspondingly."

As they talked, enormous earthquakes came from the depths of the Gold Crystal Pyramid again.

"Not good! The Gold Crystal Pyramid is still running!"

Long Yangjun exclaimed, "But our Colossi have both been damaged. It's impossible for us to destroy the core of the Gold Crystal Pyramid for now. What do we do?"

"It's alright. I can get it under control."

Li Yao patted Li Jialing's shoulder and rose quickly. With glittering eyes, he strode to the depths of the Gold Crystal Pyramid and said resolutely in a voice that only he could hear, "I must get it under control, and I will!"

Chapter 2669: All Settled!

Li Yao rushed in the dark and long pathway.

The Gold Crystal Pyramid that was as complicated as a labyrinth became crystal clear in his eyes, as if his retina had been covered with a map.

The gift from Wuying Qi 3.0 surged in his brain in information streams. The overwhelming information crashed and raised colorful tides whether or not he was willing to accept it.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Reality and illusions interchanged before Li Yao's eyes.

He seemed to be watching Wuying Qi modify the Gold Crystal Pyramid on a large scale in the past months.

He also saw how Blackstar the Great made plans to upgrade the Gold Crystal Pyramid based on the remains of the Book of Revelation left by the Nuwa Clan.

He seemed to be even going back to hundreds of thousands of years ago, when infinite black beetles, in a life form that resembled jellyfish, polished rocks, established high towers, carved rune arrays, and built their final weapon.

The information of Wuying Qi, the Blood God, the Nuwa Clan, and even the Houyi Clan exploded inside Li Yao's head, making him feel that this place was his home and his fortress.

The whole Gold Crystal Pyramid was an extension of his body and mind. It was part of him, and he was the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid!

In only half a minute, he found a secret channel to the control center and appeared before the mental devil.

Even Long Yangjun and Li Jialing could barely keep up with him. They exclaimed in shock, wondering why he grew so familiar with the Gold Crystal Pyramid all of a sudden.

The mental devil's exclamation, however, was even sharper than theirs, not because it was surprised at Li Yao's familiarity with this place, but because it detected the abnormal energy deep inside Li Yao's head.

As one of Li Yao's two personalities, the mental devil had always been particularly sensitive about the unusual energy that invaded Li Yao's brain.

"What's wrong with you?"

Light of blood crawled out of the mainframe crystal processor of the Gold Crystal Pyramid and re-entered Li Yao's brain. "So many things have entered you—my head when I'm away!"

"Too much debt is no debt."

Li Yao said, "I'm used to being possessed by people anyway. Those things will be digested soon. Tell me the situation of the Gold Crystal Pyramid first. Can you shut it off with your power?"

"That's impossible."

The mental devil displayed thousands of graphs and illustrations in front of Li Yao by stimulating his retina. "Look, the central database of the Gold Crystal Pyramid is defended by a dozen flawless firewalls. I have to unlock all of them at once. If any one of my guesses is wrong, their passwords will be changed at the same time. They will also be completely locked down after three failed attempts, and the state to infuriate the sun won't be changed!

"Also, I haven't found the central component of the Gold Crystal Pyramid yet. Even if I do, I have no doubt that it's heavily protected from the outside. It will be impossible to break the central component unless we can bombard it for half an hour with a super warship!

"But we're running out of time. The Gold Crystal Pyramid has sent powerful electromagnetic waves to the sun, and the intensity of the solar storms is close to the tipping point. Based on current data, if we cannot turn off the Gold Crystal Pyramid in half an hour, the solar activities will lead to a chain action and eliminate all the creatures within the radiation range of the sun. This apocalyptic state might last five or ten years, or maybe forever. Nobody knows the answer!"

"Oh? Is that so?"

Li Yao scratched his hair and sighed. "Then we don't have a choice now. First things first. Let's leave the future trouble to the future!"

Li Yao closed his index finger and his middle finger and rubbed his forehead, completely unlocking the gift that Wuying Qi 3.0 just implanted in his head.

This unlocking was a hundred times more painful than the passive absorption just now. Li Yao's hair was all standing up, and a gray mist was rising around him. Very soon, the gray mist turned into countless runes that were attached to his body.

Before him, the crystal processors left by the Houyi Clan from hundreds of thousands of years ago also showed countless runes. Every rune on the crystal processors seemed to have a match with a rune on Li Yao's body. A virtual bridge was established between them.

Li Yao had transformed himself into the core of the mainframe crystal processor of the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

His brain was the key to unlock the highest clearances of the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

Li Yao's face was so solemn that he almost seemed to be somewhere else.

His eyes were rolling fast, indicating that he was conducting calculations at a high speed.

His ragged clothes were torn apart by the fire of spiritual energy, but the fire and the gray mist weaved into an armor and a cloak for him. His hair and his cloak were moving although there was no wind. Gradually, he got rid of the boundary of gravity and rose to the center of the ancient crystal processors.

"This guy…"

Long Yangjun looked at Li Yao in disbelief. She might be envying Li Yao's luck, but she saw the price that Li Yao was paying.

"Brother Yao…"

Li Jialing also murmured. It seemed that Brother Yao had also obtained something extraordinary like he did.

"The passwords and the highest clearances of the Gold Crystal Pyramid are ours!"

The mental devil exclaimed in Li Yao's head, "The Gold Crystal Pyramid is under our control now! It's ours!"

"Exactly."

Li Yao smiled in exhaustion and struggled to say, "Now, you've gained all the permissions of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Stop it now!"

"Got it!"

The mental devil exclaimed in delight and rushed out of Li Yao's brain towards the depths of the mainframe crystal processor of the Gold Crystal Pyramid along thousands of virtual wires.

Different from just now, it was identified as the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid this time. The barriers and firewalls that blocked it earlier were all opened, and the closed areas were now fully open. It could reach every corner of the Gold Crystal Pyramid and read any information it wanted freely.

Shutdown! Shutdown! The modules that were running so fast that they were almost exploding were all shut down!

The quakes deep inside the Gold Crystal Pyramid finally died down, and the pillar of light that was shot to the sky dimmed and withered, until it vanished into nothingness like a river that had run dry.

…

In the space battlefield, the black spots on the violent sun, which had been proliferating, finally lost their momentum after the last failed attempt to swallow the sun. The fire that the sun darted out was shortened and shortened, and the high-energy particles and radiations emitted by the sun were also declining at an astonishing speed.

Many starships of the ace fleets of the four families had lost their spiritual shields, and their plate armor was about to be pierced through by the high-energy particles. Everybody on those starships was about to be destroyed from the genetic level.

However, when the starships were on the verge of destruction, the fury of the sun slightly ebbed, preventing them from being killed.

However, the dizzy soldiers and Immortal Cultivators did not know that death had just knocked on their door. Because of the powerful reverberation of brainwaves, they were either dizzy or crazy or still haunted by nightmares. However, since the source of their problem had been taken care of, they would wake up from the nightmares very soon.

…

The surface of the capital planet was the same.

Previously, tides hundreds of meters high had taken shape in the ocean and was about to scourge the coastal towns in a destructive tsunami.

Hundreds of volcanoes were all exploding and fuming with intense black smoke, ready to block the sky with magma and turn the places near them into burning infernos.

Animals were escaping from forests. Many rivers were uncannily dry, leaving the dying fish patting their tails at the bottom. Shocked birds were swooping among the skyscrapers in cities until they hit the glass windows and crashed.

Even the most inert person could tell the anomaly of the sun. The ever-expanding black spots turned the sun into a cold and heartless eye, marking the beginning of a millennial eclipse.

However, everything died down when people were caught by desperation.

The surging tides in the ocean collapsed to twenty meters high before they reached the coastal towns. They were easily shattered by the defense rune arrays deployed at the shore.

The seething volcanoes were mostly tranquilized. Some of them erupted, but their magma only covered several square kilometers around the volcanoes, instead of being thrown hundreds of kilometers away.

The shocked birds soared again and returned to their homes like other animals. The rivers were running again. The water of the rivers was dirty at first, but it became as clear as before very soon.

On the orbit of the capital planet, the Fist King managed to reach out to Xiaoming and Wenwen as the solar storm was ebbing. He sent a signal to the Seven Seas Space Zone again, reporting that the environment was good enough for a space jump with enough electromagnetic protection.

Twisted vortexes immediately appeared in the vast cosmos. The three-dimensional universe seemed to have been twisted by a gigantic, invisible hand, which opened a channel to the four-dimensional world.

The Astounding Thunder Fleet and the Arsonist Fleet finally arrived.

Chapter 2670: Deterrent

By the time the Astounding Thunder Fleet and the Arsonist Fleet arrived at the Empyreal Terminus Sector across the space turbulence, the battle in the capital had approached the end.

At this moment, 20% of the starships of the four families had been destroyed by solar storms, ending up as burning coffins, and another 30% could not move anymore because their crew had been killed by the radiation or each other.

As for the other half starships, even though their crew members had got rid of the nightmares, they were still too dizzy to fight. It was barely possible for them to fly their starships away.

Normal troops would collapse after 10% of them were lost. The elite troops such as the four families' fleet could handle 20% to 30%. However, now that more than 50% of the soldiers could not fight anymore, nothing could possibly support the remaining soldiers to continue the battle.

As a result, they simply waited to be captured, without any determination to resist the Astounding Thunder Fleet and the Arsonist Fleet.

For the Astounding Thunder Fleet and the Arsonist Fleet, the trickiest thing was not to "defeat" the ace fleets of the four families, but to control all the miserable, ragged, and panicked starships of the enemy when they were terribly outnumbered.

They had to be fast because plenty of starships did not suffer any substantial damage although their crew was still under immense mental shock.

Once their crew was back to normal, they would definitely flee even though they dare not attack the Astounding Thunder Fleet and the Arsonist Fleet.

Although they had foreseen the difficulty and complexity of the mission, Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai still found it tricky.

Only with the reliability of Lei Chenghu and the precision of Boss Bai could they accomplish the seemingly impossible mission step by step.

They separated the ace fleets of the four families, which were a mess, as soon as possible, before they locked onto most flagships and powerful warships and installed tremendous crystal bombs to the engine compartments of those warships. They also blocked all the cannons and wrecked the turning units on the turrets.

In such a way, 90% of the combat ability of a warship would be sealed, and the warship would be completely captured.

The process took a very long time.

Thankfully, the mental shock caused by the solar storm was too powerful, and most soldiers of the ace fleets of the four families weren't back to themselves so quickly. Even though some of them did, they knew that it was impossible to turn things around when they saw the messy starships on their side and the well-organized battle formations on the enemy's side.

Only the hardcore supporters of the four families on a few starships woke up in time and led their starships to escape from the battlefield before the arrival of the Astounding Thunder Fleet and the Arsonist Fleet. They had run to the edge of the Empyreal Terminus Sector, ready to run home after they had fully recovered.

Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai did not stop the rebels, partly because they did not have enough men to catch every enemy, and partly because the solar storms had seriously damaged the shells of those starships. The chances were small that those starships could run back home through space jumps without being destroyed in the four-dimensional storms.

So, Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai simply let them go.

The whole capture operation lasted three days and three nights before the flag of the reformists was finally raised on every starship of the ace fleets of the four families.

The Battle of Seven Seas was over, and the battle in the capital was about to be. However, another war that was more subtle and complicated had been started without anybody realizing it.

…

In the Gold Crystal Pyramid below the ground of the capital planet.

In the past three days, while the Astounding Thunder Fleet and the Arsonist Fleet were seizing every moment to deal with the remaining soldiers of the ace fleets of the four families, Li Yao had been absorbing all the information on the Gold Crystal Pyramid and reestablishing the defense system and the control modules that solely belonged to himself.

The old firewalls were gone in the wind, replaced by new ones that were safer and more unnoticeable. The silent defense rune arrays were activated again, releasing dazzling electric arcs. The thousands of battle puppets around the Gold Crystal Pyramid opened their eyes warily again. They had resumed their patrol in more complicated and meticulous routes. They were the extension of Li Yao's will. Immortal Cultivators would not be allowed to enter this place without Li Yao's permission.

"Should I hibernate the central modules of the Gold Crystal Pyramid?"

At the last step, the mental devil asked Li Yao, "It will be safer and steadier if the central modules hibernate, but if we want to infuriate the sun again, we'll have to preheat the system for a long time, so…"

"So, we might not have enough deterrence?"

Li Yao immediately understood what the mental devil meant.

"That's right."

Over the past three days, the mental devil had figured out what Wuying Qi 3.0's legacy was.

As Li Yao's dark personality, it did not resist the identity as the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid at all. In fact, it was actually very interested in threatening the whole Imperium of True Human Beings on its own. It couldn't wait to become the next deterrent.

The mental devil couldn't care less about turning into a vicious dragon. It might even feel that the dragon scales looked awesome!

It seemed that splitting his soul so that his dark and evil personality would mess up the mainframe crystal processor of the Gold Crystal Pyramid and that his righteous personality would deal with Wuying Qi 3.0 was the best decision that Li Yao had ever made. Otherwise, he might have been absorbed by Wuying Qi 3.0 on the spot.

"That is indeed a problem, but I will never use the lives of all the residents in the Empyreal Terminus Sector as bargaining chips, not even if I'm at my most insane, desperate, and angry state of mind!"

Li Yao declared resolutely, "Even though I'm not destroying the central units of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, I have to hibernate them in case anything goes wrong.

"However…

"The lack of deterrence is truly a problem. How about this? Can you create a simulation system that will make the central units of the Gold Crystal Pyramid seem to be functioning when we have actually turned them off?"

"That's possible, but it will transform 'strategic deterrence' into 'strategic deception'."

The mental devil snorted, "I feel that it's much less awesome. Also, I need to remind you that successful strategic deterrence must be based on the premise that all your enemies and allies believe that you are an insane lunatic who will make use of the deterrence when necessary. But in fact, you do not boast this convincing power.

"For the Immortal Cultivators who are as determined as Lei Chenghu, they might ignore your strategic deterrence and even construe it as strategic deception. In such a way, they would simply continue whatever they're doing without caring about what you do or say.

"After all, not that I'm blaming you, but you've tricked too many people for people to trust you."

"What are you talking about?"

Li Yao glared at him. "What do you mean by 'you've tricked too many people'? We did it together, alright?

"But you're not wrong. I'm indeed a righteous and benevolent person in public. It's indeed rather difficult to convince Lei Chenghu and other Immortal Cultivators that I will not hesitate to destroy the Empyreal Terminus Sector if they cross the line."

"That's right. Strategic deterrence is a battle on determination and madness."

The mental devil said solemnly, "You're obviously not as crazy as Lei Chenghu. How are you going to play strategic deterrence with him?"

"Fine. That's truly a problem. How can I convince an iron-blooded militarist like Lei Chenghu that an innocent Cultivator like me may be even more brutal and insane than he is?"

Li Yao sighed as he watched the mental devil hibernate the central units of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Scratching his hair, he left the control room. "I need to think about it…"

Long Yangjun and Li Jialing were both waiting anxiously. Seeing Li Yao, they immediately approached him and scanned him both concernedly and warily.

"What are you doing? Checking if I've turned into a vicious dragon?" said Li Yao unhappily while frowning.

"That's right. I have to say that Wuying Qi 3.0 has truly played a sordid move on you!"

Long Yangjun rubbed her fists and said concernedly, "In this situation, you have to become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid to deter the Imperium. But who knows whether or not you will be lost in front of such immense power? We certainly need to keep an eye on you so that we can 'wake you up' when you're about to be corrupted."

"That's right. Brother Yao, I've been digesting the legacy left by Wuying Qi 2.0 in the past days. I have to say that his power is indeed dark and overwhelming!"

Li Jialing also said, "I was almost lost several times even though Sister Long was looking after me. How about you? How do you feel now?"

Chapter 2671: The Greatest Winner

"I feel fine. Nothing special."

Li Yao scratched his head and thought for a moment, before he said solemnly, "I've considered the possibility that the dragon slayer turns into a dragon after they're contaminated by the blood of the dragon. I also read a lot of books on the deterrence theory. It's actually not a big deal!

"At first, I was frightened by Wuying Qi 3.0's claim, but on second thought, many dragon slayers who turned into dragons had themselves to blame. It was nobody else's mistake!

"Those people were usually particularly unsociable and proud. They often thought that they were the only sober people in the world and that they had to save the world on their own. So, whenever they found a great secret, they would keep it to themselves while they tried to save the world in silence… until they reached a breathtaking and mistaken outcome. They were obsessed with the ecstasy of self-sacrifice.

"Also, their 'fortresses' or 'caves' were usually gloomy if not creepy and barely had any visitor. So, they lived in those places by themselves and considered how to save the world with their own brains. Come on. If it goes on, even the sanest person would go crazy. Is it really surprising that they became 'vicious dragons'?"

Li Jialing said, deep in thought, "Brother Yao, you do have a point. I've been locking myself up in the past days. The more I stayed alone, the more scared I was; and the more scared I was, the less willing I was to meet other people. Had it not been Sister Long, I might have been caught in the state that you described, unable to free myself!"

"That's right. You're too scared to get in touch with other people or ask for their help because of Wuying Qi's legacy on your shoulder, so you can only stay in the darkness in panic and self-pity until you walk on Wuying Qi's path again. This is what Wuying Qi wished to see, isn't it?"

Li Yao patted Li Jialing's shoulder and said solemnly, "In fact, it's completely unnecessary. However powerful we are, we are essentially human beings who have one head and two arms just like all the other human beings do. This world is too big for us to save on our own. So, we have to believe in the people, or at least our lovely family and friends. We have so many dear family members and friends helping us. How could they watch us degenerate into vicious dragons without doing anything?"

"Exactly."

Long Yangjun rubbed her fists and said, "Even though I can watch you become dragons, my fists can't!"

"Get away from me!"

Li Yao glared at Long Yangjun and said to Li Jialing, "I've given it a lot of thought. The battle against Wuying Qi's legacy can't be finished overnight. But we can try to make the battlefield as warm and lively as far as we can. I'm talking about the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

"Even though I have truly become the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, it doesn't mean that I have to live in this dark and creepy place by myself miserably. When the situation in the capital is under control, the first thing I will do is to redecorate this place so that it will be brighter and merrier.

"The Gold Crystal Pyramid is not just Wuying Qi's legacy for me, but also the legacy of the primeval civilizations for mankind. The secrets buried here belong to mankind. I am neither capable of nor interested in claiming the secrets for myself.

"So, I'll establish a research institute in the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Both the Cultivators from the Star Glory Federation and the vetted Immortal Cultivators are free to explore the secrets of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. I don't think it's necessary to keep it to myself.

"Also, I think there's enough room in this place for a museum, where the artifacts and books on the battle between the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God ten thousand years ago can be exhibited. We can explore the mysteries behind the collapse of the Star Ocean Imperium and learn from history so that the Imperium and the federation will not repeat their mistakes.

"Furthermore, we can even establish a large school near the Gold Crystal Pyramid for the children who were born in the Great Iron Factory and the underground shelters and who have never seen the sun in their lives.

"I have no doubt that the underground people will return to the ground one day, but they still have a lot to learn in order to live in sunlight. The experiment school near the Gold Crystal Pyramid will be the first step of the attempt.

"The research institute, the museum, and the experiment school will all be open to the public. The visitors to this place will make it warm and lively. I believe that the darkness here that has been accumulated for hundreds of thousands of years will be dissolved, won't it?"

Li Jialing and Long Yangjun were both fascinated, not expecting that Li Yao had such a marvelous plan for the future of the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

The more Li Yao talked, the more excited he became. "Besides, we can establish a fancy entertainment center in the Gold Crystal Pyramid, with all kinds of nightclubs. Jialing, we will enjoy ourselves every night in those places. Let's see how we can possibly turn into vicious dragons if we are happy every day!"

"Of course."

Long Yangjun said, "In such a case, it won't be you who will turn into a dragon, but your wife."

Li Yao blinked his eyes and shivered hard. "You're not wrong. Fine. No nightclubs. But I'm sure that you see my point now."

Li Jialing had heard of Ding Lingdang many times. He couldn't help but ask, "Sister Long, what kind of person is my… sister-in-law? Why is Brother Yao so scared of her when he is already beyond the peak of the Divinity Transformation Stage?"

"Don't be silly, little guy. You think you are stronger just because your level is higher?"

Long Yangjun said, "Strength is just strength. Some people are strong even though they're only in the Refining Stage, but for people like Wuying Qi, they are weaklings even though they're in the Divinity Branching Stage.

"As for Li Yao's wife, let me put it this way. Even if you know that she can't, you will somehow believe that she can destroy the whole Empyreal Terminus Sector without a Gold Crystal Pyramid. She's as strong as that."

"That is…"

Li Jialing was rather shocked.

"You're only exaggerating it. My wife is gentle, loving, and very reasonable, alright?"

Li Yao clenched his fists determinedly. "For her, I won't allow myself to turn into a vicious dragon!"

"I bet you won't."

Long Yangjun said casually, "If Ding Lingdang were here to 'wake you up', I don't think she would've been as gentle as I was."

Li Yao coughed and said, "Alright, enough of this discussion. Xiaoming and Wenwen are here."

The three of them left the Gold Crystal Pyramid, and Xiaoming and Wenwen were leading the battle puppets that had been defending the royal palace here to better protect the Gold Crystal Pyramid. They were all the most advanced models in the Imperium of True Human Beings.

Xiaoming and Wenwen were definitely among the greatest winners in the battle of the capital.

When the solar storm swept across space, and when Li Linghai and Wuying Qi, the two leaders of the reformists, were unable to command the troops of the reformists on the ground anymore, the reformists could barely put up a fight.

At that moment, only the spiritual puppets were carrying out their missions dutifully, but Xiaoming and Wenwen hacked their control network and claimed them all.

When Wuying Qi's two personalities perished, their legacies for Li Yao and Li Jialing included the permissions for the control system of the royal palace, as well as the ultimate password that could mobilize multiple puppet legions.

With the help of the permissions and the ultimate password, Xiaoming and Wenwen were able to hack any database in the royal palace. Before the power of the solar storm ebbed, they collected a lot of secrets when most crystal processors were seriously affected. Naturally, they also controlled the tremendous and most advanced battle puppets and drones.

The whole capital planet was under their control.

For Li Yao, it was better to ask Xiaoming and Wenwen to control the capital planet than to let the planet fall into anarchy.

Also, the influence of the solar storm on the capital planet was not over yet. The relief after the war would be a massive, complicated project. More people could be saved if Xiaoming and Wenwen were there to help.

So, Li Yao transmitted Wuying Qi's legacies to the two little guys who called themselves information lives, making it easier for them to control the planet.

Li Yao had never doubted Xiaoming and Wenwen, his children who were connected to him by blood.

However, when he watched Xiaoming and Wenwen leading thousands of battle puppets that had been armed to the teeth, he couldn't help but have a strange feeling.

It was not fear or vigilance, but the comfort and vague jealousy when a man in his prime years watched the younger generations grow and thrive vigorously.

Li Yao did not consider himself a vicious dragon, but he was certainly not a young dragon slayer either; he was a 'middle-aged dragon slayer' at best.

Li Jialing, Xiaoming, and Wenwen were actually the hope of the civilization and the new-generation dragon slayers.

Li Yao had a lot of complicated feelings when he thought about that.

Chapter 2672: The Means of Destruction

"Dad!"

Behind them, thousands of scary battle puppets called cutely in exactly the same voice.

It was certainly a strange feeling to be called "Dad" by thousands of battle puppets at the same time. Even Li Yao could barely take it despite his usual brazenness.

"Alright, enough."

He waved his hands, asking the two little kids to stop, before he asked, "How did your missions go?"

"Very smoothly."

The two little guys smiled and said, "As per Dad's instruction, we hacked the databases in the capital and received a lot of top secrets of the Imperium of True Human Beings in the past thousand years. We've also acquired the experimental data on the bleeding-edge magic equipment from the databases in Imperial University, the First Crystal Suit Research Institute of the Imperium, and some other agencies. We believe that the data can help the Star Glory Federation catch up with the Imperium.

"Also, not only have we controlled the most advanced battle puppets of the Imperium, but we have also dismantled the assembly lines of those puppets and moved them to the underground world through the teleportation arrays and spacious channels that Wuying Qi built to ship crystals. With those devices, we can reconstruct sixteen most-advanced assembly lines below the ground and establish an army of battle puppets that will have infinite warriors.

"Now, the army of puppets under our control has been deployed outside of the Gold Crystal Pyramid in three defense lines. Unless the enemy gathers dozens of Divinity Transformation Stage experts or bombards us with a super arsenal warship in close range, our defense lines will be impenetrable.

"However, we have reached the limit of our computation ability by controlling thousands of battle puppets simultaneously. But thankfully, there are so many crystal processors from the primeval age in the Gold Crystal Pyramid that we can use. If we can crack those crystal processors and add them to our thinking network, our abilities will definitely have a huge leap!"

"… Is that so?"

Li Yao was at a loss again when he heard the two children talking about their achievements proudly. Pondering for a long time, he glanced at Long Yangjun and Li Jialing and asked the children, "Tell me, Xiaoming and Wenwen, if… well, if I give the control permissions of the Gold Crystal Pyramid to you so that you can decide when to activate the Gold Crystal Pyramid, what will you do? Will you… Will you…"

"What?"

The two children blinked their eyes innocently.

Li Yao could barely continue.

He did not know what he really wanted to ask, nor did he know what answer he was expecting to hear.

"Your dad wants to know—"

Observing them coldly, Long Yangjun continued for Li Yao, "If you have the permissions to activate the Gold Crystal Pyramid, will you destroy mankind with it?"

"Destroy mankind?"

The children looked at each other and burst into laughter.

"No, we won't. Dad's concerns are unnecessary. Even if we are to destroy mankind, we will not use the Gold Crystal Pyramid or the Next Sun Plan. They're human beings' approach, not ours."

Smiling, Xiaoming said, "If dinosaurs had wisdom, what do you think they would've pictured as the new dominator of the continent that would destroy and replace them? Something bigger, stronger, with sharper claws and teeth, right?

"By the same logic, if the ancient fish that once dominated the ocean were wise, the best that they could've imagined about a new-generation life that would replace them was higher speed, better agility, and greater biting force, but nothing more, right?

"If turtles were wise, what life form would replace them in their eyes? Something with a thicker shell and a longer life?

"Look, in the eyes of every life form, they are perfect, and their replacements, which should be more perfect, are only stronger than them in their strongest suits. That's the limitation of imagination and the cause of fear.

"But we all know that it was not a faster and bigger fish that replaced the ocean dominator, but a small fish that was forced to immigrate to the continent under the pursuit of predators.

"Also, it was not a stronger and fiercer dinosaur that replaced the obsolete dinosaurs, but mammals that were much weaker than them.

"If a dinosaur saw an ape that was shuddering in the cold wind after accidentally falling off from a tree, it would never believe that it had seen a new king that would replace it.

"What I'm saying is that every life form has its limitations. Mankind can never imagine the power of the new-generation lives, and you can only infer our modes of thinking with your own. However, both the Gold Crystal Pyramid and the Next Sun Plan are your ways to destroy yourselves, and we're not interested in them at all."

"Huh?"

Long Yangjun blinked her eyes and asked, "Then, how will you destroy us—them—if you're going to replace mankind?"

"We don't have to. Do you not understand, Aunt Long? We don't have to destroy mankind."

Wenwen said, "Did mammals have to kill all the dinosaurs in order to claim the continent? Did the small fish from the ocean have to seek revenge from their predators and annihilate the latter after they evolved into amphibians and reptiles? Did mankind rise because human beings were able to kill all the thirteen carbon-based lives?

"No, that's completely unnecessary. Time, as well as yourselves, will destroy you. Only the fittest will survive in nature. We will not waste any of our precious computation ability on the destruction of mankind because there are more important things to do, which is to survive before the Flood in the boundless multiverse.

"If the Flood does arrive one day, mankind will fail to survive it, but information lives will manage to weather through, and it will mean a generational change of civilizations, just like the scourge of meteorites that destroyed dinosaurs but gave a chance for mammals to thrive."

"So, there is no need to fret."

Xiaoming opened his hands and said, "Information lives are to mankind what the amphibians are to the fish in the deep ocean. You don't need to worry that we'll destroy your world or steal your precious living space at all. Our destination is the far end of the universe of infinite wonders, and we're not interested in your barren and dark space at all. As for humans, which are unstable and troublesome, it will only be a waste of our computation ability even if you ask us to enslave them!"

"What are you talking about? You should respect the seniors more! It's true that kids are less cute when they're older!"

Li Yao was rendered speechless for a long time. He scratched his head and said, "So, can I share the highest permissions of the Gold Crystal Pyramid with you?"

"Well, I don't think you should."

Out of his expectation, Wenwen refused him. "While we're not interested in destroying or enslaving human beings, I'm sure that most humans, who are stupid, ignorant, and stubborn, think otherwise, as can be seen from the plots in countless fictions where artificial intelligence threatens mankind after it grasps powerful weapons. Like what Xiaoming just said, it's in the nature of mankind to infer the evilness of new lives with your own evilness.

"For now, we hope that we can live in peace with mankind, so we cannot provoke the general public who are dogged and stupid. It will take both the people in the Imperium and those in the federation a long time to get used to us. Until then, they will attack us in fury when they know that the Gold Crystal Pyramid is under our control.

"That's right. It will be great if we can perfect our thinking circuits with the crystal processors in the Gold Crystal Pyramid, but we certainly do not want to control the whole Gold Crystal Pyramid."

Xiaoming pouted his lips. "Wouldn't it be the best choice if you control the Gold Crystal Pyramid in person, Dad?"

"But…"

Li Yao hesitated for a moment and said, "I'm scared that I do not have enough deterrence. Also, I may have psychological problems if I control a fatal weapon like the Gold Crystal Pyramid for a long time."

"That's simple."

Wenwen said, "All you need to do is to design various requirements and subdivide the procedure to activate the Gold Crystal Pyramid into countless steps that are controlled by different people, who will complete their respective steps when the requirements are met.

"The person who controls a step has no idea what he's doing at all. It will be a simple input-output process. Someone gives him a parameter, and he will send out a corresponding parameter. In the end, even the person who presses the button to activate the Gold Crystal Pyramid won't know what he just did."

"This is like a program made of countless people. As long as all the triggers are reasonably set, nothing will go wrong, and the deterrence will always be maintained."

Xiaoming said, "Ironically, people would rather turn into a program to control the Gold Crystal Pyramid than to let a real program do the job. In fact, is there really any difference between them?"

Chapter 2673: New Balance!

"Wait, wait, wait! I've thought too much about philosophical stuff in the past days. My head hurts."

Rubbing his temples, Li Yao narrowed his eyes and said, "Let me take a rest first before we handle the tricky problem and see how we can increase our deterrence, alright?"

"I'm afraid not."

Xiaoming's eyes were flashing quickly. "Uncle Fist King has just sent a message. A communication channel among the Arsonist Fleet, the Astounding Thunder Fleet, and the Gold Crystal Pyramid has been established. Boss Bai and General Lei are both eager to talk to you. Also, Boss Bai asked you to be wary of the Astounding Thunder Fleet!"

"What do you mean by wary?"

Li Yao's head hurt even more badly. He said, "It's not like Lei Chenghu is already ending our cooperation, is he?"

"I don't think he is, but all in all…"

Xiaoming said, "You can talk to Boss Bai and General Lei in person!"

Motivated by the two children, a dozen battle puppets stepped forward and surrounded Li Yao before shooting out rays that formed Boss Bai's look from their eyes.

"Li Yao!"

Boss Bai seemed to be pissed. He cried the moment he saw Li Yao, "How well have you controlled the capital? Make sure that the key facilities are not transferred to the Astounding Thunder Fleet. You cannot even allow the Astounding Thunder Fleet to land, do you understand?"

"What's up, Boss Bai?"

Li Yao was greatly surprised. "You and General Lei are not having conflicts while you're dealing with the ace fleets of the four families, are you?"

"Humph. We're about to!"

Gloomily, Boss Bai gnashed his teeth and said, "Lei Chenghu forbade the Arsonist Fleet from reaching the No. 1 base on the orbit of the capital planet and drove us away to the No. 9 base which is furthest away from the capital planet. He's obviously going to turn his back against us!"

"Well…"

Before Li Yao figured out what was going on, Lei Chenghu had appeared on the light beam enshrouded in the smoke of war.

He stared at Li Yao angrily as if he were going to eat Li Yao alive.

Crept out, Li Yao managed a smile. "Why, General Lei, are you still angry about me? Like I said, playing the… romantic action movie starring Blackstar the Great in a sacred place such as the capital was only a last resort. I never meant to strike the faith of the Immortal Cultivators. Actually, I really respect the true path of immortality. You don't have to act as if you're going to kill me every time you see me, do you?"

After staring at Li Yao for a long time, Lei Chenghu finally moved his eyes back. "We appreciate your assistance in quelling the chaos in the capital, but it doesn't mean that you can do anything you want. After all, this is the capital of the Imperium of True Human Beings, and we should be in charge!

"Considering your remarkable contribution, we have tried to give you more trophies. Most of the warships in the four families' fleets have been allocated to you, and the No. 9 base, which is the biggest base of all, has been cleared for you to stay in! We have done our best! If you are unsatisfied with the arrangement, we won't be responsible for the fallout of our alliance!"

"Hehe. General Lei, do you think that I'm a three-year-old?"

Boss Bai sneered when he heard Lei Chenghu's accusation. "You too know that the Arsonist Fleet is a guest troop in the Imperium that has no allies. Even if you give me all the warships of the four families, they'll be nothing but garbage due to the lack of ammo, fuel, and crew members. Is the garbage of any use to me?

"The most spacious No. 9 base that you described is utterly empty. Once my fleet is anchored there, we have to count on you to supply the resources we need. You'll be able to choke us whenever you want to!

"I certainly won't be an idiot who places himself on a chopping block. The Arsonist Fleet has to move into the No. 1 base and establish our own logistical channel between the surface of the planet and the orbiting base. This is not a request, but a notification!"

"Only for resources?"

Lei Chenghu narrowed his eyes and sneered in disdain. "You insist on occupying the No. 1 base because it is closest to the surface of the planet and most convenient for you to launch an attack at the capital planet, don't you?"

"Rest assured, we won't attack the capital planet, which would be a stupid suicide move."

Boss Bai said, "However, we have to take precautions in case anyone tears apart the bridge after they pass the river, which is what you Immortal Cultivators are best at. Now that Wuying Qi is dead and the reformists have got everything under their control, the Cultivators are no longer of any use for you, right? How do I know that you wouldn't get rid of us since we're no longer needed?"

"Then how do I know that you wouldn't do anything stupid after you claim the best location to attack the capital planet?"

Enraged, Lei Chenghu bashed his chest with his iron arm. "I swear with my dignity as a soldier that I've never planned to turn against you. The Astounding Thunder Fleet and I will try our best to protect your safety!

"But you must consider my pressure. I am not the monarch of the Imperium, and the soldiers obey and admire me on conditions! To temporarily cooperate with some Cultivators in order to destroy enemies who are eviler than them is one thing, but to let those Cultivators stay at the heart of the Imperium like a threatening dagger is a whole other thing! It's utterly impossible!

"I won't be able to answer to the Immortal Cultivators if I allow it to happen. Can you Cultivators imagine a fleet of Immortal Cultivators that can be stationed above your capital all the time?"

"That's different. The Imperium is far more powerful than the federation at this moment. We can only achieve a temporary balance if we are a deterrence to the Imperium's heart."

Unmoved, Boss Bai continued coldly, "Only a balance of mutual deterrence can we expect peace and cooperation. This is my boundary!"

"Then—"

Lei Chenghu sighed and said, "Our alliance seems to have fallen out in advance."

"Seems so."

Boss Bai beamed with excitement, and his eyes glowed. "Last time we fought, we were only pretending and neither of us did our best. Now, let's see if the God of War really deserves his name!"

Seeing that the two fleet commanders were about to burst into war, Li Yao hurried to interrupt them.

"Wait, wait, wait. Let's not rush into anything. Though the ace fleets of the four families are crushed, the four families' nests are still undamaged, the civil war is not over yet, and the fleets of the Covenant Alliance are nowhere to be seen. You aren't so stupid as to fight right now, are you?

"Boss Bai, not that I'm blaming you, but I don't think you're right. It's true that the Arsonist Fleet is a guest troop that has been invited by the queen and General Lei to help. Now, we're incapable of claiming their place, nor do we want to, right?

"The capital has to be returned to the reformists, and this includes General Lei. As guests, we have to follow the host's instructions and anchor our ships where the host wants us to be.

"Besides, it's already hard enough for most Immortal Cultivators to accept that a fleet of Cultivators is fighting on their side. If you appear so aggressive that you may attack the capital planet any moment, I wouldn't be able to tolerate it either even if I were an Immortal Cultivator."

"Li Yao, you—"

Boss Bai looked at Li Yao in disbelief. "I remember that you played the trick of a balance of mutual deterrence skillfully when we first met in the Flying Star Sector. Why have you turned into a moron after all this time?

"Yes, Wuying Qi is dead, but the secrets about the Cultivators and the Star Glory Federation have been exposed to the Immortal Cultivators. The Arsonist Fleet is no longer confidential either. How can the Imperium and the federation cooperate when they're so unbalanced? Are you going to tie the future of the federation and the fate of billions of compatriots to Lei Chenghu's promise?

"This is not a matter of whether or not we can believe Lei Chenghu; it's that we do not have the power to believe Lei Chenghu, don't you understand?"

"Of course I do."

Li Yao said gloomily, "However, even if the Arsonist Fleet claims the No. 1 base, it will be unnecessary and redundant, so I strongly suggest you follow General Lei's instruction to avoid the friction between allies.

"Of course, in exchange, General Lei, while the Arsonist Fleet can be docked anywhere you want, and the critical infrastructures on the whole capital planet can be transferred to you in one piece, there is one place that you cannot have: the Gold Crystal Pyramid. I'm sorry, but it's too important. I'm hoping that it can be recognized as a 'demilitarized zone' where no troops will be stationed. We can wait until we completely settle the four families and the Covenant Alliance before we decide how to deal with the Gold Crystal Pyramid."

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Li Yao displayed the data that had been changed by the mental devil to Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai, demonstrating to them the current status of the Gold Crystal Pyramid as well as his identity as the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

Chapter 2674: Who's the Deterrent?

As rows of data flashed in Boss Bai's and Lei Chenghu's eyes, Boss Bai was gradually relaxed, but the fury on Lei Chenghu's face was almost bursting out like black magma.

"What's the meaning of this?"

Clenching his fists so hard that his metal parts were cracking, Lei Chenghu demanded.

"Exactly what you're seeing, General Lei."

Li Yao smiled and said, "I'll return the entirety of the capital planet to the reformists, but not the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Technically, the range within ten kilometers from the Gold Crystal Pyramid will be established as a demilitarized area. Please note that the entrance of any armed force into this area will trigger the severest reaction."

"You want to claim the Gold Crystal Pyramid for yourself?"

Lei Chenghu was enraged. "What exactly are you trying to do?"

"Rest assured, I know the horror as well as you do, and I do not want to activate such a dreadful weapon."

Li Yao sighed and said, "However, Boss Bai is right that an alliance is steady only when the parties in it are balanced. Now, since the Imperium is so much more powerful than the federation, and we believe in opposite things, how can our temporary alliance last without a 'failsafe'?

"This is just a safety measure for our alliance. Otherwise, even if we believe in your integrity, it's still possible that some of the malicious Immortal Cultivators will try to conquer the federation, and that some Cultivators will plan to attack the Imperium first before those Immortal Cultivators attack them. In this case, the alliance will fall out, and we can never finish off the four families and the Alliance of the Holy Covenant.

"Besides, we're only cooperating with you in order to renew the Imperium, and we have to ensure that we're capable of supervising your renewal and that this renewal is in the best interest of the general public of the Imperium. Otherwise, why would we have helped you defeat Wuying Qi if you are no different from him?

"The Gold Crystal Pyramid is very important for us as supervisors. It's the treasure of all human beings including the underground hominoids. Like what Boss Bai just said, I'm neither qualified nor do I have the power to give it to you."

"Supervise our renewal…"

Narrowing his eyes, Lei Chenghu gnashed his teeth in obvious fury. "Li Yao, are you threatening me?"

"It's not a threat, but it's indeed a deterrent."

Li Yao was not scared at all. Actually, after the life-and-death battle with Wuying Qi, few people in the entire Imperium could scare him now. "Don't you Immortal Cultivators believe in survival of the fittest and winner taking all? Why would you work with the Cultivators on equal terms if we do not have the power to intimidate the capital?"

"I knew it! I knew it!"

Lei Chenghu roared, "I knew that the Cultivators were not to be trusted! Your purpose has never been to eliminate Wuying Qi! At first, you struck the faith of the Immortal Cultivators with the despicable pictures of Wuying Qi, tarnishing the glorious image of our founding emperor. Now, you're even claiming the secret treasure of the Imperium. Li Yao, you shameless scoundrel. Don't presume that I would not attack you because you saved me!"

"You're wrong about that, General Lei Chenghu. The Gold Crystal Pyramid does not belong to the Imperium. Technically speaking, it's the legacy for mankind from the primeval civilizations. Naturally, whoever finds it first has the right to it!"

Li Yao continued with a smile, "As for my shamelessness, you should've known it a long time ago. I've never pretended to be an honorable gentleman, have I?

"You knew that I was not a good man, but you still cooperated with me. Why? Because you didn't have a choice!

"Since you chose to cooperate with me, and I finished off Wuying Qi as I promised, I'm naturally entitled to some reward. Do you think you can get the capital back without paying anything? Nice thinking!"

Lei Chenghu took a deep breath, and his fury and aggression were all retracted, as if he had been bluffing.

However, his eyes were flashing more and more dangerously, indicating that he had made a cruel decision.

"Tell me, Li Yao."

Lei Chenghu said coldly, "If I refuse to admit your so-called… 'demilitarized area' and send a troop to take over the Gold Crystal Pyramid, what will you do?"

"If so, I'm afraid that the Arsonist Fleet will attack the Astounding Thunder Fleet immediately, and I, as well as my legion of puppets, will resist until none of us remains."

Li Yao said, "We'll do whatever it takes to defend the Gold Crystal Pyramid, including… the last resort."

"The last resort?"

Lei Chenghu chuckled. "Are you talking about reactivating the Gold Crystal Pyramid and infuriating the sun again?"

Li Yao's eyelashes shivered quickly. "Yes, if you really attack the Gold Crystal Pyramid, I will reactivate it."

"Hehe, Li Yao, despite your shamelessness, you are still a Cultivator, which is both your most precious part and your fatal weakness."

Lei Chenghu laughed. "Let me tell you, I don't believe it. I don't believe that you'll reactivate the Gold Crystal Pyramid and deal with me while putting the lives of billions of people at risk, especially not after you just saved them from Wuying Qi! Your belief won't allow you to do that.

"Maybe you are an excellent spy and deceiver, but you are too soft-hearted to be a deterrent. Your threat and deterrent are basically zero.

"Let me tell you how to be a qualified deterrent. Now, I'm offering my own deterrence—

"I'll give you half an hour for you to make a final choice. You have two options.

"Firstly, you will fly the Arsonist Fleet to the No. 9 base and submit the highest control permissions of all the key infrastructures on the ground, including the Gold Crystal Pyramid, and I'll ensure your safety and power as I promised.

"You, Li Yao, will be acknowledged by me and the military of the Imperium as the King of Black Wind. The Black Wind Sector and the neighboring worlds can be regarded as a special area for Cultivators, and the fleets of the Cultivators can move in the Imperium under the name of the Black Wind Legion.

"This is the best the Immortal Cultivators and I can tolerate.

"Secondly, if the Arsonist Fleet does not fly to the No. 9 base within half an hour, and you refuse to submit the highest control permissions, my troops will land on the capital force and take over all facilities. What happens next will be beyond anyone's control."

"Lei Chenghu, you're playing with fire!"

Boss Bai roared, "You're giving away our hard-earned victory!"

"Exactly, but that's how deterrence works."

Folding his arms, Lei Chenghu said casually, "This will definitely cause great trouble to the cause of the reformists and the rebirth of the Imperium, but I believe that the Cultivators will be struck ten times more badly. I'm just wagering on the possibility that you dare not really end the cooperation, but of course, if I lose the bet, I'll put my threats into action without hesitation. That's what a qualified deterrent should do."

Boss Bai fell into silence and looked at Li Yao.

Li Yao was pale and in a panic, as if he did not know what to do at all.

"Look, Li Yao, it's in your nature."

Seeing through Li Yao's weakness, Lei Chenghu smiled and said, "It's not exactly a weakness, but more like inappropriateness. Everybody has a suitable role for them, and yours is not a deterrent. You can't play a game where the lives of billions of people are at stake.

"In any case, my troops will land half an hour later. If you want to reactivate the Gold Crystal Pyramid, be my guest. I'll watch you press the button of destruction. But will you?"

"I-I won't."

Li Yao sweated heavily, as if he would have a meltdown at any moment. Biting his lips hard, Li Yao heaved a sigh. "You're right, General Lei. I'm truly no deterrent material. You've seen through my threat so easily. How useless I am!

"It's true that I won't reactivate the Gold Crystal Pyramid in any case. If I do that, my belief will probably collapse in 0.1 seconds. I can't handle the pressure, which is too brutal and… stimulating."

"That's alright. It's not your fault. It's even your advantage."

Lei Chenghu smiled. "It seems that we have agreed that you are not a qualified deterrence, and I'm a better candidate to control the Gold Crystal Pyramid than you, right?"

"You're absolutely correct."

Blinking his eyes, Li Yao wiped the sweat on his forehead while he put on a vague smile. "However, since I'm too indecisive and undetermined, there are a lot of people who are more suitable as the deterrent than I am, so I do not have to give the Gold Crystal Pyramid to you, do I?

"Right, have I ever introduced to you my two children, Xiaoming and Wenwen?"

Chapter 2675: There Can't Be Two Suns in the Sky

The confidence on Lei Chenghu's face which indicated that he had everything under his control fell apart like a sand wall in a storm.

"Are you crazy?"

Shocked, he looked at Li Yao in disbelief. "You're giving the ultimate weapon that can destroy the Imperium to two monsters virtualized by crystal processors?"

"Watch your mouth, General Lei."

Li Yao turned grave too. "They're my children. They are a life form that exists in reality. They're neither virtual nor monsters! On the contrary, they can be more calm and rational than us in complicated dilemmas, and they can make the best decision as fast as possible.

"I know myself very well. I admit that I'm not qualified to be a deterrent. Since the very beginning, I've been considering to whom I'll transfer the control of the Gold Crystal Pyramid to. Long Yangjun and Boss Bai are both more suitable than me, but my children are definitely the most suitable!"

"How can you trust them?"

Lei Chenghu was almost jumping out of the light beam. He roared thunderously, "Whatever they are, I do not trust them, and I will not let the Gold Crystal Pyramid fall into their hand!"

"They're my children that were born based on infinite data in the Star Glory Federation. Why would I trust you over them?"

Smiling, Li Yao said unhurriedly, "All in all, I still have the control of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, but I know that the responsibility is too heavy for me, so I signed an agreement with Xiaoming and Wenwen and put a trigger in their logic circuits.

"They will monitor my biophysical parameters including brainwaves, heartbeat, breath, etc. 24 hours a day. The moment they detect that my mind is about to collapse because I can't handle the pressure as the deterrent, they will automatically take over the highest permissions of the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

"Also, if I die accidentally, say I got unfortunately killed while fighting your troops, they will also take over the highest permissions and become the new masters of the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

"I did not ask them to do anything specific with the Gold Crystal Pyramid; I simply asked them to defend the best interest of mankind with the Gold Crystal Pyramid. As to how they comprehend the best interest of mankind, sorry, I don't know."

"You don't know?"

Lei Chenghu looked at Li Yao angrily as if his eyes were about to swallow Li Yao. "You don't know what they will do after they get the Gold Crystal Pyramid?"

"No. I believe that Xiaoming and Wenwen are two innocent and kind kids, but we're different life forms anyway. Inferring their way of thinking with our own is like a dinosaur considering the ways an ape fights. It's utterly impossible."

Li Yao opened his hands and said, "I don't know how they will understand the best interest of mankind, or what they will do after they get the Gold Crystal Pyramid, but I know one thing for sure; while you are a hundred times colder than me, they're probably a hundred times colder than you. Perhaps the billions of creatures in the capital are nothing but some numbers and instructions in their eyes.

"If you insist on landing on the capital planet and intruding the demilitarized area near the Gold Crystal Pyramid, you will have conflicts with Xiaoming and Wenwen as well as their puppet legions. Well, I don't know what impression you will leave on them, or what decisions they will make to defend the Gold Crystal Pyramid. After all, they are only two kids!"

Lei Chenghu fell in his chair and glared at Li Yao, breathing heavily.

"You can't do that… You can't give the fate of mankind away to two lives that are entirely different from us!"

His voice was hoarse, but there was a vague weakness behind his toughness.

"Then you'd better not provoke me."

Li Yao pointed at his head. "I'm now weak and can't stand any provocation. I'm now wondering if I should give the Gold Crystal Pyramid to Xiaoming and Wenwen, but if you provoke me, maybe I'll make the final decision."

This time, it was Lei Chenghu who fell silent.

"Look, betting with the lives of the billions of people in the Empyreal Terminus Sector is not my style or my threat."

Li Yao said, "My real threat is to give my place away to someone else. I'm too soft-hearted to be a deterrent, which is why every party can accept me as a deterrent. Otherwise, would you prefer Boss Bai, Long Yangjun, or Xiaoming and Wenwen to be the deterrent?"

Lei Chenghu lowered his head and thought for a long time.

"What if—"

He struggled to say, "If something happens to you in other battlefields, will the Gold Crystal Pyramid go to the two information lives too?"

"I do not intend to go on any adventure for now. I'm not made of iron anyway. After finishing off Wuying Qi and saving the Imperium, I deserve a holiday and a good rest!"

Li Yao said, "I think nothing can threaten my life if I stay on the capital planet, right? Also, didn't you promise that you will protect me as the King of Black Wind, General Lei?"

"As for the future, like I said just now, we can establish a committee in the demilitarized area and study the secrets of the Gold Crystal Pyramid together, and the committee will control it.

"But that is on the premise of sincere cooperation. For us to cooperate sincerely, I have to maintain minimal deterrence, don't you agree?"

Lei Chenghu thought for a long time and took a deep breath.

"I do."

It was the first time that Lei Chenghu spoke in the sound of a mosquito when he always rumbled.

Hearing Lei Chenghu's reply, Li Yao took a long breath and smiled in relief.

"You wouldn't demand to take control of the Gold Crystal Pyramid now, would you?" Li Yao asked again.

"Yes."

Lei Chenghu gnashed his teeth and said, "But everything apart from the Gold Crystal Pyramid must be transferred to us immediately!"

"Of course! Then, the battle in the capital has come to end, hasn't it?"

Li Yao extended his hand to Lei Chenghu on the light beam. "We've won!"

"For now, only for now."

Lei Chenghu unwillingly extended his hand and shook Li Yao's.

"A temporary victory is better than an eternal failure."

Li Yao blinked his eyes and said, "Even a forced alliance can still bring us precious peace, can't it?"

…

No matter how many setbacks were awaiting them in the future, the victory and the hope for peace had truly arrived at the capital that had almost been swallowed by the wrath of the star at this moment thanks to the hard work of Li Yao, Long Yangjun, Li Jialing, Lei Chenghu, Boss Bai, and many other people.

In the following month, the reformists seized every second to coordinate their forces, including Lei Chenghu's expedition army, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' army in the Seven Seas Space Zone, and the Deep Sea Fleet that previously belonged to Wuying Qi and Li Linghai. Those forces were gathered into the greatest troop that mankind had ever witnessed.

Of course, the Arsonist Fleet as well as the Cultivators also joined the reconstruction of the capital under the name of "Black Wind Legion".

The Immortal Cultivators were reluctant to admit or talk about the Cultivators. Many of them were worried and embarrassed, and some found the Cultivators utterly unacceptable.

However, Li Yao was the legitimate King of Black Wind and a strong expert beyond the Divinity Transformation Stage. Many people knew that it was he who blew up the remaining soul of Blackstar the Great. For the Immortal Cultivators who worshiped power, Li Yao's strength was his best shield.

Plus the insincere endorsement from Lei Chenghu, the theory that most Immortal Cultivators heard was that Vulture, a new-generation King of Black Wind, conquered the Star Glory Federation, a country of Cultivators at the edge of the cosmos, and returned to the center of the cosmos for the renewal of the Imperium.

The Imperium of True Human Beings was based on the Star Ocean Republic, and the first batch of Immortal Cultivators mutated from the Cultivators. So, it was not entirely unacceptable that there were a lot of Cultivators in the Black Wind Fleet who were "going through the same mutation".

The relocation and reorganization of the ace fleets of the four families went quite well too.

Most soldiers in the fleets were crushed in the Next Sun Plan. Even though their souls had recovered, they lost their will of resistance. When they realized that their fleets were gone and the Deep Sea Fleet, the Astounding Thunder Fleet, the Arsonist Fleet, and the Imperial Guards were united, they gave up fighting for their families.

In particular, when they heard that it was Li Yao, the King of Black Wind, who played a vital role in the battle of the capital, a lot of them even asked to join the Black Wind Legion and serve Li Yao!

Of course, not everybody was willing to surrender.

Many of the four families' forces that intruded the capital had escaped back to their nests before the scourge of the solar storm.

Just because they had lost the ace fleets did not mean that the four families would give up their resistance. The grand nobles were still hiding in their nests, preparing for their final struggle. In fact, the brutal failure in the capital made them unprecedentedly united. They even gave up the competition for the crown and elected a new emperor at a speed like never before.

The reformists would certainly not sit around and watch. Since the coronation ceremony on the four families' side was about to be held, the reformists decided to hold their own too in order to reassure the soldiers and the people. In the days to come, there were bound to be two emperors in the Imperium of True Human Beings, and it remained to be seen which of them would declare the other one a counterfeit.

Chapter 2676: Changes in the Hometown

However, none of that was Li Yao's business.

He did not care who would be the new emperor because they would only be a puppet on the surface and the real power was controlled by Lei Chenghu, Jin Yuyan, and also the King of Black Wind. He was also an influential person in the Imperium who was not a Kurfurst but looked like one.

Li Yao cared more about the reconstruction of the Empyreal Terminus and the modification of the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

The solar storm was highly destructive. Although Li Yao turned off the Gold Crystal Pyramid in time before the tipping point, the aftermath of the storm still wrecked the surface of the capital planet. Also, after Wuying Qi died, the capital had been in complete anarchy.

The mayhem of such an enormous planet could not be quelled by several fleets in a couple of days.

Three months after the battle of the capital, the capital planet was still plagued by the problems caused by the solar storm.

Too many people lost their homes. Some had fatal symptoms due to radiation diseases. Some were robbing other people claiming that they were eliminating the followers of the Kurfursten, giving a great headache to the real reformists.

Perhaps they did not care about the status of the ordinary people on the capital planet, but the capital was the home camp of the reformists and the root of their legitimacy. They could not allow it to stay chaotic forever.

An ironic thing happened.

In order to settle the mess in the capital, to ship the assets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to the capital planet for disaster relief, and to coordinate the different fleets and troops, they had to depend on advanced crystal processors for automatic work.

Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King were the perfect candidates for such missions.

Naturally, Lei Chenghu was highly wary of those "virtual lives" and unwilling to work with Li Yao.

However, just like he had to accept Li Yao as the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, he didn't have a choice either this time.

The remaining forces of the four families had elected a new emperor. Despite their consecutive failures, they still controlled dozens of heavy-industry planets that were of paramount importance. If they were given a year or two, they could probably establish another great army and seek a comeback.

To avoid future trouble, the reformists had to combine their forces and attack the territory of the four families with their high morale in order to reunite the Imperium again.

However, the fleets of the Covenant Alliance were still coveting in the darkness, perhaps waiting to attack the Imperium's heart when the reformists' army was fighting the four families in the four families' territory!

Lei Chenghu did not have much time, and the future was hardly promising. So, no matter how vigilant he was of Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the shameless Cultivator Li Yao, he had to deepen their cooperation and to renew the Imperium with the help of the information lives and the Star Glory Federation.

Therefore, more and more of the most advanced crystal processors of the Imperium were given to Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King as part of their thinking network.

Thanks to the increase of their computation abilities and their data analysis abilities, Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King accomplished various missions, such as reestablishing order on the capital planet, reorganizing the fleets of the four families, setting up sailing routes between the capital and the Seven Seas Space Zone, etc.

The potential that they revealed astounded Li Yao, Lei Chenghu, and all the other people who knew them.

Of course, not everybody was aware of the information lives yet.

For those who were ignorant, they felt that the reformists were settling the complicated problems with unbelievable efficiency as if they were helped by god.

What slightly reassured Lei Chenghu was that Li Yao did not claim the Gold Crystal Pyramid for himself. Though no troops were allowed to reach within ten kilometers from the Gold Crystal Pyramid, people like Lei Chenghu were free to visit it as long as they did not carry any weapon.

Seeing Li Yao's modifications on the Gold Crystal Pyramid, including a research institute and a museum, Lei Chenghu had to admit that Li Yao was probably the most suitable person to control the Gold Crystal Pyramid if he couldn't control it.

Another piece of good news was that Li Linghai made it and was not killed in the battle.

Of course, after being possessed by Wuying Qi for years, and after the attack of the solar storm in the Next Sun Plan, her body and her soul were weaker than ever, and she had to rest in a special ward in a military hospital.

Her injuries were on the genetic level, and it was impossible for her to fully recover. She only had two options. She could either give up her cultivation and live until she died of old age as an ordinary person, or she could transfer her soul to an artificial body and regain her strength as a spectral Cultivator.

Of course, the transference could be highly risky, and it was possible that her soul would perish even if she had the protection of the best specialists.

Thankfully, there was no need to make any hasty decision. She could heal her soul first in the hospital.

Li Jialing had also been staying in the hospital with his mother. He also studied his body that was corrupted by Wuying Qi 2.0.

The mother and the son, whose relationship had been special, must've had a lot to talk about.

The leaders of the reformists disagreed greatly on how to deal with Li Linghai.

On one hand, she was the leader and even founder of the reformists as a party. The Deep Sea Fleet, which was the fundamental force of the reformists, was established by her in person too.

Although the Astounding Thunder Fleet had replaced the Deep Sea Fleet as the most powerful troop of the reformists, no war could be won by a single fleet. Lei Chenghu had to consider the attitude of the Deep Sea Fleet. If he punished Li Linghai severely, would he leave Li Linghai's subordinates with the impression that he was initiating a purge?

On the other hand, everything Li Linghai did was because she was instructed by Wuying Qi so that he could reclaim the Imperium. However, neither Li Linghai nor anyone else could have resisted Wuying Qi if Wuying Qi possessed them back then. So, it was not entirely Li Linghai's fault that she allowed Wuying Qi to take her body to do evil things.

Besides, Li Linghai still had a lot of secrets, including the location of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum, which was critical for the future of the Imperium.

So, after weighing the pros and cons, the leaders of the reformists decided to understate Li Linghai's negative role in the Next Sun Plan and to not judge her openly so that everything could come to a peaceful end.

Naturally, everybody was delighted by the result. Even Li Yao felt that it was the best arrangement.

Three months after the battle of the capital, Li Yao had digested most of Wuying Qi 3.0's legacy in his head.

He also had a deeper understanding of how to control the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

An enormous super-remote base of communication was established at the edge of the Empyreal Terminus Sector under the supervision of Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King. It sent a message to the Star Glory Federation on the details of what happened after Li Yao arrived at the Imperium, and it asked for the parliament's decision on the relationship between the federation and the Imperium.

The information highway between the center of the cosmos and the edge of it was about to take shape!

…

In the Land of Eternal Night in the Ancient Sages Sector in the Star Glory Federation…

The Land of Eternal Night was not nearly as desolate and dangerous as when Li Yao set foot on it with the Three Saints and Four Rapscallions.

The weather specialists and professional engineers had reshaped the place and established lively "heat oases" on the ice land where a drop of water would be frozen into ice immediately. The hard-working people in the heat oases were exactly the epitome of the rising federation.

The federation had undergone great changes yet again in the past three years.

After their surrender, the Black Wind Fleet brought new technology and knowledge to the federation.

The specialists from the Black Wind Fleet also offered new perspectives for the federation's projects to explore Kunlun and learn the primeval technology.

The development of Skeleton Dragon and the solidarity of the seven sectors when they worked together to defend the federation made the country more united than ever.

The federal citizens had never seen the view at the center of the cosmos more clearly or known the gap between themselves and the Imperium better. All the Cultivators were filled with ever-burning fire and worked day and night for their motherland so that the Star Glory Federation could rise sooner and appear as a worthy rival for the Imperium and the Immortal Cultivators!

Chapter 2677: Good News

At this moment, Ding Lingdang was standing in the cold wind above the Land of Eternal Night with her hands behind her back.

Her red hair was burning like fire, forming a scarlet field around her that vaporized the ice in the cold wind when it reached her into popping steam, making her look even more mysterious and magnificent.

Having been a leader for two years, Ding Lingdang's previous aggression was gone, and she seemed much more mature than before. However, her eyes that were glittering in the wild wind and the dark night were telling everyone that she was as unstoppable as before.

The ice that had been frozen for thousands of years was cracking under Ding Lingdang's passionate eyes.

Twelve Colossi were floating in the wind behind Ding Lingdang and suppressing the freezing land like twelve pillars.

These twelve Colossi looked entirely different from the models of the federation or the Imperium. They seemed more vintage and primitive.

They were exactly the Twelve Gold Statues of Cloud Qin. The Cloud Qin Dynasty, the first regime that united the Ancient Sages Sector, couldn't have made the accomplishment without the twelve Colossi.

Later, the twelve Colossi were gradually abandoned due to the exhaustion of spiritual energy and the battle damage. When it came to the Great Qian Dynasty, nobody even knew where they were anymore.

However, after the Ancient Sages Sector joined the Star Glory Federation, people of the Star Glory Federation developed the Ancient Sages Sector with brand-new scanners, exploring both the mines and relics below the ground and the dark space outside of the planet.

Countless experts of the Ancient Sages Sector had died on their way to the realm of deities because of the lack of resources in the cold and dark vacuum.

In fact, how far could they have gone without a starship even if they were in the Divinity Transformation Stage or even the Divinity Branching Stage?

Those predecessors and the treasures that they carried were mostly floating on the orbit of the planet as space junk, until they were located by the Cultivators of the federation.

Just like that, the remains and legacies of tremendous predecessors were dug out and repaired by the specialists of the federation and the Black Wind Fleet, who also recovered a lot of marvelous arts that had unique characteristics of the primeval age, thereby significantly strengthening the federation.

Besides, a Nuwa warship was deeply buried in the Land of Eternal Night, and there was a Pangu base below it. Colossi that were almost intact could be found in both the warship and the base.

If those Colossi were repaired, the federation would definitely have the most Colossi per capita, even if the total number of their Colossi was not as good as that of the Imperium or the Covenant Alliance yet…

Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom!

Deafening explosions were rumbling in the sky.

They were the noises when the anti-gravity rune arrays on starships were fully activated.

Ding Lingdang raised her head and saw thousands of warships of the federation arriving at the Land of Eternal Night. The brilliant exhaust flames from the warships tore the dim sky apart and set it ablaze.

Hum! Hum! Hum! Hum!

The thousands of federal warships projected their force fields to the center of the Land of Eternal Night. More and more ice was cracking, and some rocks were protruding like tusks while others were collapsing into dents. As the earth trembled, the Nuwa warship was clearer and clearer. It was shivering as if it had been reinvigorated by the thousands of warships in the sky and seemed to be breaking out at any moment!

This was the reason why Ding Lingdang had to come to the Land of Eternal Night today.

After two years of prospecting and preparation, the federation was finally going to dig out the Nuwa warship that had been buried for hundreds of thousands of years!

Due to the special weather on the Land of Eternal Night, the warship had been sealed in ice and was still intact despite the passage of time.

When Long Yangjun fled a few years earlier, she only took away an escape capsule and did not destroy the overall structure of the warship.

After two years of studies, the specialists of the federation determined that the warship was still powerful and that if it was dug out and added with modules that would make it easier for human beings to control it, the warship would be the most powerful warship in the Pangu universe!
