It was certainly a big thing. Also, it involved the Pangu base that was beneath the Nuwa warship, which could lead to a disaster if any accident happened. Therefore, many top experts, including Ding Lingdang herself, had arrived in person to supervise the excavation of the Nuwa warship.

Thankfully, the excavation went quite well. The ice near the Nuwa warship was heated and melted first, the hard rocks were shattered by soundwave cannons, and then the Nuwa warship was slowly pulled out by hundreds of thousands of ropes to make sure that it would not fall and cause any accident.

"Hooray! Hooray!"

As they watched the warship from hundreds of thousands of years ago be reclaimed by the federation, the specialists and workers who had been working hard for two years in the depths of the Land of Eternal Night cheered and cried with tears of joy.

Even Ding Lingdang was in a trance as she stared at the glamorous patterns on the shell of the Nuwa warship, finding it hard to believe that the federation, which had been dwarfed by the Blood Demon Sector in the past, had grown to such an extent.

"Masteress! Masteress!"

Ding Lingdang heard a feeble voice among the raging wind and the noises of the warships.

She smiled. She had distinguished the master of the voice and did not expect that she would come.

When she turned back, she saw a silver super shuttle rushing to her like a crescent moon or a saber.

The pilot of the super shuttle seemed to think that she could be faster. She simply leaped out of the vehicle and jumped to Ding Lingdang with her enormous spiritual energy.

The guards from the Secret Sword Bureau, greatly shocked, tried to stop her.

"That's unnecessary. She's with me."

Ding Lingdang waved her hand with a smile. The guards immediately felt that they were pushed back to their places by a gentle force.

Speaking of which, those guards had always been awkward. They had been responsible for the safety of the Speakers, but Ding Lingdang was certainly different from any of the previous Speakers. If any accident happened, it would remain to be seen who protected whom!

The person leaped agilely in the cold wind as if she were moonlight. When she drew near, all the guards' portable crystal processors buzzed and showed her identity—Jin Xinyue, the former president of the Dim Moon Foundation and one of the two heads of special agents of the federation!

Ever since she almost made an unforgivable mistake in the battle to defend the federation because of the Dim Moon Plan she initiated, Jin Xinyue had resigned from the leadership of the federation and stopped showing up in public anymore.

However, she was certainly not a woman who would enjoy her retirement.

Over the past two years, she had been in charge of the communication with the Imperium. The intelligence from the Imperium had been analyzed and processed by the new department she established.

"Updates from Li Yao again?"

Ding Lingdang could easily tell it from Jin Xinyue's excitement.

Jin Xinyue wouldn't have passed the intelligence in person so hastily if it were only a piece of regular intelligence obtained by the Black Wind Fleet.

It must be the updates on Li Yao, who probably had caused great trouble again.

When she recalled the message that Li Yao sent last time, Ding Lingdang was amused again, wondering whether she should feel happy or angry.

She had given birth to two children without her knowing it!

"Yes, but it's 100% good news this time!"

Jin Xinyue said quickly, "My master… My master killed Blackstar the Great in person and helped the reformists occupy the heart of the Imperium!"

"W-What?"

Though Ding Lingdang had been through plenty of big events in the past two years, and Li Yao had told her a thing or two about it in his previous message, she was still dumbfounded for a long time. "Blackstar the Great? How could Li Yao have run into him? Is Li Yao fine?"

"It's true, and it's indeed rather… unbelievable. But I think my master is fine. Anyway, you'll know everything after you check this jade chip!"

Jin Xinyue gave the jade chip that stored the intelligence from the Imperium to Ding Lingdang.

"Well, well…"

Before she activated the jade chip, Ding Lingdang had started laughing. "It's good that he's fine. He's really a troublemaker anywhere he goes, but he always resolves the trouble in his own way.

"Blackstar the Great? You were truly unlucky to run into Li Yao.

"Today is really an exciting day for us. We've got a Nuwa warship and a piece of good news from Li Yao! What are you waiting for? Now, spread out all the details where Li Yao defeated Blackstar the Great to the whole Star Glory Federation so that all the Cultivators and federal citizens will be more courageous knowing what a fearless man Li Yao is!"

Chapter 2678: The First Step Into the Future!

"Well…"

Jin Xinyue seemed rather embarrassed. "Well, Masteress, well… I think it's a bit tricky."

"Huh?"

Ding Lingdang was confused. "How could it be tricky? Aren't you best at propaganda? Years ago, Li Yao was idolized as 'dominator of three Sectors' when he hadn't done anything yet. Now, he has crushed Blackstar the Great for real with his courage and wisdom. Even if you just tell it truthfully without any exaggeration, I bet that all our citizens will still be excited and encouraged!

"Chances are that more people will awaken their spiritual roots because of the motivation of Li Yao's glorious feat. They certainly will! So, just spread out the news already!"

"Well, I don't think so…"

Jin Xinyue smiled even more bitterly. "You'd better read the intelligence on the jade chip before you reach any conclusion."

"Huh?"

Ding Lingdang blinked her eyes. "Did Li Yao use any trick? That's understandable. Blackstar the Great was too formidable an enemy. I'm sure that Li Yao had to try everything he could. Can't you cover up for him as long as he didn't cross the line of the Cultivators?"

"I'm not sure whether or not my master crossed the line, but I'm sure that my propaganda machine can't work this time."

Jin Xinyue said, "Perhaps, Masteress, you can teach me how I can edit and modify those materials so that they can be publicized to the whole federation?"

Suspiciously, Ding Lingdang unlocked the jade chip.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Infinite information streams were launched into her brain as mystic rays.

Five minutes later, her expression changed from shock to embarrassment, and from embarrassment to stupefaction.

Ding Lingdang's face was even redder than the exhaust flames on the warships in the sky.

"This is…"

She was dumbfounded. "What… What the hell?"

"You're bothered, too, right?"

Jin Xinyue said, "I've given it a lot of thought, but I don't know where I should focus. Should I telecast the videos that my master displayed to the whole universe or the part where my master sang 'Mom is the Best' toward the end?"

"I think…"

Holding the jade chip, Ding Lingdang took a deep breath and said, "Forget it. Just ignore all the details and simply say 'Vulture Li Yao, the superhero of the federation, has defeated Blackstar the Great with his hot blood, his wisdom, and his persistence'."

"That's all?"

Jin Xinyue asked, "Wouldn't it be too simple?"

"No, it's enough."

Ding Lingdang rubbed her nose hard. "I don't know what I should say about this guy!"

The two women stared at each other for a long time and both burst into laughter.

"Only my master could've come up with such a way and defeated an enemy as powerful as Blackstar the Great!" Jin Xinyue remarked.

"That's right. In any case, he's still as alive as can be. That's good enough."

Ding Lingdang smiled, with tears of relief in her eyes.

"Then, masteress, what about that other thing that my master mentioned, about the federation's strategy next?"

Jin Xinyue said, "The civil war in the Imperium is not over yet, and Master has controlled the Gold Crystal Pyramid. I don't think the next emperor of the Imperium will be crazy enough to attack the Star Glory Federation. The safety of the federation is guaranteed in the coming decades.

"But the question remains, should we step forward and send an expedition army to join the war at the center of the cosmos?"

Ding Lingdang was deep in thought.

Things were changing too fast. A couple of years ago, the federation only wished to keep itself safe under the threat of the Black Wind Fleet, and it never thought that it could really march further into the sea of stars.

However, a rarely-seen opportunity had been presented to the Cultivators now. Should they seize it?

March to the center of the cosmos, help the reformists eliminate the four families and resist the Covenant Alliance, expand the influence of the Cultivators within the Imperium, and gradually change the nature of the Imperium… That seemed to be the best way for the Star Glory Federation to spread out its doctrine.

The powerful technology and magic equipment at the center of the cosmos had to be studied by the specialists in person too since the simple data that Li Yao sent back were not nearly enough.

Soon, the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum would be explored, and the Cultivators had to be part of it.

At the very least, Ding Lingdang, Fiend Blade Peng Hai, Jin Xinyue, and many other experts of the federation were all highly interested in the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum. They would not allow Li Yao to explore it with a bunch of Immortal Cultivators, in which case Li Yao would have nobody to help him if the Immortal Cultivators turned against him.

So, it seemed inevitable for the federation to join the rivalry at the center of the cosmos.

However, the risks were obvious too.

The federation was only as powerful as one of the four families, and the expedition army of the federation was only equal to one of the ace fleets of the four families at best.

Even four ace fleets had careened in the battle of the capital. Who knew whether or not worse battles would happen in the war between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance?

If the expedition army of the federation entered the Imperium, it would have no trustworthy allies because the reformists could likely change their attitude.

Once the expedition army was lost, the federation would be barely able to protect itself, and it would definitely be swallowed.

But in the meantime, the federation's location was no longer a secret. After the war was settled, the final victor, be it the Imperium or the Covenant Alliance, would definitely move their eyes to the federation.

So, waiting and doing nothing would mean doom too.

It was too important a decision to make. Even Ding Lingdang was deep in thought despite her decisiveness.

"Masteress…"

Jin Xinyue smiled bitterly. "Do you think that the next step is very tricky too?"

"Yes. It's a very important decision. I have to listen to the opinions of the think tanks and the parliament. I can't make a decision by myself."

Deep in thought, Ding Lingdang stared at the Nuwa warship that was rising higher and higher and murmured, "I remember that when I was a little girl, my town was once surrounded by a beast tide. The overwhelming demon beasts drowned our town, and everybody shuddered in fear…"

Jin Xinyue blushed and said, "It was a long time ago, when we were not clear and couldn't see the future."

"That's right. Nobody can see the future. When I shivered and cried in the shadow, I couldn't have foreseen what would become of me or the federation!"

Ding Lingdang clenched her fists and said, "But everything has changed after a hundred years. Who knows whether or not the new generation of the federation will be the master of the sea of stars a hundred years later?

"Anyway, the future entails infinite possibilities, but only if we make the first step toward it!"

"So to speak…"

Jin Xinyue said delightedly, "You agree to send an expedition army to reinforce the reformists, Masteress?"

"As the Speaker, I should not express my attitude before the parliament and the staff discussed this piece of critical intelligence."

Ding Lingdang smiled. Her red hair danced in the wind, and her fists were shivering beyond her control in excitement.

She seemed to be seeing the heart of the Imperium at the center of the cosmos through the Nuwa warship and the reddened sky. "I've finally got the postwar situation under control after working as the Speaker for such a long time, and I'm not interested in reelection. You know that I never intended to become the Speaker. I only competed with you because I was hoping to help you unite the forces that can be united. Over the past years, I've almost been driven crazy by the astronomical files that I had to process every day. I wished that my term could come to an end sooner so that I could go on adventures again in the most dangerous places. I'm more used to the unrestrained lifestyle.

"So, if I'm not the Speaker, and if I'm only your masteress and Li Yao's wife, I'll definitely go to the center of the cosmos. If the parliament does not agree to send out an expedition army, I'll go find Li Yao by myself when I'm off duty. I can't let him fight on his own anymore.

"Also, I believe that a lot of people are interested in the Imperium and will come with me."

Ding Lingdang glanced at the Colossi behind her that were driven by the twelve experts of the Ancient Sages Sector. One of them was measuring the Nuwa warship in the sky with its longsword, as if it were considering if it could cut the Nuwa warship into halves.

It was exactly the Colossus controlled by Yan Liren, the Sword Maniac.

Ding Lingdang's smile was even more brilliant.

"How I look forward to it!"

She mumbled, "The future federation, the future universe, and the future civilization of mankind!"

Chapter 2679: Gods' Mausoleum!

At the same time, in a military hospital in the Empyreal Terminus Sector at the center of the cosmos…

Before Li Yao met Li Linghai again, he never thought that the queen of the Imperium would be painting butterflies in a small garden under the warm sun.

Judging from her vivid work, she seemed to be as good as a professional painter.

Her body had been greatly weakened, but she did not look very fragile. Instead, warmed by the sunlight, even her white hair and her transparent skin did not seem as chilling as before.

"Well…"

Standing behind Li Linghai for a long time, Li Yao watched her dye the wings of a butterfly with glamorous colors, not knowing what he should say. He finally said, "Your Highness, have you decided to get rid of this body and turn into a spectral Cultivator? If you have, I can make an artificial body for you in person. After all, I'm still your special refiner, haha!"

"Thank you…"

Li Linghai finished the last stroke and smiled. "… For everything."

"Everything…"

Li Yao was even more embarrassed, feeling that his head was itchy.

When he fought Wuying Qi, he was too focused on his enemy to feel anything wrong. But several months later, after he calmed down, when he thought about what happened in retrospect, displaying Wuying Qi's porn videos and singing "Mom Is the Best" were both humiliating.

Even Vulture Li Yao did not love losing face. This battle was the greatest taint of his glorious life. He was not brazen enough to accept Li Linghai's gratitude.

Noticing his awkwardness, Li Linghai smiled and continued, "Believe it or not, no matter how I feel about the emperor and the four Kurfürsten, this is my homeland, and I never meant to destroy the Imperium. Thank you for stopping me and Wuying Qi. Thank you for saving the Imperium!"

"It was nothing. As long as you still believe in renewing the Imperium and fulfill your earlier promise, I would save the Imperium again if I have to."

Li Yao paused for a moment and couldn't help but ask, "I've been curious about one thing. Were you threatened by Wuying Qi, allured by power, or deceived by the bright future that Wuying Qi painted? That day, I said to Li Jialing that Wuying Qi threatened you with your other kids. Am I right?"

"Threat, deception, or allurement… Who can tell it precisely?"

Li Linghai smiled and murmured at the dancing butterflies, "Admiration in Wuying Qi, resentment at my family and the emperor, love for my kids, lust for power, and the curiosity about the universe beyond our universe… I think they all played a part."

"Fine."

Though Li Yao was not entirely satisfied with the answer, he knew that it was probably the truth. Taking a long breath in relief, Li Yao asked again, "Your Highness, there's another question that's more private. You can refuse it if you would rather not answer it. Did you… really fall in love with my foster father? Or rather, how did you awaken an emotionless person of the Covenant Alliance and turn him into a normal man?

"Please don't misunderstand me. I just think that your answer will be helpful for us in resisting the Covenant Alliance's attack."

"I loved him."

After such a long silence that Li Yao thought that she would not reply, she finally said, "Also, you're wrong. Even the people of the Covenant Alliance have feelings and desires, except that their feelings and desires are suppressed. However, feelings and desires are in the nature of human beings. How can they be completely suppressed?

"On the contrary, if you find an appropriate way to provoke their feelings and desires, it will be like a starving man who finds delicious food or a desert that has rain after decades of drought. It's so unforgettable that even Blackstar the Great could not completely wipe it out!"

"So, it's like the harder you press a spring, the higher it will bounce back later?"

Li Yao mumbled, "If we find the trick, we may be able to finish the Covenant Alliance without any soldier by waking up their feelings and desires…"

"How could it be so simple?"

Li Linghai was amused. "If it were so simple, the Covenant Alliance wouldn't have been the Imperium's headache for a thousand years. I was able to wake up your foster father because of many fortuitous factors and even Blackstar the Great's unspoken help. It's hardly duplicable."

"It does make sense."

Seeing that Li Linghai was in a good mood, Li Yao pressed further and asked, "Your Highness, can you tell me my foster father's real name and the details when you were together in the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum?"

"No."

Li Linghai shook her head and let the gentle sunlight gather into a butterfly on the back of her hand. She smiled. "It's a memory that only belongs to me. I won't tell it to anyone, especially not to you."

"You can keep it a secret."

Li Yao said, feeling embarrassed, "But why 'especially not to me'?"

"I do not want to tell you my privacy, but as compensation, I can tell you something else."

Li Linghai smiled. "You've known a thing or two about the coordinates of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum and its secrets from Wuying Qi 3.0, but do you want to know more?"

"Of course I do!"

The Supreme Emperor's mausoleum was an important location for Li Yao in the next phase. He pricked his ears and said, "Please, Your Highness. I'm all ears!"

"In the past thousands of years, the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum has been a top-secret for all the experts. Everybody tried to find it, but until a thousand years ago, nobody had any success."

Li Linghai introduced, "The reason is very simple. That's because the entrance to the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum is not fixed but moves randomly.

"To put it simply, the world fragment where the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum is located is not entirely in the three-dimensional universe. Instead, it's floating in the 3.5 dimension between the three-dimensional universe and the four-dimensional one.

"If we break the void luckily and place us between the three-dimension and the four-dimension, we may be able to see the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum and its trajectory accurately, but from the three-dimensional perspective, it will always be wandering among dozens of Sectors without any pattern.

"Years back, your foster father and I were blown to the entrance of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum by a space storm by accident, but Wuying Qi had a special equation that he learned from the Blood God, which allowed him to calculate the location of the entrance of the mausoleum in every cycle.

"Later, Wuying Qi taught the equation to me, and I'm teaching it to you today. Then, in a few special time windows in a year, you'll be able to find the entrance to the mausoleum and enter the mysterious world fragment!"

"That sounds amazing."

Li Yao was astonished. No wonder nobody had ever found this world fragment in the past thousands of years. Its entrance turned out to be unpredictable!

"However, there won't be any use even if you can go to the world fragment."

Li Linghai suddenly said, "Because most of the valuable things in this world fragment have been excavated by me in the past decades as resources to unite the reformists. There can't be better treasures in the world fragment than what you've already seen in my base."

"What?"

Shocked, Li Yao realized what she meant. "Are you saying that we cannot get the real secret until we open the core of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum and find out the Supreme Emperor's remains?

"Right, the key! The key that is divided into two parts can open the core!"

"Yes and no."

Li Linghai said, "The key can open the core, but the core is not a simple tomb but probably another world fragment. The Supreme Emperor's mausoleum is like a cave within a cave. The world fragment on the outside is only a platform to the internal world fragment, which is a mausoleum of gods."

"A mausoleum of gods?"

Li Yao keenly sensed Li Linghai's different choice of words. "What do you mean? Isn't it the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum?"

"It's both the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum and a mausoleum of the gods."

Li Linghai's eyes turned sharp. She grabbed her painting and declared resolutely, "It's the Gods' Mausoleum!"

Chapter 2680: Neither Alive Nor Dead!

"The Gods' Mausoleum?"

Li Yao gasped hard.

In the language of mankind, "gods" only referred to the ultimate experts of the thirteen carbon-based lives in the primeval age.

"Is it the place where the ultimate experts of the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, the Kuafu Clan, the Houyi Clan, and other primeval species were buried? Is it an ultra-huge prehistory mausoleum?"

Li Yao found it hard to believe.

"That's right. If you think about it, the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God met each other by accident below the ground of the capital planet. Neither of them was ready to die with each other. Where did the Supreme Emperor have the time to prepare a mausoleum for himself?"

Li Linghai asked, "After he went missing, the Star Ocean Imperium was falling apart. Who would build a mausoleum for him?"

Li Yao realized that it did make sense. The Supreme Emperor's and the Blood God's troops mostly ran loose after their commanders went missing. The Supreme Emperor's mausoleum would've been torn down by them even if it had been built.

Could it have been built before the Blood God's rebellion? At that time, the Star Ocean Imperium was still expanding. Li Yao did not think that the Supreme Emperor would've devoted a lot of resources to build a mausoleum for himself.

So, while people called the place the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum, it couldn't have been the Supreme Emperor's at the beginning. It belonged to somebody more ancient and powerful than the Supreme Emperor, the primeval experts!

"Are you not curious about the Supreme Emperor's rise?"

Li Linghai continued, "Neither the Star Ocean Republic nor the Imperium of True Human Beings solved the puzzle of the Supreme Emperor's rise. When the prospect of mankind was still gloomy, the Supreme Emperor shined like a shooting star and accelerated the renaissance of mankind almost purely on his own.

"Nobody knows how he picked up his unparalleled strength and his unbelievable commanding arts.

"In many legends, the Supreme Emperor became the strongest Cultivator in history because of three volumes of the Book of Revelation. But where did he get the three volumes of the book?"

Intrigued, Li Yao asked, "The Supreme Emperor found the three volumes of the book from the Gods' Mausoleum and passed them on to mankind? Everything makes sense now!"

"Yes."

Li Linghai said, "The Blood God told that to Wuying Qi, and Wuying Qi told it to me. As a person who sort of experienced it in person, the Blood God is definitely more trustworthy than the scholars outside.

"The Supreme Emperor was only a gladiator caged in a bloody arena at the beginning. He escaped in an uprising and accidentally found the Gods' Mausoleum on his way of escape. He grasped unparalleled arts in the Gods' Mausoleum and found three volumes of the Book of Revelation. It was the beginning of his glory.

"At that time, he still lacked the personnel and resources to develop the Gods' Mausoleum.

"But after he united the sea of stars again, he mobilized tremendous starships and engineering legions to the external world fragment, ready to open the gate of the Gods' Mausoleum.

"However, the rebellion of the Blood God disrupted the Supreme Emperor's development plan, and the magic equipment of the engineering legions were left in the world fragment. Those gears were what the reformists were based on at the beginning.

"After the Supreme Emperor had a battle with the Blood God and nearly got himself killed, he had to escape into the Gods' Mausoleum, ending exactly like the primeval experts hundreds of thousands of years earlier!"

Li Yao rolled his eyes and keenly noticed a problem. "The Supreme Emperor fled to the Gods' Mausoleum when he was dying, and the place was where the primeval experts were buried. Is there something wrong with the place? Why did the Supreme Emperor have to die there?"

"You're very smart to see the key."

Li Linghai smiled and said, "Maybe you're right. It is said that the Gods' Mausoleum is not bound by natural laws. Time is even frozen inside. While the Supreme Emperor would die for sure because of his heavy wounds, he would remain in a mysterious state of 'neither alive nor dead' in the Gods' Mausoleum."

"Not bound by natural laws?"

Li Yao was at a loss. "What do you mean? Also, what do you mean by 'time is frozen'?"

"I have no idea. Those were what the Blood God told to Wuying Qi before Wuying Qi forwarded to me."

Li Linghai said, "While the Blood God was the Supreme Emperor's strongest clone, he only had the Supreme Emperor's parts on battle, exploration, and conquest, and he had few memories except fighting. Also, I'm not sure he told Wuying Qi everything, or that Wuying Qi told everything to me.

"All in all, my understanding is that the Gods' Mausoleum is even more distant from our three-dimensional universe. If our three-dimensional universe can be compared to a planet, the Gods' Mausoleum will be a satellite that is very far away from us and may be under the attraction of another planet, which gives it unbelievable laws!

"As for 'neither alive nor dead', Wuying Qi also forwarded to me something the Blood God said to him: 'Until an external observer opens the Gods' Mausoleum, the Supreme Emperor will be in the state of superposition where he's neither alive nor dead'. I don't know what it means. Maybe you can find it out and explain it to me one day."

Li Yao thought for a long time.

The intensity of spiritual energy in the different parts of the universe varied, resulting in the different natural laws. It was nothing to be surprised at.

However, another thought that popped up in his head made him shiver.

"Hold on a second…"

Li Yao mumbled, "Assuming that the Blood God did not lie to Wuying Qi and Wuying Qi did not lie to you, and time is really frozen in the Gods' Mausoleum, making all the creatures inside neither alive nor dead, wouldn't it mean that countless primeval experts are still 'neither alive nor dead' in the Gods' Mausoleum besides the Supreme Emperor?"

"I'm afraid so."

Li Linghai smiled bitterly and said, "Why else do you think Wuying Qi never had the courage to set foot in the Gods' Mausoleum?"

Li Yao was going to say that it was because Wuying Qi lacked half of the key.

However, on second thought, half of the key was controlled by Li Linghai, and the other half was controlled by his foster father. Those two were together in the world fragment a hundred years ago.

In other words, Li Linghai, his foster father, and Wuying Qi could've combined the parts into a complete key!

Even though Wuying Qi was terribly weakened back then, he must've been irresistible for Li Linghai and his foster father.

If he weren't concerned for what was in the Gods' Mausoleum, he probably wouldn't rob them of the key and enter it.

"No wonder Wuying Qi 3.0 kept reminding me that I was not worthy enough to explore the Gods' Mausoleum."

Li Yao said in fear, "He said that I must not open the gate to the Gods' Mausoleum until I reach the Divinity Branching Stage."

"Yes. Nobody knows what will happen if the gate to the Gods' Mausoleum is opened."

Li Linghai said, "The worst-case scenario is that hundreds of primeval experts who were 'neither alive nor dead' are woken up after the Gods' Mausoleum is opened. Who can bear such a consequence?"

Li Yao sweated hard in fright.

He remembered the member of the Pangu Clan he executed in Kunlun. That creature could fight a Colossus bare-handedly!

Besides, that member of the Pangu Clan must've been a common officer or a noble, instead of an ultimate expert in his age.

Nobody could tell whether those experts would be alive or dead when an external observer opened the Gods' Mausoleum and terminated the superposition state!

If he explored the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum only to unleash hundreds of living primeval experts, he would be the greatest criminal for mankind.

"We'd better not rush into anything yet!"

Li Yao wiped his cold sweat and said, "Let's finish the four families and the Covenant Alliance first. It will be best if I can reach the Divinity Branching Stage first before we do anything. Wuying Qi left a secret chamber in the Gold Crystal Pyramid for his training. After all this mess is done, I'll train myself in seclusion for a while to make another breakthrough. I would be embarrassed if I get beaten up by Li Jialing in a couple of days, wouldn't I?"

Li Linghai chuckled and nodded her head. "You're not too tempted by the mysterious mausoleum to notice the danger in it, which is great! However, while we can't rush into anything, we cannot make preparations all the time either. As far as I know, the Covenant Alliance is also searching for the Gods' Mausoleum, and they're attacking the Imperium partly for the Gods' Mausoleum too."

Chapter 2681: After the Divinity Branching Stage!

"W-What?"

Li Yao stammered again, "How is the Covenant Alliance related to the Gods' Mausoleum?"

"Opening the Gods' Mausoleum has been one of the Covenant Alliance's purposes since it was established!"

Li Linghai said, "Think about it. The people of the Covenant Alliance believe in the Path of Ultimate Benevolence under the boundary of the three fundamental laws. They consider themselves as the simple tools of the primeval civilizations. So, the only significance of their lives is to wake up or resurrect the primeval civilizations.

"The Gods' Mausoleum, on the other hand, is likely to be a shelter of the ultimate experts of the primeval age. Opening the Gods' Mausoleum means restoring the glory of the primeval age. When the Covenant Alliance learns of the Gods' Mausoleum, how could they not be interested in it?"

"It does make sense."

Li Yao scratched his head and said, "That is to say, the Covenant Alliance's ultimate purpose is to open the Gods' Mausoleum and wake up the primeval experts. As long as they could achieve the purpose, it doesn't matter whether or not they can defeat the Imperium, or if the Covenant Alliance can survive the war!"

"Exactly. That's why the Covenant Alliance is dreadful."

Li Linghai smiled bitterly. "I wouldn't be scared of the Star Glory Federation even if it were a hundred times more powerful because it's a country of normal people, and we can understand each other's purposes and strategies. Both of us need territory, population, and development.

"However, the Covenant Alliance thinks entirely differently from us. They're even more zealous than the most extreme fundamentalists. They're willing to sacrifice billions of people to bring the primeval civilizations back to life without caring about their own future. How can you deal with such lunatics?"

Li Yao said in fear, "Thankfully, the key to the Gods' Mausoleum is in our hands."

"Not necessarily."

Li Linghai said, "It might not be the only key to the Gods' Mausoleum, which could have more gates or ways to be opened. It's even possible that it will be opened automatically at a certain point. If the Gods' Mausoleum is not a tomb but a shelter, would the primeval experts have not set a timer to wake themselves up?"

Li Yao was greatly shocked.

He remembered that when he traveled over a long distance in hibernation, he would wake himself up every couple of years to ensure that nothing was wrong.

The similar facility of the primeval civilizations must've boasted similar functions.

"We don't have a lot of time."

Li Linghai said, "At the very least, the message that Wuying Qi received from the Blood God was urgent. If the Blood God had a choice, he wouldn't have taught Wuying Qi everything at great risks. So, I'm highly suspicious that the Covenant Alliance is not trying to conquer the Imperium. It cannot swallow the Imperium with a single war anyway. However, it's possible for the Covenant Alliance to claim the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum after heavily wounding the Imperium."

"Yes. In such a case, it would be understandable why the Covenant Alliance suffered brutal failures in the Imperium's counterattack earlier."

Li Yao murmured, "The Alliance of the Holy Covenant cannot be regarded as a normal country. It's a collection of lunatics that do not care about territory or people and only want to open the Gods' Mausoleum!"

Li Yao felt that the situation was getting tricky again.

But his personal goal was clearer than before.

First of all, he must help the reformists defeat the four families and restore the Cultivators' influence in the general public of the Imperium.

Secondly, he would stop the Covenant Alliance's conquest and maintain the balance at the center of the cosmos.

Of course, he couldn't allow the Covenant Alliance to open the Gods' Mausoleum and wake up the terrifying primeval experts. If an external observer was to open it, it had to be him!

By the time he figured out a way to open the Gods' Mausoleum without waking up the sleepers and get the whole Book of Revelation, it would probably be the time for him to cross the black wall and explore the vast world beyond the Pangu universe.

He was sure that the mysterious Earth would be revealed by then!

Pangu and the Supreme Emperor represented conservativeness and stability, whereas Nuwa and the Blood God represented radicalness and rashness. Li Yao was undoubtedly on the latter's side.

It was not because he was reckless or influenced by the Blood God, but because he had analyzed it calmly and rationally.

The greatest reasoning of the conservatives was that the Flood might be wandering beyond the black wall and would destroy the Pangu universe the moment it detected the Pangu universe.

However, the very existence of Li Yao had proved that the black wall was not insurmountable and that the information from the universe outside could come in!

If Li Yao could transmigrate from Earth to the Pangu universe, would this place be a secret to the Flood forever?

Hiding here would still mean doom. Mankind did not have a choice except to face the challenge!

"I have to try to enter the Divinity Branching Stage even though my foundation is not stable yet!"

Infinite fire danced in Li Yao's eyes. He clenched his fists and declared, "I will certainly reach the Divinity Branching Stage!"

"Few people have reached the Divinity Branching Stage even in the Imperium, particularly the battle-type ones."

Looking at Li Yao with complicated feelings, Li Linghai hesitated for a moment, but in the end, she still said, "You're very courageous. However, the Divinity Branching Stage is highly different from the Divinity Transformation Stage in that it has a much higher demand on your understanding of the nature of lives.

"When I was possessed by Wuying Qi, I was able to witness the wonders of the Divinity Branching Stage, which was truly… incredible. It's a difficult breakthrough from 'individual wisdom' to 'collective wisdom'."

Intrigued, Li Yao listened attentively.

"You know that mankind is the offspring of thirteen carbon-based lives, and our genes contained their best parts. However, not all of the carbon-based lives were individuals. Some of them, like the Houyi Clan, boasted collective wisdom, like swarms of bees."

Li Linghai said, "A single bee does not have wisdom but only simple reactions. But as their number reaches a tipping point, they will boast something that can almost be called wisdom. When their groups split and regather, wisdom can even spread among them like water.

"Such a life form does not have the concept of individuals. Small communities make up a big community, and big communities make up a tribe. It's like our body is made of countless cells and the loss of several cells does not mean that we're dead. For the collective civilizations, the loss of half of their individuals does not mean that those individuals are dead.

"Individual wisdom and collective wisdom have their own advantages. Neither of them is better than the other. Human beings are individuals under normal circumstances, but with the genes of the Houyi Clan, we can activate certain techniques invented with the collective wisdom. That's exactly the mysteries of the Divinity Branching Stage.

"You were attacked by Wuying Qinxin in the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in the Seven Seas Space Zone. Wuying Qinxin was to some extent one of the individuals that Wuying Qi controlled. I, Wuying Qi, Wuying Qinxin, and some other people formed a small colony back then.

"Of course, collective wisdom, just like domain, has range limitations. Also, Wuying Qi's soul was too broken for him to carry out the wonders of the Divinity Branching Stage. Otherwise, you couldn't have defeated him if he had completely controlled the hundreds of bald experts in the Gold Crystal Pyramid and combined them into one creature, could you?"

"Well…"

Li Yao was going to say disgusting, but then, he changed his opinion and said, "It's so amazing?"

"Do you think that it's too much for you to bear?"

Seeing through his real thoughts, Li Linghai smiled and said, "The Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators have already reached the limits of mankind in terms of physical and spiritual strength. The higher levels are more about the understanding of life and the universe, and many concepts are indeed hard to accept. After all, human beings are but grains of dust in the universe. How can a grain of dust not be shocked when it tries to understand everything?

"The Divinity Branching Stage is all about activating the genes of collective wisdom and splitting your soul into thousands of parts while keeping all those parts in unity. The stages above the Divinity Branching Stage is said to be about seeing through the falseness of the universe and finding the truth in it. It's even more mysterious and hard to understand!"

Attracted, Li Yao couldn't help but ask, "Then what?"

"Then?"

Li Linghai smiled and said, "After you completely understand the truth and the falseness of the universe, I think this universe will be too small for you. You'll have to find other places to stay in.

"This is the conclusion that the Blood God, Wuying Qi, and I have made after studying many classics on training. The ancient people exaggerated a lot of stuff in their books, but as technology advances, we are closer and closer to the truth of the world now.

"All in all, how can we picture what's beyond when it's already so difficult to understand the universe as it is? There is no need to get ahead of ourselves."

"It does make sense."

Taking a long breath in relief, Li Yao smiled. "I'll just focus on training instead of thinking. I'll be a glittering grain of dust that illuminates every place that I float to."

"If you mean it, I'll believe that you'll go beyond the Divinity Branching Stage and find the secrets of the universe someday."

With a brilliant smile, Li Linghai said, "But for now, it would be better for you to focus on improving yourself. Right, you can practice with Jialing in the rehabilitation room behind. There's something important that he would like to talk to you about."

Chapter 2682: Transformation of the Little Lion!

When Li Yao met Li Jialing, the young man had just finished a daily training session under twenty times the regular gravity.

This military hospital was not a sanitarium, and people who were treating themselves in this place did not mind inputting more pain into themselves to stimulate the growth of their cells so that they could recover faster.

However, even the No. 1 training room, which had the most advanced facilities and could cause the most pain, could not meet Li Jialing's needs.

As if he were a hungry lion, he wandered among electric arcs and flames even though every inch of his skin was crying.

Li Yao had been too busy modifying the Gold Crystal Pyramid to talk to his little brother. After they saw each other again today, Li Yao sensed that Li Jialing had grown a lot without him realizing it. Li Jialing now had much more muscles than before, and his shoulders were broad enough to support a warship. Ripples were spreading out from his body and tearing apart the air all the time. The gold streaks in his skin that only glittered under lights further added to his dominance, suggesting that a young boy had evolved into a real man.

The spacious No. 1 training room was empty except for Li Jialing as well as the debris all over the ground.

Li Yao scanned the ground and the data on the mainframe crystal processor nearby, only to discover that Li Jialing had torn down a hundred spiritual puppets and more than twenty crystal tanks under twenty times the normal gravity in the past five minutes. He had punched those heavy tanks into pieces single-handedly.

At this moment, dozens of flying swords were still dancing around Li Jialing and darting at his critical parts under the control of mini crystal processors.

Li Jialing's body was huge, but he wandered among the dozens of flying swords relaxedly as if he were a streak of gold light.

Li Yao had a lot of complicated feelings seeing how much his little brother had grown since he was rescued from the City in the Sky a year ago.

Li Yao had thought that his speed of advancement was astonishing enough, but Li Jialing had truly opened his eyes.

It couldn't be helped. Although Li Yao had encountered many fortuitous incidents and trained crazily, he did not have a great teacher or enough resources before the age of eighteen. His father was a remarkable refiner but did not know much about fighting, and Li Yao learned how to fight based on his real experience with the scavengers in the magic equipment graveyard.

Li Jialing was quite different. He had received Blackstar the Great's attention before he was even born, and after he was born, Li Lingfeng and Wuying Lan modified him without caring about the cost. Though their methods were cruel, they did inject unparalleled power into his cells. When the power met Wuying Qi 2.0's legacy, they had amazing reactions and finally built such a dominating expert!

"Not that I'm not talented enough, but Jialing is simply too talented!"

Li Yao could only comfort himself like that.

"Brother Yao?"

Thanks to his keen senses, Li Jialing detected Li Yao even though Li Yao did not make a sound. He turned back in surprise.

When he was distracted, his flawless dodging was broken, and the flying swords seemed to be hitting his critical parts.

The young man, however, bit his teeth, and a gold spiritual shield expanded from his body, locking the flying swords, which could not press forward at all no matter how they tried.

"Good!"

Li Yao couldn't hold on anymore. He stepped into the training room and warmed himself up. "Let me practice with you. I'll see how much you have grown in the past months!"

"Really?"

Li Jialing was so excited that his nostrils were bulging. He roared, "Okay, Brother Yao, I'm coming!"

Golden flames of spiritual energy soared from him, gathering the dozens of flying swords that came at him to Li Yao. Under the cover of the torrent of flying swords, Li Jialing rushed to Li Yao, leaving deep claw scratches on the ground!

Li Yao could see nothing but golden flying swords.

He smiled thirstily. His battle robe was immediately ripped into shreds, but his real body inside the robe flashed to Li Jialing's back from an unbelievable perspective and grabbed the barrel of a broken crystal tank.

"Ho!"

His upper half body naked, Li Yao waved the crystal tank that weighed dozens of tons and hurled it at Li Jialing, blowing the young lion up into the sky.

It was far from the end. Right when Li Jialing lost balance, Li Yao stomped on the ground and knocked another ten or so broken crystal tanks into the sky too. Then, he leaped and threw the crystal tanks at Li Jialing one after another.

Li Jialing was like a poor fly that was patted by swatters nonstop. He was immediately disfigured.

However, after six tanks fell apart on Li Jialing, the seventh tank missed the target.

Li Jialing had disappeared!

Floating in midair, Li Yao narrowed his eyes and scanned the training field, only to see nothing, until a beastly vibe suddenly burst out right below him!

"Hooooooo!"

Li Jialing roared crazily. His skin was cracking and his hair was rising as he turned into a horrendous monster more than four meters tall!

Metamorphosis! It was his talent that he received because of the twenty years of painful modifications on the City in the Sky!

Earlier, when Li Yao blew up the City in the Sky, he had fought Wuying Lan hard, and this skill left a deep impression on him.

Li Jialing's transformation was even more violent and complete than Wuying Lan's. Li Yao even felt slightly alarmed.

Li Jialing stomped on the ground, leaving cracks on it, before he charged at Li Yao like a cannonball. In the meantime, he threw out two pieces of burning debris, forcing Li Yao to take his attack the hard way instead of dodging it.

Boom!

After the collision, Li Yao was bounced high all the way into the dome by the force, and Li Jialing was hit into the ground and embedded into the floor that was made of superalloys.

"I didn't know that you were so strong already, Jialing!"

Wiping away the blood from his bleeding nose, Li Yao said in amazement, "I'm bleeding! Few people in the whole Pangu universe can make me bleed now. You can definitely go anywhere you want in this universe with your strength!"

"…"

Though he had turned into a hideous monster, Li Jialing still felt like spitting at him.

But before he spat out, Li Jialing suddenly had an ominous feeling.

On the dome, Li Yao was gradually twisting and dispersing. It turned out to be only an illusion.

The real Li Yao, on the other hand, had been hiding behind Li Jialing under the cover of the blast just now!

When Li Jialing realized what was going on, it was too late.

His left ankle was grabbed by Li Yao, and Li Yao smote him to the wall and the ground like a hammer for hundreds of times before he threw Li Jialing away.

During the whole time, Li Yao had also penetrated threads of spiritual energy into Li Jialing's veins and bones so that he could not counterattack!

"I would be disappointed if this is what you've accomplished after months of training."

Li Yao said casually, "This can't be your ultimate form, is it? I remember that you can transform three times, right? Come on, show me who you really are!"

"Good!"

Li Jialing grimaced in agony, and his fighting will was soaring under the pain. Gold streaks popped up on his skin, and his enormous body was changing. Soft hair was growing out too. He changed from a hideous monster into a lion demon that was standing like a human!

However, before he made his pose, infinite burning remnants and raging blasts were smashed at him.

"Let Brother Yao teach you another lesson! If you have to transform, make it fast. No idiots will wait for you to transform in a battlefield!"

Hooting, Li Yao threw all the remnants around that could be used as a weapon to Li Jialing while he secretly mumbled, "I better enjoy myself while I still can. When he is stronger than me two years later… I'll never practice with him again. Hehehehe!"

Very soon, the poor gold lion demon was submerged by the remnants.

The remnants were then melted and quickly cooled down into an enormous metal cube.

Li Jialing did not make any sound from inside. Even his vitality field could not be detected anymore.

After having enjoyed himself, Li Yao began to worry. "Really? The little young lion should be sturdier than this, right? Wuying Qi 2.0's heritage can't be that unremarkable. Huh, wait. This is…"

Boom!

The metal garbage made of countless remnants suddenly exploded, and a brand-new Li Jialing flew out. He had restored the appearance of a human, but he looked completely different from before. His hair became golden in color and extended all the way to his waist, and his eyes were filled with a gold ocean. The gold stripes all over him formed a flawless armor, with purple electric arcs dancing on it like dragons, making him look more magnificent than ever!

"Wow!"

Li Yao was dumbfounded and dazzled. "How can you be so cool?"

"Excuse me, Brother Yao."

Li Jialing wiped off the dirt on him softly and said, "After I digested Wuying Qi 2.0's heritage in the past three months, I've developed a new shape. The lion demon form just now was only my transitional form, and this is my perfect ultimate form!"

Chapter 2683: Brother Yao's Support!

"What?"

Every inch of Li Yao's skin was under the bombardment of Li Jialing's storms, as if thousands of needles were poking him.

His bloodshot eyes were filled with disbelief. "This is so cool. Tell me exactly how you trained yourself to get it. Why could you have developed such a cool form? It's even more awesome than me in the peak of the Divinity Transformation Stage. How can it happen? I won't allow it!"

"Huh…"

Li Jialing thought for a moment and said in embarrassment, "Actually, I didn't do anything on purpose. I just trained casually, and I became as handsome as this."

"Ah!"

Li Yao shrieked, and the bloodiness from his left eye surrounded him and picked up the remnants all over the ground, gathering them into an appalling enormous saber.

Boom!

The saber of remains was slashed at Li Jialing, while Li Yao said in a heartfelt way, "Jialing, you're on the wrong way! Such precious spiritual energy should be used to fulfill your cells and your soul, instead of making those cool sounds and visual effects!"

"I don't want that either!"

Li Jialing crossed his arms, and the golden fire and the purple dragons entwined and protected his face, blocking Li Yao's unstoppable slash and even crushing the saber of remains into pieces again.

The young man roared, "I think this is too eye-catching too. I'll definitely be the center of attention if I transform in a battlefield. But I can't control myself. Whenever I upgrade myself to the ultimate form, some of my spiritual energy will flow out naturally and get me into this look. What can I do?"

"It's alright. Let Brother Yao help you!"

Li Yao grinned hideously. His bulging veins circled before his chest like nine bloody dragons and unfolded a pair of bloody wings on his back. Two crystals the size of goose eggs appeared in his hands again, with dazzling electric arcs in them. "Actually, Brother Yao was ten times cooler than you before, but I've learned to abandon the superficial things. Now, let me teach this precious lesson to you!"

Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom!

Li Yao stepped forward quickly.

After every step, the enormous training field would shake as if it were about to fall, until he disappeared in the end, replaced by an overwhelming tsunami of blood!

Li Jialing, on the other hand, unleashed his light and heat nonstop like a gold volcano that was erupting in the blood ocean.

The two of them fought a hundred rounds in the blink of an eye. While Li Jialing was knocked away by Li Yao dozens of times, Li Yao had been hit by Li Jialing's magnificent purple dragons too. Neither of them was really hurt, but the wall, the floor, the gravity control units, and the defense rune arrays all over the training field suffered. It was not like the training of two experts, but more like the mutual bombardment of two super arsenal warships in this tiny room!

Very soon, shocking footprints, dents, and cracks had appeared on the floor, which was as soft as mud now.

Enormous human-shaped marks could be found on the wall too. Some of them were even half a meter deep!

Noises were bursting out everywhere. Both the gravity control units and the defense rune arrays were about to be crushed by the aftermath of their battle.

The lights were also flashing, until all the illumination rune arrays exploded and the training room fell into darkness. However, a golden fire and a red tide were still colliding in complicated shapes, as if they were two lines that had tangled.

Boom!

After another astounding explosion, the defense rune arrays finally collapsed, and a huge part of the dome fell too.

The whole training field was as vulnerable as if it had been soaked in water for three days, and it might collapse at any moment.

"Hu… Hu… Hu… Hu…"

Both Li Yao and Li Jialing panted quickly in the darkness.

"This is exhilarating!"

Li Yao couldn't have sounded more excited. "This is more like what Vulture Li Yao's brother and Wuying Qi 2.0's heir can do!"

"I'm never more satisfied either!"

Li Jialing was even more ecstatic than he was. "It seems that only Brother Yao can make me unleash all my potential! I feel great! I think I can still fight! Brother Yao, I know that you're not trying your best, are you? Just bring it on. Don't fear that you might hurt me. I'm fine!"

"Really?"

Li Yao sounded doubtful. "I've made some breakthroughs in the past months. I'm afraid I won't be able to control myself if I unleash all my power!"

"It's fine. I can handle it!"

Li Jialing said eagerly, "I'm confident in my ultimate form!"

"Fine then!"

Li Yao took a deep breath and closed his eyes in the darkness.

Then, thunderous music burst out from his abdomen, his head, and the air around him at the same time.

"This is…"

Li Jialing gasped hard. "What's this?"

"Music."

Li Yao said matter-of-factly, "Do you not know that my domain is triggered by music? The louder my background music is, the more powerful I will be. Together with the mental attack that I launch with my song as the medium, I'm unstoppable!

"In the past months, I've reviewed the battle with Wuying Qi and realized that cultivating and singing can definitely work together! So, I practiced a few new songs, but nobody was willing to listen to them. Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, and the Fist King ran away whenever I told them that I got a new song. Even Xiaoming and Wenwen have no time! It's great that I still have you, my good brother!

"Are you ready? If you are… Behold my 120% soundwave attack!"

"…"

After a half-second daze, Li Jialing suddenly spewed out blood. "Ouch, it hurts so much! No, Brother Yao, I don't think I can handle it anymore! I think I just suffered internal wounds. I can barely control myself anymore. I'm vomiting blood. I give up!"

"Huh?"

Li Yao was disappointed. "Seriously? I thought that your ultimate transformation should be something better."

"Yes, it should be. Brother Yao, you're right that strength is more important than superficialness. I'm too… weak after all."

Li Jialing said quickly, "It seems that Brother Yao is always Brother Yao however stronger I've become. I'm no match for you at all. I can't take it anymore. Brother Yao, I have to treat my wounds!"

"Oh? Is that so?"

Li Yao was suspicious. "Did I really hurt you so heavily?"

"Of course! The whole training field was almost blown up by you, and you think it was not heavy?"

Li Jialing hurried to run out. "This is just a minor training game. We really shouldn't have torn this place down. I can't carry on anymore. Brother Yao, you're so much stronger than me!"

The young man turned back to what he was as he slipped out not like a lion but more like a weasel.

Five minutes later, in the medical room next to the training field…

Both of them were experienced fighters, so none of their wounds were critical. So, they simply treated each other without asking for a doctor's help. After they were done, they sipped medical drugs on the top of the medical cabins.

Both of them laughed when they saw each other's miserable looks, but they grimaced in pain as their wounds were stretched when they laughed.

"Hey…"

After a while, Li Yao nudged Li Jialing and asked, "The queen said that you had something important to tell. What is it that you're keeping so mysteriously?"

Li Jialing coughed and lowered his head in embarrassment without saying anything.

"Huh?"

Li Yao said, "That's a rare reaction. It must be something special and important. Come on, tell Brother Yao!"

"Brother Yao…"

Looking at the stripes that were vaguely flowing on his palm, Li Jialing made up his mind and said, "After the battle just now, you should see that I'm no longer a boy but a real man. If I make a tough decision as a man, will you support me, Brother Yao?"

"Ohhhhh!"

Li Yao blinked his eyes and grew excited. He patted Li Jialing's back and said, "I completely understand you. Hahahaha! Congratulations! It's indeed very important! What's her name? Whoever she is, Brother Yao will definitely root for you!"

"Huh?"

Stunned, Li Jialing stammered, "S-She? What are you talking about, Brother Yao?"

"The girl who turned you from a boy into a man!"

Li Yao found it odd. "Weren't you going to share it with me? How could you have turned from a boy into a man without a girl? Could it be… Hiss… Are you saying…? Oh, I got it. No wonder you seem so reluctant and hesitant and ask for my support. Well, it's actually fine. I… I totally support you. Really. We're in a different time now, and men and women are the same. This is an age when human beings are about to get rid of their shells, and the communication of minds instead of bodies is more important. Maybe human beings will fall in love with information lives in the future. So, I definitely support you!"

Li Jialing opened his fingers and buried his face in his hands.

"It's my fault. I really shouldn't have put it so subtly."

The young man said helplessly, "Let me just be straightforward. Do you know that Emperor Chengwu, the fake emperor that the four families elected, has been crowned on the Song family's base?"

Chapter 2684: Great Emperor!

The news of Emperor Chengwu's coronation came to the capital three days ago.

Together with the news was a grand ceremony and parade.

Under the fear of fall, the four Kurfürst families had invested whatever they had left and established a new army. Although it was not as astounding as the ace fleets that they had earlier, the new army did look rather powerful on the surface.

This Emperor Chengwu was Emperor Shenwu's cousin. His bloodline was unquestionable, but he was utterly useless and was only the four families' puppet.

The four families wished that they could reunite the Imperium under his name.

They sent the message of the new emperor's coronation and the parade throughout the Imperium to expand their influence.

People in the Imperium generally believed that a country needed a monarch. Even though they knew that Emperor Chengwu was only a puppet and the coronation ceremony was not even held in the capital, the emperor looked sacred and inviolable after he ascended to the throne, making the Immortal Cultivators have second thoughts.

When the Imperium was so chaotic, the authority of an emperor could be a weapon more powerful than a fleet.

"I'm aware of that."

Li Yao nodded his head. "This Emperor Chengwu is nothing but a new puppet."

"A puppet he is, but convention is such a powerful thing. For a thousand years, the people of the Imperium have been taught to be devoted to their emperor. It cannot be changed overnight."

Li Jialing paused for a moment and said, "It's been almost a year since the late emperor's demise. Now, a fake emperor on the four families' side is claiming the crown, but the reformists do not have an emperor to lead them when their slogan at the beginning was to respect the emperor and renew the Imperium. How can the reformists unite the Imperium without an emperor?"

"That's indeed a headache. Hasn't General Lei Chenghu been looking for a new emperor? He even asked me to take part in the inspection several times, but the candidates were hardly satisfactory."

Li Yao was one of the most powerful people in the Imperium. Leaving aside the backup from the Star Glory Federation, he was the legitimate King of Black Wind, had the support of the Arsonist Fleet, and was one of the strongest warriors in the Imperium. He was definitely among the most influential people in the Imperium, and Lei Chenghu was probably the only person who could compete with him. The smaller warlords in the Imperium were not a match for him at all.

Therefore, though Li Yao was not a Kurfürst, he had the power of one. Both Li Linghai and Lei Chenghu had to respect his opinion when they were electing a new emperor.

However, the royal family had been turned into a wasteland by the generations of Kurfürsten who intentionally indulged them. The candidates in the royal family were hardly normal, much less competent enough for the title.

"Like I've told General Lei Chenghu many times, the election of a new emperor is an internal affair of the Imperium, and I shouldn't intervene much as an outsider."

Li Yao sighed and frowned, "However, those candidates are simply too unworthy. Some of them are greedy and ambitious, and some cannot think clearly at all. In short, they cannot contribute to our cause at all!

"Even a puppet should have some wisdom. I really don't care who's going to be the emperor, as long as he understands the simple idea that there are no eternal friends but only eternal interests, so that he can leave aside the differences between the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators for now. However, none of the candidates can meet such a simple requirement!"

Li Jialing smiled. "It seems that you're really angry with all those candidates, right?"

"Angry?"

Li Yao said, "I helped General Lei Chenghu devise a few tests, and those guys all revealed their ambitions. They hated Cultivators so much that they wished they could swallow the Arsonist Fleet and the Star Glory Federation in the next second!

"I'm not saying that the emperor of the Imperium can't be hostile to the Star Ocean Imperium, but you'd better figure out who's a greater threat first, alright? Now, the four families are not destroyed yet, and the Covenant Alliance's hasn't revealed itself, but you're hoping to slaughter all the Cultivators, devour the Arsonist Fleet, and march to the Star Glory Federation? You could ask for a better way of death!

"If they can slightly hold themselves back until we finish off the four families and the Covenant Alliance before they attack the Cultivators, I would think more highly of them. But now, not just me, even General Lei Chenghu is not satisfied with them."

"Yes. General Lei has talked to the queen a lot in the past days about the new emperor. I heard his complaints all the time."

Li Jialing smiled and said, "Actually, General Lei attaches much more importance to the new emperor than you do, Brother Yao, and he's not just electing a new puppet."

"Huh?"

Brightly stunned, Li Yao asked with a smile, "No puppets? Does Lei Chenghu intend to elect a 'sage emperor'?"

"That's General Lei's plan."

Li Jialing nodded and said, "General Lei believes that the decline of the Imperium in the past hundreds of years boils down to the imbalance between the monarch and his subjects, where the monarch was too weak and the subjects were too powerful.

"If the reformists elect a new puppet, would he be any different from the four families' puppet? Where would the reform be?

"Yes, General Lei Chenghu and the queen can ensure that the ideology of the reformists are unchanged with their prestige when they are alive, but General Lei is old and may die within a hundred years, and it remains to be seen if the queen can turn into a spectral Cultivator. They can't protect the puppet forever.

"If one day both General Lei and the queen perish, and the new-generation warlords grow powerful, what can the puppet emperor do? Would the reformists not degenerate into the new-generation four families by then?"

What Li Jialing said made Li Yao grow respectful for Lei Chenghu. "That's quite right. General Lei Chenghu is quite a visionary. Even though we're on different sides, I have to admit that he's a pure soldier who's devoted to his country!

"However, although it's great to have a wise emperor instead of a puppet, how can you find a wise emperor so easily? Hasn't General Lei Chenghu looked into the files on the members of the royal family already? I've read the files myself. Few of them are worthy enough. It's not like we can find several toddlers and groom them into a wise emperor in the coming decades, right? No, by General Lei Chenghu's rigorous standards, I'm afraid that a baby has to be groomed since when he was back in his mother's womb in order to meet his demands. Hahahaha!"

Li Yao cackled, but Li Jialing did not respond. The atmosphere was weird and awkward.

"Wait, why are you talking to me about that?"

Feeling that Li Jialing's face was weird, Li Yao had a mouthful of the strengthening drug and said, "Let the queen and General Lei Chenghu worry about the election of the emperor. What exactly do you want to talk to me and ask for my support?"

"Brother Yao…"

After a long silence, Li Jialing made up his mind and took a deep breath before he said, "If General Lei Chenghu and the queen would like me to be the new emperor of the Imperium, will you support me?"

"Pu!"

Li Yao spewed out the strengthening drug and coughed so hard that his tears were running out. "Cough, cough, cough. You… You've got to be kidding me. Cough, cough, cough. Did my ears just deceive me, or is something wrong with Li Linghai's and Lei Chenghu's heads? You, the new emperor? Your family name is not Wuying at all!"

"That's not important."

Li Jialing said casually, "You wouldn't think that General Lei Chenghu is one of those stubborn men who have to kneel before the Wuying family, right? The Imperium does not belong to any family, and General Lei is loyal to the Imperium, not Wuying Qi or his unworthy offspring.

"Of course, most people of the Imperium can't accept an emperor with a different last name, but it's fine because few people really know me.

"I'm the queen's biological son. The queen, Lei Chenghu, and the King of Black Wind will endorse that I'm the thirteenth son of the late emperor and that I was secretly sent out of the palace to avoid political conflicts. Now, I've returned to the capital after growing up in the Li family. Who can question my legitimacy?"

Chapter 2685: Change the Universe!

"But…"

Li Yao's head was a mess, and his computation ability was blocked, making it impossible for him to say anything.

Never could he have thought that Li Jialing would bring up such a shocking topic!

"Brother Yao, do you want to say that the four families will expose me, or that the royal family can examine my bloodline with advanced technologies?"

Li Jialing smiled and said, "When Li Lingfeng brought me to the Li family, he changed my personal files, and the truth can never be found now that Li Lingfeng is dead. How can the four families find out my real identity?

"Even if they do, so what? It's only natural that they demean the emperor that their enemy elects as 'fake emperor'. I know that the propaganda machines controlled by the reformists are about to attack Emperor Chengwu fiercely. The four families' attack on me will only be the same. Nobody will believe it.

"The bloodline test is even less of a problem. The queen still has her influence. She and General Lei will definitely take charge of the bloodline test, and they can make up whatever result they want. Who will dare to object?

"In the Imperium of True Human Beings, strength is what matters most if you want to be the emperor! Now, General Lei, the queen, Jin Yuyan, and Brother Yao all support me on the reformists' side. Do you think any idiot would jump out and argue that I'm not qualified?"

Looking at Li Jialing's confident face, Li Yao tried to calm himself down. He asked solemnly, "Tell me whose idea it is. Did Lei Chenghu come up with it? Why?"

"Yes, it's indeed General Lei's proposal."

Li Jialing said calmly, "Brother Yao, have you not realized yet what kind of person General Lei is? He's an out-and-out Immortal Cultivator, the most stubborn militarist, and Wuying Qi's greatest devotee. His admiration for Wuying Qi is deep to his bones, and he dreams to restore the glory in Blackstar the Great's age!"

"But Wuying Qi is dead!"

Li Yao said coldly, "Also, what he worships is the Wuying Qi in his imagination, which is nothing like the real Wuying Qi!"

"Yes, General Lei realized that too. That's why he worked with you and disrupted the Next Sun Plan."

Li Jialing said, "However, joining hands with the Cultivators does not mean giving up his faith. He still believes in Blackstar the Great—not the dead one but a new, more perfect emperor who can 100% represent the true path of immortality!

"Do you see? General Lei believes that he can create another 'Blackstar the Great', the most perfect sovereign that rules over mankind! Of course, that is on the premise that this sovereign has got the heritage of the old Wuying Qi so that he can be approved by most Immortal Cultivators including General Lei himself."

"I-I think I'm getting it."

Li Yao murmured, "Lei Chenghu always worshiped Blackstar the Great as a god, but he discovered that the god he believed in was a devil. Other people would've given up their faith under such circumstances, but Lei Chenghu has crazily decided to create a true god and reshape the faith of all the Immortal Cultivators!"

"Yes, it can't be helped. It was really sordid of you to play Wuying Qi's porn videos to the whole Empyreal Terminus Sector, which shook the belief of too many Immortal Cultivators in the true path of immortality. They're even considering taking the Cultivators' side."

Li Jialing said, "Do you really think General Lei, an unyielding Immortal Cultivator, will watch the Immortal Cultivators change sides without doing anything?

"While he would never admit that the devil who initiated the Next Sun Plan was Wuying Qi, it's not enough. He has to introduce a charismatic new emperor who's directly related to Wuying Qi so that the collapsing belief of all the Immortal Cultivators can be recovered."

"Got it. Now, Blackstar the Great has two direct heirs in the Imperium, which are you who got 2.0 and me who got 3.0!"

Li Yao slapped his thigh and said, "Damn it, but it doesn't mean that he has to go to you, right? You're too young for such complicated power struggles. Why didn't he come to me? I can also… Well, I don't think I'm suitable to be the emperor of the Imperium, right?"

"I'm afraid not…"

Li Jialing nodded slowly. "General Lei is not the only one who needs a healthy, vigorous new emperor who boasts Wuying Qi's heritage and infinite potential. The young officers in the army also need such an emperor, a person that they can understand and who can renew the Imperium. I'm the best candidate for that. Don't you think so, Brother Yao?"

"I…"

Li Yao was briefly lost for words.

Though his head went blank after Li Jialing's proposal, the more he thought about it, the more he realized that Li Jialing was the perfect choice.

Although the reformists won the Battle of Seven Seas and the battle in the capital, Wuying Qi, as a traitor among the reformists, still caused huge losses.

The reformists were now subtly changing from Li Linghai's age to Lei Chenghu's age, and the Cultivators, led by Li Yao, had inevitably added to the complexity of the situation despite their help to the reformists.

At a moment like this, it was hard to find a new emperor that everybody was satisfied with, and the reformists might fall apart with a bad choice.

However, ignoring his bloodline, Li Jialing was a rarely-seen candidate for the emperor that everybody could be satisfied with!

For Lei Chenghu, a crazy admirer of Blackstar the Great, it was his lifelong dream to groom a new-generation Blackstar the Great that was more perfect.

The imperial guards who were loyal to Wuying Qi wouldn't object to the leadership of Wuying Qi's heir either.

For Li Linghai, as well as Li Linghai's subordinates who had been fretting that they might be kicked away, it would be most reassuring if Li Linghai's son became the emperor and Li Linghai turned from the queen to the empress dowager. The power succession of the reformists would be in order!

As for the Cultivators including Li Yao, it went without saying that nobody would be a better emperor than Li Jialing!

However… However…

"What about it?"

Li Jialing stared at Li Yao. "Will you support me, Brother Yao?"

"From the perspective of returns, I will definitely support you. You would be so much better than those unworthy candidates as the emperor!"

Li Yao tried to hold it back, but he failed to. "However, as your big brother, I have to remind you that it wouldn't feel great when you sit on the throne! I've never felt that you're a man who's passionate about power. Didn't you always crave for a free, unrestrained life? Let's leave aside General Lei and the queen's attitudes and focus on you. What exactly do you think? Do you want to be the emperor?"

Li Jialing's eyelashes shivered, and he lowered his head.

In the next second, his lips curved as he smiled from the bottom of his heart.

"Thank you, Brother Yao."

His eyes glittering, Li Jialing said sincerely, "I know that you care about me from the bottom of your heart, and you want the best for me. You do not want me to shoulder the responsibility that might be too heavy for anyone to bear.

"However, like I told you just now, I've turned from a boy into a man, and a man has to shoulder responsibilities that he is expected to!

"It's true that I craved for freedom in the past, but do you know why? Because I had nothing back then. What else could a man who had nothing pursue except freedom?

"But things are different now. Now, I have a lot of new things. I have friends, I have family, I have you, Sister Long, my mother, and so many other things that I do not want to lose and deserve to be fought for. How can I watch you fight, bleed, and sacrifice while I pursue freedom?

"It was indeed General Lei who proposed the idea that I became the next emperor, but before that, I had been thinking really hard how I could protect my family, my friends, and my home, and how I could fight as bravely as you!

"I couldn't have made the decision without your teaching. Do you remember what you said to me deep inside the Great Iron Factory? 'With greater abilities come greater responsibilities'. I've grown so much in the past year that I never thought that I could be as strong as I am today. However proud I am, I dare not say that it's all my own achievement.

"My power was not exclusively cultivated by myself, but more like endowed to me by fate. I cannot waste the power. I must use it to find and carry out my own beliefs. I will use it to change the whole universe!"

Chapter 2686: Wuying Ling!

The young man roared desperately, and two purple flames darted out of his hands and hovered around his arms like two purple dragons before they went all the way along his veins to his head where they turned into a crown.

At that moment, a vague halo appeared behind the young man, and he seemed to be a combination of the Blood God, Wuying Qi, and Li Jialing. It was impossible to distinguish them anymore.

However, he became the young lion that Li Yao was very familiar with again in the next second. No, perhaps he was not so familiar with the young lion at all…

"You're very dangerous, Jialing."

Scorched by the young man's power, Li Yao couldn't help but ask, "It's great that you want to do something with your power, but I fear that you might be lost in the power. Do you know that you look like another Wuying Qi just now? It's too dangerous! I can't even tell whether or not you're still haunted by Wuying Qi."

"Yes, it's truly dangerous. Every time when the purple electric arcs and the golden flames flowed among my cells, a voice that sounded like Wuying Qi 2.0 would whisper to my ears, making me wonder whether I'm 100% Li Jialing or some other things."

The young man smiled at the flames and electric arcs in his hand for a while before he slowly retracted them and looked back at Li Yao. "What about you, Brother Yao? Are you not as dangerous as the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid who has absorbed Wuying Qi 3.0's power? Are you not scared that you would go crazy and lose your mind when you sit on the thorny throne as the sole balancer between the Imperium and the federation, that you would not turn into another vicious dragon with black scales and bloody wings? If you know that it was dangerous, why did you accept Wuying Qi 3.0's heritage?"

"Because I didn't have a choice!"

Li Yao said solemnly, "Nobody except me can be the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. I do not want anyone else to take the risks of turning into a vicious dragon, particularly not you!"

"Well said. That's also my answer. Just like you, I don't have a choice."

Li Jialing said fast, "First of all, the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators are in a subtle relationship. You said yourself that every candidate for the new emperor was eager to swallow the Cultivators and the federation. Even General Lei Chenghu wouldn't work with you forever, would he?

"No. We're putting our disagreement aside only to deal with the four families and the Covenant Alliance. But one day, when our mutual enemies are destroyed, will the Imperium and the federation start a war?

"No emperors can hold back their resentment at the Cultivators and the federation except me. Only I can treat the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators equally, suppress the eagles who intend to annex the federation, and bring real peace to the Imperium and the federation!

"Also, haven't you been trying to reform the Imperium in the Cultivators' way? I know that you're adopting a bottom-top approach by gradually changing the general public.

"But it's impossible! The Imperium is too big and chaotic, and the true path of immortality has been spread for a thousand years. It's hardly possible to change social customs! Your reform is bound to be caught in trouble. It may even raise friction between the Imperium and the federation!

"The Imperium today cannot bear a bottom-top reform. We have to change the obsolete architecture from the top with new power and go down to the bottom. That's the only way!
