"The queen and General Lei Chenghu are naturally real reformists. On one hand, they're too old to lead the reformists until the completion of their cause, and on the other hand, they're too bound by the ideas of the Immortal Cultivators, and their reforms are too trivial.

"I'm not overestimating myself. I do not think I'm better than the queen, General Lei, you, or Sister Long. No, I'm not nearly as good as any of you right now, but none of you can be the emperor of the Imperium! On the other hand, I'm far better than any of the emperor candidates!

"If any of those pieces of garbage becomes the emperor of the Imperium, what do you think they will do? How will they treat the Cultivators and the federation? How will they face you as the deterrent in the Gold Crystal Pyramid? Will you carry on the reforms instead of being swallowed by their great ambitions?

"No, they won't. You know very well the catastrophic consequences if they become the emperor. It's why you and General Lei have never selected one of them yet!

"So, I cannot watch those pieces of garbage become the emperor of the Imperium only to ruin the Imperium, the federation, and everything I've finally got.

"Lastly, there's a small but not unimportant reason. The queen, controlled by Wuying Qi, almost caused a disaster in the Next Sun Plan. Although she was stopped by Brother Yao in time, severe losses were caused, and too many people lost their lives.

"The queen's crime is certainly unforgivable, but those who died because of her cannot be brought back to life even if she's cut into pieces.

"So, as her son, I should answer for her crime and atone for it every day for the rest of my life!"

Looking at the young man who was blushing in excitement, Li Yao was at a loss again.

Or rather, he was about to be melted by the young man's passion.

"Have you really thought it through?"

Li Yao asked, "Is it your final decision?"

"Yes."

Li Jialing declared resolutely, "I've made my decision. Nothing can change my mind!"

"Then, it seems that…"

Li Yao smiled. "I don't have a choice except to support you, right?"

Li Jialing also smiled. "If you don't support me, Brother Yao, I'll have to cling to your thigh and beg for your support. Though many people are behind my back at this moment, what I need most is your support!

"Remember the other thing you said to me in the Great Iron Factory? You said that even a tiny butterfly might raise a destructive tornado with a flicker of its wings. Come to think of it, maybe I'm the tornado that you've already raised. Now that you've started a tornado, how can you make it die down quickly?"

"Sounds fair."

Li Yao thought for a moment and looked at Li Jialing. "Then, answer my last question. What are you right now? Are you a Cultivator or an Immortal Cultivator?"

"I don't know. I've been bullied by the Immortal Cultivators since I was born, and Li Lingfeng and Wuying Lan built me into a monster. I'm never fond of the true path of immortality."

Li Jialing said, "However, about the path of the Cultivators, no matter how you sell it to me, I feel that it's still too unrealistic and impractical and requires too many lucky accidents.

"I remember what we talked back in the emperor's mausoleum, about how it would be best if we could combine the ideas of the Cultivators and those of the Immortal Cultivators. Of course, I haven't quite figured out the details yet. If I'm lucky enough to be an emperor for several decades, I think I'll probably be able to find an answer."

"There was a guy named Lu Qingchen in the federation who had a similar idea. He believed that both the ideas of the Cultivators and those of the Immortal Cultivators were outdated and had to be revised."

Li Yao said, "I think you have a lot in common."

"Oh? Is that so?"

Li Jialing said in delight, "I didn't know that there were such wise men in the federation. Where is he right now? I think it's necessary that we invite him to the Imperium for our cause!"

"Well, I think it would be tricky."

Li Yao rubbed his nose and said, "This man has already been killed, though not entirely. Part of his soul escaped, but I don't know where he is right now. I'm afraid that he can't help your cause in the Imperium anymore."

Li Jialing and Li Yao stared at each other for a long time before they burst into laughter at the same time.

"What a shame."

Li Jialing said, "He could've given me new perspectives!"

"It's indeed a shame."

Li Yao sighed. "Come to think of it, the ideas of many people, including Lu Qingchen's, had their shining parts, but why did we have to fight instead of discussing in peace to find out the best solution? They had to throw themselves at me and get themselves killed, making me feel upset and guilty. Why bother?"

"Aren't we discussing in peace right now?"

Li Jialing said, "Let's hope that we can learn from the past and do not make the same mistake while we look for the most suitable path in the sea of stars!"

Li Yao stared at the young man for a long time again.

"Something else suddenly occurred to me."

Li Yao said, "When you become the emperor, I don't think you can use the name Li Jialing anymore, can you?"

By saying that, he was expressing his support for Li Jialing.

"I'm Li Jialing, and I'll always be Li Linghai's son and Li Yao's brother."

The young man was not delighted at all after receiving Li Yao's support. Instead, he hesitated for a moment, as if he were going to cut himself loose from his past life and embark on a dangerous expedition.

However, after a brief struggle, golden flames were ignited in his eyes, burning up all his hesitation.

"But on the surface, I will be using a different name. I have to 'return' to the Wuying family!"

The young man rose and opened the door of the medical room. Allowing the scorching sunlight to don him with a burning armor, he looked back at Li Yao with the most brilliant smile. "From today onward, I will be called 'Wuying Ling'!"

Chapter 2687: Spring of the Imperium 28 days ago

Spring had arrived in the Imperium.

It arrived earlier to the underground world of the capital planet that had never witnessed spring than it did to the surface.

Xu Zhicheng, a former distinguished miner and a leader of Nepenthe, stood before the Great Iron Factory that he found both familiar and strange, unable to believe that the place where he was born and brought up could turn into such a paradise!

The suffocating factory had been added with infinite automatic assembly lines, and the purification and fashioning facilities here were state-of-the-art even in the whole Imperium. More than 85% of the workers did not have to waste their lives for the dangerous jobs anymore.

But those workers were not fired. After training, they became new construction workers and created new living spaces by excavating the rocks around the Great Iron Factory. Beautiful towns and industrial parks were established, transforming the dark slave factory into a model underground city of the Imperium.

Even though Xu Zhicheng had personally witnessed all the beautiful changes, he still found it hard to believe his eyes when the whole underground city was celebrating the opening of the brand-new industrial park in the southern district.

It was like a dream that had been going on for half a year and would still continue!

When Li Yao, Long Yangjun, Li Jialing, and Xiaoming and Wenwen left earlier, they were ready to fight for ten years, twenty years, and even a century. Their ancestors had struggled in darkness for ten thousand years. Why couldn't they wait for another hundred years?

They did not expect the victory to come so fast. After only half a year, they were told that Li Yao secured a triumph on the surface and was about to win over the whole Imperium!

At first, everybody was suspicious.

It was understandable. They had been tormented for too long, and their previous resistances all ended up in destruction. They dare not ask for good news because they did not want to be disappointed.

However, the precious resources in cabinets shipped from the surface made them realize that the victory was real.

When teams of cold, iron-boned spiritual puppets came to the underground town, they panicked for a moment, thinking that the machines were ordered to slaughter them.

However, the spiritual puppets were holding not swords but spades. They were not here to kill, but to help repair the shelters, expand the towns, and establish roads among the towns!

Ever since then, the lives of the underground people got better and better.

Xu Zhicheng, as the leader of the Nepenthe believers and Li Yao's old friend, naturally took the responsibility of leading the renewal of the underground world.

Through Xu Zhicheng, Li Yao hired a bunch of healthy men as the guards of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Since the Gold Crystal Pyramid was a demilitarized area, neither the Arsonist Fleet nor the Astounding Thunder Fleet was stationed in the area, and the underground people were the best guards.

It was not just a job for the underground people, but also an eye-opener for the excellent young men from the underground world, who would soon return to their hometown and bring more light to their compatriots.

As for the other Nepenthe believers, they returned to the Great Iron Factory and devoted themselves to reconstruction works. According to the plan, they would work with Xiaoming and Wenwen, as well as their puppets, to build the Great Iron Factory into a beautiful, futuristic city!

It was not just part of the reform in general, but also a powerful weapon.

The modification of the Great Iron Factory was made into videos and sent to the areas that were still under the control of the four families, showing the hominoids what the future Imperium would look like and what good lives they could live!

Now, while part of the Nepenthe believers took part in the construction of the city, other Nepenthe believers were building roads among the shelters and the underground cities with their mining skills. Then, as census enumerators, they helped the Imperium get the data of the underground people who had always been ignored so that those people's lives could be improved with aid. As a result, more and more underground people were joining the work to make their lives better.

There was no shortage of resources for now.

The four families had plenty of industries in the Empyreal Terminus Sector, and their ace fleets had carried a lot of resources during the invasion. Those were now the reformists' trophies.

The sailing route between the capital and the Seven Seas Space Zone was dredged again, allowing resources to be shipped to support the reconstruction.

The only problem was how to distribute the resources from the ground into the hands of those who were really in need.

Xiaoming and Wenwen, as information lives, made great contributions to that. They were building a new, unbelievable world of information below the ground.

On the surface, it was impossible for Xiaoming and Wenwen to disrupt the reign of the Immortal Cultivators completely, and they had to be careful all the time.

But the underground people, who could barely survive, certainly would not care whether or not the crystal processors would control human beings someday. They could do whatever they wanted to survive!

Faced with unseen technologies, the less developed areas were more likely to absorb and apply them than the more developed areas were. It was not unusual in the history of mankind.

It was exactly the case for the people living below the ground of the capital planet. They had skipped the age of the magic equipment industry and jumped into the brand-new age of ultra information!

They became accustomed to Xiaoming and Wenwen very quickly, and they were used to working with iron puppets. Even the toddlers were following the puppets and learning how to control them.

Watching the innocent kids play with the metal pets in the beautiful gardens, Xu Zhicheng felt that his heart was melting in the intoxicating spring breeze.

He felt that he was dozens of years younger and back to the days when his wife and his children were alive.

The underground people were no fools. Xu Zhicheng, as a leader of Nepenthe, knew the concerns for the development of crystal processor technologies.

In the past, they had shuddered in fear when faced with the cold and ruthless slaughter machines and gnashed their teeth, hoping to smash all those iron puppets into smithereens.

However, the spiritual puppets were only as evil as those who controlled them.

Even if the underground people were reigned by crystal processors someday, so what? Would it be any worse than the darkness in the past ten thousand years?

"Those from the new-generation who grow up with crystal processors and spiritual puppets will definitely be different from the people on the ground after they grow up."

Xu Zhicheng thought to himself. "But so what? We're never the same as the people on the ground. The Immortal Cultivators despise us and do not even treat us as their own kind, so why don't we consider the cold machines as our compatriots? After all, in the eyes of the Immortal Cultivators, we're just machines that have body temperatures!"

"Uncle Xu! Uncle Xu!"

While Xu Zhicheng was thinking, a girl hopped at him on the top of a red lizard.

Xu Zhicheng recognized her as Bing Bing, formerly a resident of the 10,084th shelter and now a messenger of the underground towns who would deliver them things that could not be delivered by the Spiritual Nexus.

"Bing Bing."

Xu Zhicheng grinned and stopped Bing Bing's red lizard. "What have you brought to me this time?"

"It's an invitation! It's an invitation to the coronation ceremony!"

Bing Bing blushed in excitement and could barely catch her breath. She said, "We're going to the royal palace, Uncle Xu. Do you hear me? We're going to the royal palace!"

"A new emperor is to be crowned?"

Xu Zhicheng was not as excited as the girl. He was not interested in who would become the head of the Immortal Cultivators. After all, they were all just as wicked.

However, it was still a surprise for him that even hominoids like them would be qualified to attend the coronation. Time had truly changed.

"Yes, exactly!"

Bing Bing nodded quickly and asked, her eyes glowing, "Uncle Xu, do you know who the new emperor is?"

"How would I know?"

Xu Zhicheng was amused. "We're just underground wildlings. How can we speculate who the new emperor will be?"

"No, it's been announced. I'm sure it will be all over the Spiritual Nexus soon. But I've received the message sooner with the Black Wind King's personal invitation card!"

Bing Bing almost jumped to her feet. "The new emperor is Brother Jialing, whom we fought alongside earlier! Oh, Li Jialing is his fake name. His real name turns out to be Wuying Ling. He's Emperor Shenwu's thirteenth son and the most loved one. However, he was sent out of the palace to avoid the hunting of the four families, and he only met us by accident."

"What?"

Xu Zhicheng was dumbfounded for a moment.

Chapter 2688: Coronation! 28 days ago

Seven days later, it was a sunny and cloudless day.

Though they had been on the ground for a week and were no longer surprised at the blue sky or the scorching sun, the representatives of the underground people, including Xu Zhicheng and Bing Bing, were still overwhelmed by the boundless square that had been jam-packed with people.

The huge square that boasted infinite space was something that they could never imagine below the ground.

All the underground representatives felt that they had been reborn into a whole new world.

Other than the spaciousness, they were also amazed by the millions of people here.

The place was spacious enough, but there seemed to be more people in the capital than the capacity of any place. The streets, the towers, the square, and the auditorium that was floating with anti-gravity units had all been crowded. Even the sky was occupied by warships!

The delegation of the underground people walked for more than three hours from dawn to morning before they finally found their designated location. They were like a tiny spot that was embedded into an array of infinite spots.

Standing on tiptoe, they saw that the ocean of people stretched all the way to the horizon.

Glamorously-decorated loudspeakers were floating in the sky and roaring, now playing exciting songs and now announcing new orders and policies. Their noises crashed each other like torrents, making it impossible for the underground people to know what was going on at all. They only felt that they were dizzy.

Xu Zhicheng craned his head to look at the royal palace, but he could not see the new emperor on the high platform at all. So, he simply browsed through the few fliers and leaflets in his hands.

Though the news of the new emperor's coronation had been spread throughout the Spiritual Nexus, some places, such as the remote resource planets or the territory of the four families, might not receive the message in time. The fliers were officially printed to make up for that so that the participants of the ceremony could disseminate the message with the fliers to every corner of the Imperium.

The fliers included an introduction to the new emperor and Emperor Shenwu's remark on him, which said that his son took after him and would certainly carry on his cause.

The emperors of the Imperium of True Human Beings were different from those of ancient times anyway.

The succession of the emperors in ancient times did not need to care about social influence. All they needed to do was to win the approval of the nobles and the powerful warlords.

However, the Imperium of True Human Beings was a modern country anyway, and the emperor was a representative of the country instead of a simple dictator.

In particular, the new emperor elected by the reformists had to compete with "Emperor Chengwu", which was selected by the four families. Since Emperor Shenwu had been dead for a year, neither of the two new emperors seemed legitimate enough, so it was all the more reason to beautify this new emperor.

So, these luxurious fliers introduced the life of the new emperor, claiming that he was the late emperor's thirteenth son who had been raised in another family because his father feared that he would be set up. Bing Bing had told Xu Zhicheng that earlier.

Xu Zhicheng was not at all interested in the fliers that were official and hence boring.

What he carefully studied was the leaflets that were of a much lousier quality.

The leaflets were also introductions to the new emperor, but they were much more exciting. There was no telling who published or spread them, but almost every participant of the ceremony was holding one.

According to the leaflets, the late emperor once fell asleep when he was too tired from working before his demise, and he saw a golden lion with wings flying into his palace and circling around him. There was even a glittering star on the forehead of this lion!

The black-starred lion gazed at him first, before it leaped out to the back of the palace!

The late emperor woke up from his dream and got the news that the queen had given birth to his thirteenth son.

Because of the unusual dream, the late emperor paid special attention to the thirteenth prince and secretly decided to let him inherit the crown.

However, the spies that were all over the royal palace somehow spread out the unusual dream, and the four Kurfursten certainly wouldn't allow the prince who was the reincarnation of the black-stared lion to grow strong. They decided to murder this thirteenth prince.

But fortunately for the Imperium of True Human Beings, one of the assassins found his conscience. He secretly replaced the thirteenth prince with another baby and carried him out of the royal palace into the Li family boldly!

After that, the thirteenth prince gradually grew up, and he realized his bloodline and his identity at a critical moment. Under the support of his most loyal subjects—the Black Wind King and Dongfang Mingyue—he left the Li family for the Seven Seas Space Zone, before he marched back to the capital, vanquished the four families, and secured a glorious victory as well as the crown that belonged to him!

Xu Zhicheng had no idea who wrote such a leaflet, whose language was simply bordering on vulgar and whose content was hard to believe. However, it was indeed exciting to read the leaflet. Everybody would feel that the new emperor was a young hero who was destined to lead them after reading it!

The leaflets, naturally, were not official.

But Xu Zhicheng was not a fool. If the authorities really disagreed with it, they would've censored those leaflets, yet the fact was that almost every participant of the ceremony was holding onto one of the leaflets, which showed the authorities' indirect support.

While Xu Zhicheng was considering the matter, the noises above his head turned ten times louder. He raised his head, only to see that the blue sky had turned into a golden ocean as thousands of starships had arrived over the capital.

Thousands of starships would be a grain of sand in space.

But within the atmosphere, when they were only several hundred meters from the ground, their overwhelming exhaust flames seemed to be tearing everything apart!

The sun was completely eclipsed by the fire from the starships. When the sunlight passed through the gravity fields of the starships, it turned into colorful fragments that added to the magnificence of the ceremony, as if it were fireworks for the coronation of the new emperor.

"Ah…"

Xu Zhicheng could not breathe anymore. He cheered with the millions of other people around him.

As an underground rebel, he was never proud of his identity as a member of the Imperium, but when he saw the grand scene of thousands of starships, he suddenly had an uncanny urge.

If the new emperor was really the young man he knew, with the support of Li Yao and Dongfang Mingyue, the underground people might really have a chance to melt into this unparalleled power and become part of the Imperium, right?

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The unexpected shots of cannons nearly made Xu Zhicheng jump to his feet.

Thankfully, he remembered what he was repetitively taught a few days previously. It was said that cannons would be fired during the coronation.

As he expected, the deafening explosions did not cause any damage. The cannons from the thousands of starships launched the most dazzling flames of light, paving a golden path from the sky to the ground, as if they were delivering a certain power from the universe to the high platform before the royal palace!

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Thousands of light beams were unfolded in the sky, displaying the picture where the new emperor was slowly setting foot on the high platform so that the participants of the ceremony and all the people in the Imperium could see him clearly.

The new emperor turned out to be a tall and vigorous young man. He was wearing golden formal clothes that extended to a dozen meters away. The gold scales stitched to his clothes made him look like a golden dragon that was slowly stretching out. Holding the sword that represented royal power, he slowly stepped on the platform.

His black hair, mixed with some golden strands, was dancing as if it were alive. Deep in his eyes, golden light and purple light were fighting with sparks bursting out. After his every step, a footprint was left where he set his foot on. He looked much more majestic than Emperor Chengwu that was elected by the four families. Many people who learned of his background from the fliers and the leaflets were growing more and more respectful of him, vaguely realizing that this new emperor was different from any of the puppets in the past hundreds of years!

Chapter 2689: The Emperor's Order 27 days ago

"Wow!"

In the delegation of the underground people, Bing Bing and the other young people who met Li Jialing before exclaimed, "Is he our new emperor? Why does he look completely different from the past?"

The new-generation underground people were completely fascinated by the charm of Li Jialing, or Wuying Ling.

Xu Zhicheng put on a bitter smile, as those little guys had acknowledged a new emperor when he had taught them to be independent and unyielding for such a long time.

However, it was not entirely their fault. Even Xu Zhicheng himself could barely control himself in the passionate atmosphere.

At the center of attention, the new emperor finally stepped on the platform.

On the platform, several officials and members of the royal family in formal clothes had been waiting respectfully, with the seal that allegedly had been passed on from the Star Ocean Imperium.

Having been taught with the procedure of the ceremony many times, Xu Zhicheng knew that the officials had to make a long and tedious announcement and put the crown that represented royal power on the head of the new emperor before the members of the royal family gave the seal that represented the country to the new emperor.

However, this new emperor did something unexpected at this point.

One of the officials coughed and was about to start his speech, and Xu Zhicheng was ready to be bored for the next hour, when the new emperor stepped forward, grabbed the crown from the officials, and put it on his own head!

To echo with the rumor that he was the reincarnation of the black-starred lion, the newly-made crown was like a lion that was opening its bloody mouth. There were also two dragons on its two sides that were holding a glittering pearl, making the new emperor seem more domineering than ever!

For a moment, the whole royal palace was silent. Everybody was too shocked by the new emperor's move to say anything.

The new emperor, however, did not stop there. While everybody was dumbfounded, he grabbed the seal from the members of the royal family and pressed it on the first decree that he was about to issue, skipping all the redundant formalities and sending his voice to the whole Imperium!

As he spoke in a deafening voice, glittering words popped up in the sky, allowing everybody to see it clearly. They were even more shocked than before!

"This… This… This is…"

Hearing the new emperor's voice and reading the rolling words, Xu Zhicheng found it hard to believe his ears and his eyes.

The new emperor had brought him more shock than the real sun did when he saw it for the first time!

"What do you mean?"

Bing Bing and the other young people from the underground, however, did not understand why the new emperor seemed so tough at the coronation ceremony. They asked curiously, "Uncle Xu, what is the emperor talking about? Why does everyone seem so excited? Isn't it a good thing that the emperor is pardoning criminals? I'm told that all the emperors did the same thing when they were crowned. Is it any different this time?"

"Of course it is."

Holding back his dizziness, Xu Zhicheng explained to the young people from the underground patiently, "It's true that every new emperor pardoned criminals when they ascended the throne, but they never released those who might jeopardize their reign.

"However, the new emperor is mainly pardoning three kinds of people this time.

"Firstly, the Immortal Cultivators within the Imperium, whom the new emperor said to be 'terrible but understandable'. Many of them took the side of the Cultivators because of the exploitation of the four families. In the Battle of Seven Seas and the battle of the capital, they proved their loyalty to the Imperium. So, the new emperor has given the clemency so that they can serve their country again.

"Secondly, the space pirates led by Boss Bai. Those space pirates were also forced to the wrong path by the four families. Now that a new emperor has claimed the throne, the Imperium is a place of fairness again. Those people can totally change themselves into loyal warriors of the Imperium. Why can't they be pardoned?

"Thirdly, the soldiers of the four families' ace fleets who surrendered in the battle of the capital. The new emperor said that those soldiers were deceived and threatened to attack the capital by the four illegal Kurfursten. However, since he prevented the capital from being sabotaged under the support of the Black Wind King and Lei Chenghu, he has decided to give the soldiers another chance to atone for themselves by attacking the four families!"

"Wow…"

The young people, including Bing Bing, did not entirely understand it, but it sounded quite awesome.

"Not just that. The pardon order was astounding enough, but the abolition order next is even more so!"

Xu Zhicheng said in a low voice, "You should know that the slavery system is kept in many Sectors of the Imperium. Those who unfortunately became slaves have been as miserable as us. However, the new emperor has announced that the slaves under the control of the four families are all freed by him! Even more, if they stand up against their former owners, they can even get parts of their owners' properties and become citizens of the Imperium!"

"Ah!"

The underground people, including Bing Bing, said in delight, "That's… That's fantastic. In such a way, the slaves under the control of the four families will definitely take our side and help us fight their masters!"

Xu Zhicheng nodded his head first, but he put on a bitter smile again.

The slavery system had been going on in the Imperium for a thousand years. How could it be abolished by a simple royal decree?

Also, not just the four families, but the Immortal Cultivators who claimed to be reformists mostly had some slaves in their houses.

The warlords who took the reformists' side in the end, in particular, might even have thousands of slaves in their far-flung territories.

If the slaves controlled by the four Kurfursten families were freed, what about the slaves controlled by the reformists?

It was definitely a critical matter, and the foundation of the reformists would be shaken if it was not dealt with properly.

That was why even Wuying Qi had never thought to strike the four families when he held the power of the reformists, because the reformists would've suffered just as much.

The abolition order released by the new emperor did not specify the arrangements for the slaves outside of the territory of the four families. Although it did not force all the Immortal Cultivators to liberate their slaves, it did mention that if anyone 'volunteered' to liberate their slaves, they would receive abundant compensation, and that the earlier they did that, the more they would receive.

Basically, the compensation was slightly higher than the value that a slave could create in ten years. However, there was only one minor problem, which was that the compensation would be given as bonds that could not be redeemed until the four families were taken care of.

Obviously, the new emperor was going to refill the national treasury and carry out the new policies including abolition with the fat of the four families.

So, after the pardon order and the abolition order, it was the shocking mobilization order against rebels!

After the coronation ceremony, the Astounding Thunder Fleet, the Arsonist Fleet, and the former four families' ace fleets would march to the territory of the four families unstoppably and eliminate the illegal Kurfursten as well as the illegal emperor to restore the glory of the Imperium!

"I… really didn't see this coming!"

Looking at the spectacular image of the new emperor in the sky, Xu Zhicheng had a lot of complicated feelings. He did not know that the young man whom he fought with would grow to such an extent one day.

Whether the orders were the young man's own intention or the intention of Li Yao, Lei Chenghu, and other people behind him, they had at least brought new hope to the Imperium!

The only problem was, did they really have enough capabilities to carry out their reforms?

It was not only Xu Zhicheng's question but also the question of the millions of Immortal Cultivators in the ceremony.

The new emperor had brought them more shock than any previous emperors did.

The pardon order, the abolition order, and the mobilization order would lead to paradigm-shifting reforms and painful changes to their lives.

All the Immortal Cultivators were hesitating and wavering, wondering what they should do.

Some of them were even secretly complaining. Is this little emperor crazy? Does he really think he is a powerful emperor when he's actually a puppet? Reconciliation with Cultivators, abolition, and attacking the four families so eagerly? Why?

The Immortal Cultivators' suspicion was understandable.

After all, the new emperor was barely heard of until then. Nobody knew anything about him except the information on the fliers and leaflets that Li Yao and Long Yangjun wrote.

However, in the next second, they all learned it!

Chapter 2690: The Least Bad Ending (End of Volume VI: Imperium) 27 days ago

As the last word on the decree was frozen into a gold mark in midair, the new emperor suddenly drew his royal sword and pointed it at the sky.

Unparalleled power surged out of him in visible gold tides that swept across the whole royal palace above everyone's head!

They could hear the roar of the new emperor and sense his overwhelming ambitions!

The Immortal Cultivators who knew nothing about the new emperor shuddered palely.

They had thought that the new emperor was only a puppet of the reformists and that it was Lei Chenghu and Li Yao who actually held power.

Little did they know that the new emperor boasted such appalling strength!

A new emperor with such remarkable abilities could not be a simple puppet!

Before they exclaimed, an even more unbelievable scene had shown up!

The new emperor's rising power attracted natural spiritual energy and affected clouds, gathering the clouds above him into a golden beast that looked like a half-lion and half-dragon.

The lion-dragon made of golden clouds, like the representation of the new emperor's will, stared at the ground from the sky coldly!

A moment later, the clouds slightly dispersed, as if the lion-dragon was opening its mouth, and a Colossus with mirror armor all over its body slowly descended from the hole.

It was exactly Blackhole's Heart!

Blackhole's Heart, known as the most powerful Colossus of the Imperium, boasted the most advanced interference force fields and stealth techniques. It had twisted the rays of light around it and covered itself with the most spectacular colors.

The sky had turned into a stage for Blackhole's Heart.

"This is—"

Many senior Immortal Cultivators gasped hard. They vaguely guessed the Colossus' identity from its vibe.

Before the ceremony, they were suspicious about Li Yao's leaflets and would not believe that the new emperor was the reincarnation of the black-starred lion. However, after the new emperor unleashed Dragon Over The Sky and Blackhole's Heart, they had to admit that the new emperor had indeed got Blackstar the Great's heritage since Blackhole's Heart, which had been gone for a thousand years, had reappeared!

Boom!

Boom!

Another two deafening explosions burst out from the southeast and northwest of the sky, and two clouds of abundant spiritual energy floated to the new emperor.

One of the clouds was gathered into a black tiger, whereas the other took the shape of a bloody vulture.

They represented Lei Chenghu and Li Yao, the two most powerful men in the capital and the greatest supporters of the new emperor!

The royal palace was enshrouded by the clouds of the lion-dragon, the black tiger, and the bloody vulture, implying that the iron triangle of Li Yao, Lei Chenghu, and Li Jialing would suppress the whole Imperium!

"Long live the emperor!"

"Long live the emperor! Long live the Imperium!"

Strength mattered most for the Immortal Cultivators. They might've secretly complained when they heard the radical pardon, abolition, and mobilization orders, but faced with the pressure of the iron triangle, none of them could put up any resistance. They could only cheer beyond their control.

After a while, the whole royal palace was filled with roars. Everybody was cheering for their new emperor and the new era name.

Starting from this moment, the emperor stepped into a new phase, and the era name was changed into Yongjia!

…

At night, the whole capital was ablaze with the lights of celebration.

Li Yao, however, was not interested in the celebration of the Immortal Cultivators. He simply stared at the lights from a high tower in the royal palace with a glass of juice in his hands.

"Why are you here?"

One of the spiritual puppets, which had been cleaning behind him, suddenly said in Xiaoming and Wenwen's voices, "Dad, you don't seem very happy. Are you dissatisfied with such an ending?"

"I wouldn't say… dissatisfied."

Li Yao was used to Xiaoming and Wenwen's unannounced visit. He could even accept the weirdly-shaped spiritual puppets calling him dad now. Sipping the juice, he mumbled, "You know what? Just now, I slipped into Li Jialing's palace and enjoyed the special toilet for His Majesty of the Imperium of True Human Beings. That was definitely one luxurious toilet.

"All I'm saying is that I had foreseen countless worse endings before I came to the Imperium, like the destruction of the federation or the end of the world. However, little did I expect that I could use the toilet of the emperor of the Imperium of True Human Beings so blatantly! How can I be dissatisfied? It's just that…"

"It's just that Dad feels that it's not the best ending."

This time, it was Wenwen's voice. "In your plan, you should defeat all your enemies in a glorious battle, awaken all the bottom-level people in the Imperium, and convince the Immortal Cultivators of taking the side of the Cultivators. It will be best if the Imperium joins the federation as part of the federation. However, as it turns out, you have to manipulate an Immortal Cultivator emperor as a Cultivator for your purposes, and this emperor might be unwilling to be manipulated by you forever!"

"Well…"

Li Yao scratched his head. "Now that you put it that way, my plan does sound unrealistic. I don't know. Maybe I'm just worried that the incomplete victory of this battle will lead to greater problems in the future.

"I'm worried about the relationship between the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators and between the federation and the Imperium. I'm worried that Wuying Qi and the Blood God will return and turn me or Li Jialing into a vicious dragon. I'm worried that the opening of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum will be catastrophic. I'm worried that my ultimate identity and the mysterious Earth are related to the Flood. I'm even worried—"

Li Yao did not know what to say.

"That Xiaoming and I will betray mankind?" asked Wenwen.

"No, that's not what I meant. I know that you're both good kids."

Li Yao paused for a moment but continued, "But we're different civilizations anyway, and we have different understandings about what's good. Even if we both think we're doing a good and righteous thing, we may still have conflicts. It's true that I'm worried about that.

"Tell me, Xiaoming and Wenwen, shouldn't I be worried? Why do I feel that there are too many problems for me to resolve?"

"You should be worried, but not too much."

Wenwen said, "Contradiction is the only momentum to push life and civilization forward. Exploring the unknowns, discovering problems, and working them out are exactly the recurring steps of evolution.

"Every living person and civilization has problems. Only the dead people are problem-free, and only the gone civilizations are absolutely harmonious and perfect. Xiaoming and I find problems and settle them in our database and algorithms every day. That's our greatest pleasure.

"We can't imagine the day when we can't find any logic error, because it will mean that our lives are no longer of any significance. Why would we live on if we're already perfect?

"From another perspective, if you're dissatisfied with this ending, what about another one? You resolved all the problems that mankind was faced with. Everybody lived in a perfect country and had no worries. Li Jialing, Lei Chenghu, and all of us became your puppets that absolutely worshipped you and would never betray you.

"Henceforth, mankind would have no challenges, no threats, and nothing unexpected, and everything was under your control. Is this the perfect ending that you're looking for?"

Li Yao thought for a moment and shuddered in coldness.

"This ending seems even worse."

Li Yao said, deep in thought, "So, on the trip to the Imperium, although we didn't get the best ending, we've got a least bad ending, haven't we?"

"Yes, Dad."

Xiaoming and Wenwen smiled at the same time. "Most of the time, the least bad ending IS the best ending!"

"Then, let's cheer for the least bad ending!"

Li Yao grinned, feeling greatly relieved after the talk with the two little kids.

Finishing the juice, he stared at the spectacular capital and murmured, "How can 'Vulture Li Yao' remain a hero without thrilling challenges? Wuying Qi was too weak for me! I do wonder who's going to be killed by my fists next. Weird. I haven't rested for a few days yet, but my bones are itchy again. How I look forward to new challenges…"

Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud!

Heavy footsteps interrupted Li Yao's murmur.

Lei Chenghu reached Li Yao gravely.

"Huh, why are you here instead of celebrating with His Majesty and the other Immortal Cultivators?"

Li Yao grimaced. "Your troop will set off to attack the rebels of the four families in three days. You wouldn't have a lot of time to drink by then."

Lei Chenghu's face was so awful as if it had just been bombarded by a warship.

Without saying anything, he tossed a jade chip that stored the latest report to Li Yao.

"According to the urgent report sent by the expedition army, a fleet of the Covenant Alliance, whose scale is unknown, broke into the Thick Soil Sector, which is at a critical part of our supply line, a day ago."

Lei Chenghu said solemnly, "The guards of the Thick Soil Sector were wiped out, suggesting that the enemy was very strong. It seems that the main forces of the Covenant Alliance that we've been worried about have finally emerged!"

(End of Volume VI: Imperium)

Chapter 2691: Gao Huan and Song Jingang (Beginning of Volume VII: Covenant Alliance) 26 days ago

Three days earlier, in the camp of the fourth regiment of the garrison of the Thick Soil Sector…

Gao Huan tried to control his muscles and stand up, only to fall into his vomit yet again.

As if ten thousand burning needles were poking his eyes, he saw Jiang Xue's face that he had been dreaming about again…

The medic said that he had recovered. All his broken cerebral nerves had been connected, and his burnt brain was healed. However, only Gao Huan knew that he was sicker and sicker, because everybody, including the man who was roaring at him and who was as brawny as a gorilla, looked more and more like Jiang Xue, his wife.

But the smell from the gorilla's mouth slightly woke Gao Huan up and made him realize that it was not his wife but his boss, Song Jingang, who was a captain of the regiment.

"Captain Song, be smart."

Gao Huan heard the man behind the desk speaking in a creepy tone. "Don't you know what's going on? We're in a reform…"

"F*ck reform! F*ck the emperor! F*ck Lei Chenghu! F*ck Li Yao!"

The captain threw profanities as skillfully as usual before he bashed the desk and shouted, "I don't care if you have reformed or not! I simply dislike you, you shameless traitor!"

"Ah."

The man behind the desk said, "Are you going to avenge General Yun? I never knew that you were so loyal to General Yun and your family."

"F*ck the general! F*ck the family!"

The captain roared again, "I never care about the family or any jerk that suddenly became my boss because of the superior's command. I simply hate the traitors like you. You're even more disgusting than this drunkard!"

The captain pointed at Gao Huan, and Gao Huan managed a smile, but the captain simply spat again.

"What can I do?"

The man behind the desk crossed his fingers without being infuriated. He continued unhurriedly, "You know what General Yun did. When the whole expedition army was undergoing the reform, he hoped to instigate a few officers from the four families to hijack a few starships and escape back to the territory of the four families… Tsk, tsk, tsk. How idiotic. If he had succeeded, those starships would've been destroyed on the way, and too many innocent people would've died because of him, including you and me. Don't forget that we're both from the four families!"

"Is that why you ratted in the military department and sold out the fourth regiment?"

The captain roared, "All the people with the surnames Li, Song, Yun, and Dongfang in the fourth regiment have been arrested!"

"Calm down. They're not arrested. They are only isolated for investigation. After it's figured out who's involved in General Yun's defection plan, the rest will be set free."

The man behind the desk smiled and said, "The new emperor is about to be coronated. In this subtle situation, isolation is good for them. It's better than them talking to other people about their plan. General Yun and I are brothers, but to save more brothers, I had to do what I did."

"Hehe. You had to? I hardly think so. You saved the lives of so many brothers, but more importantly, you proved your loyalty to the reformists, my 'acting commander'!"

The captain sneered. "If you're having such a thriving career among the reformists, why on earth did you get me out of the dark dungeon? Keep isolating me! Let the dogs of the military department bite me! Shoot me with General Yun if you want!"

"Please, Brother Jingang. I don't know why you're so prejudiced against me. I thought we were good bros in the same trench."

The acting commander opened his hands and said sincerely, "Listen, I want to save you. I really do. Do you have any idea how many people you've pissed off? Many of them are trying to kill you for your association with the four families!

"I want to save you. In three days, the new emperor will be crowned, and he will definitely pardon many people. By then, General Yun's defection will be unimportant. No other people will be involved.

"But there are three days to go. We have to survive these three days. You have no idea how much pressure I'm bearing and how many people want to quietly kill you.

"I need a reason to protect you, so I said to them that Song Jingang's team was the best reconnaissance team in the fourth regiment—no, the whole garrison of the Thick Soil Sector—and that this goddamn mission could only be accomplished by him. So, instead of torturing you in the dark dungeon and killing you for no good reason, they'd better set you free because I needed you!

"This is the last chance for you and your team. Do you understand?"

"Hold on a second…"

The captain asked, "How is it related to my team? Most of the soldiers from my team are not from the four families."

"But some of them are."

The acting commander said, "Besides, if their captain has been conspiring with the four families, they will all need some vetting and purging, won't they?"

The captain gnashed his teeth. "Are you threatening me?"

"Not threatening, but persuading."

The acting commander said, "Or rather, not persuading, but begging. I'm begging you to hold back your temper and get your a*s out of here with your team to accomplish this mission. Only in such a way can you save yourself as well as your team!"

After a long silence, the captain said solely, "You only took the mission because you were eager to make contributions as a newly-appointed commander, and you're asking us to carry out the risky mission for you so that you can be promoted, right?"

The acting commander smiled. "Brother Jingang, it's a miracle that you're still alive with your meanness."

After another long silence, the captain clenched his fists but eventually lost his spirit. "I'll need a Supersensitive Agent."

"He's right before you."

The acting commander pointed at Gao Huan and said, "He's the best Supersensitive Agent in the fourth regime. Everybody says that he has a dog nose. He can detect the weakest changes over the longest distance better than any magic equipment can."

"You've got to be kidding me!"

The captain jumped to his feet. "Everybody knows that he is completely ruined after his wife was taken by the people of the Covenant Alliance! His brain was burnt by excitants and industrial alcohols! You want me to take this Drunk Cat to investigate the Unchaotic Valley, the most dangerous place on this planet? He'll only get everyone killed! He's still drunk even now!

"I said that soldiers should not be married, and even if they are, a married couple should not be put in the same unit. Look at him! He used to be my best soldier! Drunk Cat, do you hear me? I'll be happy if you can rise and punch me!

"He can't work anymore. Just let him rest in the hospital before he drowns himself in alcohol. Where is Yun Hao? He's a Supersensitive Agent too. Get him for me!"

"I don't think I can. You know how he's related to General Yun. He's a dangerous suspect."

Pausing for a moment, the acting commander said, "Also, he tried to escape during the isolation, and he cursed even more badly than you did. So, his interrogators… Anyway, he's not dead yet, but he'll probably be in the ICU for half a month."

The captain's eyes bulged. "You son of a bi*ch…"

"Calm down, Brother Jingang, the lives of many people, including Yun Hao's, is in your hands. Think before you do anything!"

The acting commander said, "Also, Gao Huan is not as unworthy as you describe. He has recovered, and the medic proves that he can return to work now. Also, he's not drunk. It's just the side effects of the drugs. He'll be used to it soon."

"Oh? Is that so?"

The captain was suspicious. "Drunk Cat, do you know who you are and who I am?"

"Yes, boss!"

Gao Huan finally struggled back to his feet from his vomit. He stood at attention and saluted. "I… I'm Gao Huan, and you're Song Jingang, my boss!"

"Ha!"

The captain grew excited. "This is the only good news that I have today. Gao Huan, you're finally sober! Like I said, women are just like clothes, and you can always get a new one if you lost her. You've been drunk for half a year. That was really embarrassing…"

Bam!

When the captain said "if you lost her", Gao Huan's pupils constricted, feeling that the nerves in his head had broken again. He could not control himself at all when he lunged at the captain.

Then, he heard the sound of his fist smashing the captain's face and almost breaking his bones.

The captain then roared desperately, "F*ck!"

Chapter 2692: Unchaotic Valley In the Mist 26 days ago

When Gao Huan woke up again, he found himself in a light tank, which was crowded with smelly soldiers, making the cabin smell like a can that had been sour for half a month in a hot summer.

His itching skin reminded him of what happened just now. Infuriated, the captain shot him with a water gun for half an hour and finally washed the filth and the drunkenness off from him.

Gao Huan was finally back to himself now, and he could more or less distinguish the fuzzy faces of the companions next to him now: Bug, Leech, Black Leopard, One-eyed Tiger… All of them were his comrades who had been fighting with him.

Their clothes were ragged and sloppy, making them look more like beggars than the elite soldiers of the fourth regiment, but the same tattoos on their chests spoke of their toughness in silence. It was a burning saber that pierced through a black heart, with "Never To Forgive" engraved on the saber.

It was the emblem of their battle unit.

Gao Huan had the same emblem imprinted on his chest too.

Of course, there was also their beloved boss, Song Jingang, the captain of the team who had been secretly addressed as "gorilla king".

"Cheer up, you a*sholes! Where's your spirit! Now, listen to me!"

The captain roared. As a matter of fact, occasions were rare when the gorilla king did not roar.

"The Unchaotic Valley used to be the largest city on this planet hundreds of years ago. It was razed to the ground last time the Covenant Alliance invaded, and when we reclaimed the Thick Soil Sector, it was sabotaged and completely ruined by the Covenant Alliance again!

"Not only has the Covenant Alliance destroyed everything in the Unchaotic Valley, but they also planted infinite landmines and automatic defense rune arrays in the place. There might even be the remaining soldiers of the Covenant Alliance in the city. It can't be helped. We've been marching too fast to examine every inch of land on every planet. God knows if the lunatics of the Covenant Alliance are still active in some of their hideouts!

"Anyway, the Unchaotic Valley is the most dangerous place on this planet. The military department had been ignoring the place until a fog rose there a week ago.

"The uncanny fog covers hundreds of square kilometers and blocks all kinds of probe magic equipment. It cannot be driven apart with weather weapons. The military department suspects that someone is behind it.

"Given that the garrison of the Thick Soil Sector is short of hands, and they're busy in political struggles, no large starships can be deployed to investigate the fog. So, they can only send a team of unlucky b*stards to figure out the cause of the fog!

"Yes, bingo! We're exactly those unlucky b*stards. It's us again! It's always us! I believe that you're all used to it. So, no more complaints. Just shut up!

"Right, there's a piece of good news. Oh, maybe it's a bad one. Anyway, the Drunk Cat is back. Are you sober, Drunk Cat?"

"Yes, boss."

Gao Huan said, "I'm wide awake. What about you? Is your nose still at the right place?"

"Ohhhhh!"

The soldiers chuckled. Some raised their thumbs at Gao Huan, and some whistled at him. "Welcome home, Drunk Cat. Hitting the gorilla king the moment you're back, you do have balls!"

Even Song Jingang laughed at him. "You f*cking… Forget it. You can read the details of the mission on the report. All in all, don't play a hero, and don't do anything stupid. Nothing is worth dying for, do you understand?"

"Got it!"

The soldiers of the team roared crazily.

Smelling the stink, Gao Huan felt that his every cell was waking up. For a moment, he did forget his wife's face. He put on a smile while he took the blocking agent.

"Drunk Cat, what are you taking? Sh*it. Is it medical alcohol?"

"Flea", his life-and-death brother, approached him and smelled the blocking agent in his hand. His eyes bulged. "You're still drinking?"

"It's fine. The medic gave it to me. The gorilla king knows it too."

Gao Huan said, "You know my conditions. The stimulation of such an extent cannot affect my brain at all right now. It will only keep me refreshed so that I can carry out my talents as a Supersensitive Agent."

After a brief silence, Flea asked prudently, "Drunk Cat, you still haven't forgotten… Jiang Xue yet?"

Gao Huan grinned. "How could I? I can accept if she were killed on the battlefield, but she was captured by the Covenant Alliance. She must be alive. I have to save her. I will save her!"

"But…"

Flea couldn't help but suggest, "You know what will happen to the prisoners of the Covenant Alliance. She must've been deprived of her humanity even if she's alive!"

"That's fine. I'll remind her of everything."

Drinking up the medical alcohol, Gao Huan leaned against the cold wall and mumbled, "I'll save her and help her remember everything."

Flea sighed and smiled bitterly. "You should be called 'Love God' instead of 'Drunk Cat'. I finally understand why Jiang Xue fell in love with you, of all people in this team."

Gao Huan smiled. Unwilling to continue the topic, he asked, "What has happened outside when I was in the hospital? Our brother troops seem to be in a low morale."

"That's right. Too many things happened in the past year."

Flea said, "The reformists and the four families have been fighting vehemently and the situation is capricious. Today, the people from the four families may get control of supplies and try to starve the troops that are not loyal to them, but the next day, the reformists may defeat them and decide to purge all the officers that are from the four families. A week ago, our captain joined a defection group of the four families in his stupidity, hoping to escape back to his hometown with his trusted subordinates and the military secrets of the Thick Soil Sector, only to be caught by the reformists right on the spot. As a result, many people in our team are now kept isolated for investigation.

"All in all, the gorilla king is not wrong. The emperor is an a*shole. Soon, the Imperium will have two emperors, so double a*sholes! How can you keep a high morale when you don't know who you're serving?

"The situation in the Thick Soil Sector is not the worst. I was told that the troops of the reformists and those of the four families even attacked one another in other Sectors, completely forgetting that they were both parts of the expedition army.

"Thankfully, the Covenant Alliance has been crushed by us. Otherwise, if the Covenant Alliance sends an army back, the Imperium will have to give away the hundred Sectors that it has swallowed in the previous counterattack, and the whole expedition army will be wasted!

"Forget it. None of that is our business. For the soldiers like us, we better seize the day while we're still alive and make sure that we kill at least one enemy before we die!"

Flea laughed innocently, and so did Gao Huan.

He did not really care about bulls*it like morale, reform, Kurfürst, or the emperor. He was just making a conversation with Flea.

He wasn't scared of death either. Having been in the expedition army for so many years, he had already turned numb.

The only thing that he cared about was Jiang Xue, his wife.

Jiang Xue must be alive, whatever she had turned into.

So, he could not die. He must rescue Jiang Xue, or die together with her.

"Wait for me, Jiang Xue. I'm sober now. I'll get you out soon."

Gao Huan rubbed the mini jade chip on his neck that stored the videos and photos of him and his wife. If possible, he really wanted to implant the jade chip into his flesh.

Boom!

The dull noises of air currents came from the outside.

Gao Huan finally realized that the light tank they were on was actually loaded on a flying carrier.

"Hurry up, you gutless maggots! Inject buffer gel to your butts!"

The captain roared, "We've arrived at our destination, and we'll land for reconnaissance in ten seconds!"

Crack! Crack! Crack! Crack!

The deck of the carrier was slowly opened, and fifteen light tanks fell like dumplings. The dazzling exhaust flames that they spurted out tore the intense fog apart and lowered their speed, until they landed in the hazy Unchaotic Valley.

Without any instruction from their captain, the lazy soldiers suddenly turned into the most vigilant wolves and tigers. They put on crystal suits the moment they jumped out of their tank as they protected the tank and pressed into the fog.

Chapter 2693: Sacrifice 25 days ago

The Unchaotic Valley in the fog was like a physics nightmare.

Pale flames flickered in the debris that collapsed hundreds of years earlier, staring at the scouts as if the eyes of countless ghosts that were still alive.

The remains of battle puppets, crystal tanks, and starships were all over the place. Most of them were left hundreds of years previously and therefore were rusty and moldy. At first look, they looked like the rotting internal organs of a behemoth that were emitting a disgusting spell.

Even crystal tanks and crystal suits could not prevent the smell. Or maybe… that was fear.

Sitting at the center of a reconnaissance car, Gao Huan put a dark cold cover on his head and detected the unusual changes deep inside the fog with the talents of a Supersensitive Agent.

"How about it, Drunk Cat?"

Surprisingly, the captain did not roar but asked in a low voice, "Is the dog nose in your head as sharp as before?"

"Of course."

Narrowing his eyes, Gao Huan mumbled as if he were in a different place. "Something is seriously wrong with this place. There's a regular burst of magnetic waves every once in a while. It does not seem natural but more like some kind of… signal, and the fog around is meant to cover it.

"That's weird. This signal is too powerful. It's like a pillar rising to the sky in my senses. Even the fog is covering it. The fleets on the orbit of the planet should've detected it a long time ago."

"Ha. What a coincidence."

The captain said, "The commander of the orbit troops happens to be 'a rebel from the four families'. He was arrested a week ago, and a purge was conducted within the orbit troops. Who has the time to study some insignificant signals when their own life is at risk?

"Forget it. None of it helps right now. Tell me, where is the source of the signal?"

"It should be…"

Holding back the excruciating pain in his head, Gao Huan tried to locate the source of the signal.

Right before he found it out, somebody suddenly said in the communication channel, "Boss, Black Freckle stepped on a landmine!"

When the captain and Gao Huan reached the front of the team, the atmosphere was still quite relaxed. Black Freckle, the poor victim, was still smiling and mocking back at his companions.

Every part of the planet had been planted with landmines before the Covenant Alliance retreated, but the landmines were not very powerful. Besides, the soldiers of this team had the protection of crystal suits and enough experience in dealing with landmines. They could dismantle 99% of the landmines safely. Even if they couldn't, they would not wail as if it were the end of the world since they would only lose a foot or a leg.

At this moment, a soldier was studying the landmine below Black Freckle's foot. He prudently dug the ground, revealing the rusty shell of the landmine. It seemed to be an old Heavenly Rainbow I, which was powerful but not difficult to be removed.

However, as a Supersensitive Agent, Gao Huan smelled something unusual.

It was also the reason why the captain brought him here.

"Wait, this landmine does not smell right."

Gao Huan stopped the soldier's work. "It looks like the components of Heavenly Rainbow III in the shell of Heavenly Rainbow I."

Everybody gasped hard and stepped back, activating their spiritual shields to the highest intensity.

Black Freckle, who had been grimacing, was dumbfounded as if he had been hit by lightning.

"It's… It's really Heavenly Rainbow III!"

Reminded by Gao Huan, the soldier carefully examined the landmine. His face turned pale as he declared Black Freckle's death sentence.

Heavenly Rainbow III was an anti-infantry landmine. Or rather, it was designed to deal with crystal suits. Once it was detonated, an area of hundred meters around it would be destroyed, and the standard crystal suits of the bottom-level infantrymen of the Imperium could not resist the storm of shards at all!

"Soldier, come back! Black Freckle, stay there!"

The captain couldn't have looked more awful. Staring at Black Freckle, he gritted his teeth. "You've hit the jackpot. You know the power of Heavenly Rainbow III very well. We don't have enough technologies or tools to remove the landmine. But as long as your heartbeat changes slightly, the landmine will explode. There's nothing we can do."

"F*ck!"

The muscles on Black Freckle's face were twitching. "F*ck! F*ck! F*ck! F*ck! F*ck! F*ck!"

"Unfortunately, you have to die before us, but not now."

The captain said, "You have to wait until your brothers are safe and away from you, and you must die a hero's death, do you understand?"

"I… I…"

Black Freckle's face was twisted.

"Look at me and listen to me. It's hopeless now. We all know it very well. But you have your wife and children in your hometown. You have to leave something for them. I can delete some of the battle videos that our crystal suits record, but I cannot fabricate any because it will be detected."

The captain said patiently, "So, you have to die heroically. The new emperor is about to be coronated. He's going to need war heroes, and he will be generous with their families. If you become a war hero, your wife and your children will live a better life, won't they?"

"F*ck war hero! F*ck the emperor!"

Black Freckle was shivering. "How would I look like a hero? F*ck!"

"Don't shiver, and don't wail like a wuss!"

The captain roared, "Hold your head high and say something like long live His Majesty. No, don't say 'long live the reformists'. We don't know yet if they will win in the end! If you dare not do it, I can give you a hand. Rest assured, I'll help you. On the count of three, alright? There won't be any pain. I promise. Come on, one…"

"What is this s*it all about?"

Black Freckle said, his voice shivering, "Long live His Majesty! Long live the Imperium! Is that—"

Before he finished, the captain suddenly drew his gun and blew up Black Freckle's head.

Hardly had the headless body fallen when a spring of colorful light dashed from his foot and covered hundreds of square meters nearby.

Thankfully, all the soldiers had retreated a hundred meters away and protected themselves. They suffered no greater losses except their weakened spiritual shields.

Black Freckle was completely gone. Only the spots of blood on the ground proved that he was once alive.

"It was painless, was it not?"

Gloomily, the captain asked the soldiers around him.

"Yes, it was."

The soldiers all nodded their heads after a brief silence.

"It seems that we've found the right place."

The captain looked at Gao Huan and said, "Heaven Rainbow III is too advanced to be planted for common soldiers like us. Something of paramount importance must've been hidden behind the landmines. Let's remove the landmines and be ready for battle!"

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The tanks rained a storm of bullets on the landmines, triggering them and raising a splendid fireworks show.

Before the fire above the landmines died down yet, the team had started pressing deeper.

The loss of their companion did not depress them but made the soldiers even angrier than before.

After half a year's rest, Gao Huan seemed to have found the instincts of a Supersensitive Agent again. He keenly sensed the unusual waves in the air and adjusted their directions accordingly, locking onto the source of the signal at the center of the fog.

At this moment, strong signals were still being released from there like a burning torch.

Gao Huan even felt that the source of the signal was so blatant that it did not care about being noticed anymore.

Navigated by him, the team finally arrived at their destination, only to be shocked by what they saw.

Deep inside the valley was a narrow, long chasm. A certain type of piton-shaped magic equipment was protruding into the sky from a hundred meters deep in the chasm. The four pitons on the magic equipment formed an array, with dazzling electric arcs dancing among them!

Even more dreadfully, deep inside the chasm, below the weird array whose purpose was unknown, countless hibernation capsules were densely placed like bug eggs. Inside each hibernation capsule was a person of the Covenant Alliance. Many of them had been woken up, and the rest were also opening their emotionless eyes under the glass cover.

A secret base of the Covenant Alliance had been buried on the planet that the Imperium had reclaimed and occupied for three years!

Chapter 2694: The Devoted Lover 25 days ago

When the secret base was discovered by the team, the ritual of the Covenant Alliance was close to completion.

Infinite electric arcs were soaring to the sky like an upside-down waterfall, tearing apart the clouds and gathering into a chaotic vortex that seemed to be leading to another world.

Something with immense spiritual waves was landing on this planet through the vortex!

Now, even the people who were not as super-sensitive as Gao Huan could tell that it must be a beacon or even a space gate.

"Those sons of bi*ches hid a super teleportation array, or whatever that crap is, on our planet!"

The captain cursed again, "How is it possible? What kind of super teleportation array is this? Or is it a brand-new space gate? Why can it send signals into space through the atmosphere without being detected by the morons in the military department?"

"What do we do, boss? Are we going to fight them or not?"

Flea's voice was shaking, which was understandable. The soldiers of this team were all fearless of death, but they were only here for reconnaissance anyway. The people of the Covenant Alliance who were waking up were ten times more than themselves, and launching an attack right now would be suicide.

Their mission did not include destroying the enemy's important facilities!

"Of course!"

The captain glared at Flea and roared while pointing at the vortex of electric arcs, "Do you not see the gap over there? If we do not interrupt their summoning, who knows what will be teleported to this planet? We might all be killed by then!

"Fight them and blow that eyesore up into pieces! Let's kick those b*stards back to their homes!"

The captain grabbed two cannons that were thicker than his thighs and fired like a crazy gorilla. The other soldiers also roared and charged like tigers, catching the people of the Covenant Alliance unprepared.

However, the people of the Covenant Alliance did not know what panic meant. They immediately counterattacked with their advantage in number. For a moment, the whole Unchaotic Valley was consumed by fire.

For a Supersensitive Agent like Gao Huan, keen senses were both a gift and a burden. They could detect the waves dozens of kilometers away with this ability, but in a battlefield, they would take hundreds of times more blast and pain than normal people would.

At this moment, the battlefield turned into the most splendid painting in Gao Huan's eyes. Everybody was like a burning torch, and tsunamis of spiritual energy were rising in his brain, overwhelming him as if he were a sinking boat.

But at this moment, as if lightning struck in his head, he suddenly saw her among the thousands of enemies.

Although he could barely distinguish anyone at this moment, and although his optic nerves and his brainwaves had been blasted by the shock of spiritual energy, making him feel that the world was swirling, burning, and exploding, he was sure that he was not mistaken: that hue of redness could only belong to his wife. It was Jiang Xue!

"Jiang Xue!"

Gao Huan's head went blank as he roared in ecstasy.

Cannons were firing, bullets were flying, and the altars and the teleportation of the Covenant Alliance were collapsing. The battlefield couldn't have been more chaotic.

Gao Huan did not know how he passed through the battlefield, or how many times he was hit. He could see nothing but the redness. After stumbling and falling, he finally made it to her alive.

She was a female soldier with a red mole on her eyebrow. Rigid and indifferent, she looked as mechanical as all the people of the Covenant Alliance around her.

Gao Huan, however, couldn't help but cry, "Jiang Xue, I knew that you were not dead! I knew I could find you! It's me! It's me!"

The female soldier, unmoved, raised her saber and aimed at Gao Huan's neck.

However, Gao Huan's tears raised a subtle reflection in her frozen eyes, making her saber hesitate for a moment.

Because of the brief hesitation, Gao Huan was not killed.

Right when the thoughtfulness in the female soldier's eyes was gone and she was ready to behead Gao Huan, a cannonball exploded next to them and blew them in different directions.

"Retreat! Retreat!"

In his trance, Gao Huan did not know who was exclaiming.

"The enemy's teleportation array has taken shape. It's pointless to ruin the ground facilities now. Let's report to the military department now!"

Gao Huan thought he had slept for a long time when he relived his life with his wife in the dream.

But when he opened his eyes again, he found that he was still in the light tank that smelled like a sour can, next to his defeated, blackened companions.

Gao Huan soon figured out their situation through the light beams.

There were fifteen armed tanks when they set off, but only three escaped from the Unchaotic Valley.

Behind them, the sky had been riddled with wormholes, through which the starships of the Covenant Alliance were arriving. Some of their commanders had even landed and were sprawling all over the planet like a tide.

"This planet is doomed. Chances are that the whole Thick Soil Sector is over."

Flea's left eye had turned into a bloody hole, but he was still laughing. "It seems that the Covenant Alliance has summoned their main troops to this bloody planet through a new teleportation technology. There are other portals for the Covenant Alliance besides the Unchaotic Valley. They're ten times more than the garrison of this place. We can't win this war."

"We…"

Holding back his sickness, Gao Huan asked, "What do we do? Return to our base?"

"Our base was the focus of the enemy's bombardment. It's already been blown up!"

Flea said to Gao Huan, "Now, the whole planet is occupied by the Covenant Alliance. Our reconnaissance mission is unimportant now. We better try to save our own lives first!"

"But—"

Gao Huan wanted to ask where they could possibly run to if the whole planet was occupied by the Covenant Alliance and their base was destroyed.

"We'll go to the Long Wind Canyon. There's a spaceport that has been abandoned for decades. General Yun and his accomplices hid three fast shuttles there with fuel and everything as their backup plan."

The captain grinned. With the blood on his face, he looked even more like a gorilla. He said cockily, "Those interrogators of the military department did not elicit that information from me at all!"

"Is this…"

Gao Huan thought for a moment and asked, "Is this desertion?"

"Of course not!"

The captain smiled shamelessly. "The garrison of the Thick Soil Sector is about to be destroyed due to the new teleportation array or whatever it was that the Covenant Alliance had hidden earlier, and we're the only people who have witnessed it in person. This piece of intelligence is very important, alright? So, we're not deserting! We are delivering the precious intelligence to His Majesty, Lei Chenghu, the Black Wind King, and the other b*stards in the capital. We're going to be heroes!"

Gao Huan was rendered speechless. His head was a mess.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Suddenly, several fireballs rose behind him, and their blasts made the three tanks hobble on the uneven mountain road.

"Damn it! The starship is after us again!"

The gunner roared, "I cannot lock onto it at all on this bumpy road. Also, our cannon is too weak. Unless we stop the tank and transmit all the spiritual energy to the cannon, it's impossible to shoot the starship down! We can never make it to the Long Wind Canyon if it tails us!"

There was nothing but silence in the tank.

"Let me stay."

Staring at the burning horizon behind him, Gao Huan suddenly said, "Give the tank to me. You'll all go to the other two tanks. I'll cover up for you."

"Have you lost your mind?"

Flea grew anxious. "You will die if you stay! We have to go!"

"No, I can't go."

Gao Huan smiled. "I saw Jiang Xue just now. It's really her. I'm not crazy. I cannot leave her alone here."

All the soldiers were stupefied, but none of them dared to question him—not after they saw the hope in Gao Huan's eyes.

"Wake up already!"

Flea, his best friend, was still hoping to talk some sense into him. "Even if she is really Jiang Xue, so what? How are you going to save your wife when this whole planet has been occupied by the Covenant Alliance? Besides, she's been brainwashed by the Covenant Alliance, and all her feelings and memories about you are gone. Why would she go with you?

"There will only be two possibilities if you stay. You will either die, or even worse, you will also be captured and brainwashed by the Covenant Alliance. Then what? You and your wife both serve the Covenant Alliance? Isn't it pointless?"

"No, it is not."

With the fire of devotion running in his eyes, Gao Huan said, "I will not forget my wife even if I'm brainwashed. Also, I don't believe that Jiang Xue has forgotten me even though she's brainwashed."

"Do you know that she recognized me just now? She really did. I'm not drunk, and it's not my illusion. I saw the light in her eyes. She recognized me! She was not completely brainwashed!

"So, as long as I can find her in the Covenant Alliance, I will definitely restore her feelings and memories as well as mine, and we're going to escape together!"

Chapter 2695: Take You Home! 24 days ago

"Do you really think you're a Love God?"

Flea's bloody eye socket was almost spewing out a fire as he glared at his best friend. "I know that you haven't recovered at all. You're absolutely crazy! Someone, help me tie him up in case he does crazy things again!"

"Don't tie me up! Let me go!"

Gao Huan's eyes were bloodshot too, making him look like a ferocious wolf. "You need someone to cover the retreat for you, and I have reasons not to leave, so I'll stay! Flea, don't argue with me, or none of us will get to go!

"Boss, stop the tank, please! You know I'm not crazy! I simply can't watch Jiang Xue suffer here. I really saw her just now. I really did! Please trust me! I won't return to the capital as long as she's here. It's impossible!"

"Stop the tank…"

The captain gave the order after a long silence.

They accelerated in a turn to temporarily escape from the Covenant Alliance's starship. Then, they found a hideout, where all of them crammed into two tanks and left most of the ammos as well as an intact tank for Gao Huan.

"Cut the crap. Go now. Don't stop me from finding my wife!"

Gao Huan grinned at the captain and Flea.

Flea glared at him, and his face suddenly collapsed.

"That's right. All of you, get the hell out of here!"

Before Flea said anything, the captain suddenly shut the door from the outside and stood next to Gao Huan.

"Boss!"

Gao Huan looked at his captain at a loss.

"Why are you looking at me? You think you can shoot down the enemy's starship when you can barely walk straight?"

The captain said casually, "Besides, I don't want to go to the capital either. I'm from the four families, and I'm among the few survivors who weren't killed in the Thick Soil Sector. I would only be executed as a deserter or a traitor if I go to the capital, wouldn't I?"

"But—"

Gao Huan stammered, "You'll be a hero. You're not among the leaders of the four families anyway, and you've done something wrong to the Imperium. Maybe you'll become an awesome war hero after some simple vetting. Like you said just now, we're not deserters but deliverers of important intelligence!"

"Some simple vetting?"

The captain laughed and spat a mouthful of bloodstained saliva before he opened his fingers and said, "Do you see my hands? They're born to kill people, not to write confessions and fill out forms! When the dogs of the military department interrogated me, I swore that I would never be vetted by anyone ever again. Neither the emperor nor Lei Chenghu or the f*cking Black Wind King will ever make me write a single word of confession for them!"

"Boss!"
