Gao Huan did not know what to say. "If so, you can escape back to the Song family's territory. Didn't you say that General Yun stacked multiple shuttles? You could separate with them and go home!"

"Forget it! The way I see it, the four families are even more worthless than the reformists. Emperor Chengwu won't survive for long."

The captain waved his hand and said, "Besides, you know how I always piss people off. I wouldn't have been kicked to the frontline to defend the Thick Soil Sector with you if I had friends in my hometown in the first place, would I? Hahahaha!

"All in all, forget it. The world in the rear is not suitable for me. This place is good."

"Boss!"

"Captain!"

"Gorilla king!"

Hearing his laugh, all the soldiers couldn't hold on anymore. Their eyes turned red.

"Stop! Please don't be so emotional. I'm having goosebumps all over my body!"

The captain cursed and smashed the soldiers' heads back into the tank with his gun. "Do you know what you look like right now? Those a*s-kicking Cultivators!

"All in all, get the hell out of here in three seconds, go to the Long Wind Canyon, escape from this goddamn planet, report what happened here to the capital, and ask the emperor for rewards. If I'm rewarded too, you'll split up my share and help me f*ck a couple of bi*ches. Are we clear?

"Now, carry out my order!"

The captain smashed the last head back into the tank.

The noises of the starship came again from the mountains not far away.

The soldiers cried from the two tanks before they left unwillingly.

The captain was finally relieved when both of the tanks disappeared. He turned around and adjusted the turret on the last tank with Gao Huan.

They dismantled the protection devices of the turret and focused all the spiritual energy in the tank on it so that it could fire astoundingly for once.

The two of them worked in silence without saying anything.

When the noises were louder and louder and the crazy wind from the enemy's starship was about to blow away their disguise, Gao Huan suddenly asked, "Boss, do you think I really saw Jiang Xue just now?"

The captain looked at him thoughtfully, as if he were going to say something. But he paused for a moment before he continued, "Well, I think I saw her too."

"Really?"

Gao Huan almost burst into tears of joy. "You saw her too? Is it really her?"

"I… think so?"

The captain said, "How could I be certain when the whole battlefield was on fire? But I think it was her?"

"Then, did you see her movement?"

Gao Huan asked excitedly, "She hesitated when she attacked me, right? It means that she still has some feelings that aren't washed off, right?"

"Huh…"

The captain said, "Now that you pointed it out, I think that's exactly what happened."

"You're not lying to me? You're really not lying to me?"

Gao Huan finally shed tears. "You're my boss. You can't lie to me!"

"Of course I'm not. Have I ever lied to you?"

The captain narrowed his eyes and looked at the sky. "I will definitely go to hell and be broiled there after I die if I lie to you!"

"Well…"

Gao Huan was more or less embarrassed after the captain swore to him. After a long time, he said while blushing, "Boss, do you think I can get Jiang Xue back? Can I bring back her feelings and memories?"

The captain stared at Gao Huan for a long time… until he laughed in the end.

He patted Gao Huan's shoulder and said in an unprecedentedly gentle voice, "Don't be silly. Even god will have goosebumps seeing how much you love your wife. You won't die. Like you said, you'll find your wife in the Covenant Alliance, wake up her feelings and memories, and escape from the s*ithole. Really. I promise you that it will happen!"

Gao Huan looked at his captain's uneven and hideous face and smiled like a fool.

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

The cannon on the tank beeped when the starship had entered their best shooting range.

"Boss!"

Gao Huan licked his lips and said, "Should we say something heroic now? Like what we're fighting for?"

"Forget it!"

The captain couldn't help but laugh. "There's no chance for us to upload our battle videos. What are we fighting for? We're Immortal Cultivators. Do Immortal Cultivators need a reason to fight?

"Forget those a*sholes and scumbags. For once, we're completely fighting for ourselves. Are you ready? On the count of three. Three, two, one!"

Boom!

The cannon was fired, and Gao Huan was blown away by the blast that almost tore the tank apart. He was dizzy again.

In his trance, he saw a hideous fireball rising in the front of the shark-like starship, which consumed the front part of the starship and destroyed its anti-gravity system, making the starship fall as if it had been bitten by an invisible beast.

He saw that his captain laughed and grabbed another two portable cannons that were thicker than his thighs. Opening fire at the people of the Covenant Alliance and pressing forward, the captain truly looked like an unbroken gorilla, until he ran out of ammunitions and was swallowed by fire.

Hearing the familiar cries of his brothers from his back, Gao Huan turned around, only to see Flea and the other soldiers marching back in the second tank.

Flea was dancing up and down on the top of the tank with crystal bombs all over his body. He was truly like a vigorous flea at this moment.

"Drunk Cat! Drunk Cat!"

Flea shouted delightedly, "Don't forget that your wife was the goddess of everybody in this team! How can we watch her be taken by those b*stards of the Covenant Alliance? Come on. We'll help you get her out together!"

Together with Flea, Leech, Black Leopard, and their brothers, Gao Huan charged at the enemy's formation following their captain's footprints.

When he was hit by that scorching bullet, he was still wearing a casual and confident smile.

"Wait for me, honey!"

Gao Huan thought, Boss promised me, and my brothers are so loyal. So, I can't die. I will find you, think of you, and take you back… home…

Chapter 2696: Heavy Burden 24 days ago

One day later, in the capital…

The news that the Thick Soil Sector had fallen again had been spread to the capital. The devastating outcome that the garrison of the Thick Soil Sector was entirely wiped out enshrouded the capital with blood and gloom. It was like a bad joke when the new emperor was faced with attacks from the rebels of the four families and the Covenant Alliance after he just ascended to the throne and made bold declarations.

Before the party was over, an emergence and confidential meeting began. The participants of the meeting included the new emperor, as well as Li Yao, Lei Chenghu, Li Linghai, Boss Bai, Jin Yuyan, Long Yangjun, the Fist King, and some other people.

Those were not necessarily the most powerful people in the Imperium. Many big shots in the expedition army had high titles and forces comparable to theirs.

However, they were the only people that the new emperor could really trust and rely on.

"The Covenant Alliance's attack began two to three days ago. They made thorough preparations for the attack. Before the battle, they blocked all the signals that the Thick Soil Sector sent out and simulated peacefulness with fake signals, buying them a crucial day."

Gloomily, Lei Chenghu introduced to Li Yao and the rest of them with an enormous light beam on his back. "The expedition army had been scattered on the hundreds of planets in the hundred Sectors that we occupied in the first place. Many of them were recalled to take part in the battle against the four families. So, there were not enough soldiers in the Thick Soil Sector, and the invaders were dozens of times more than them. They tried to resist, but it was impossible for them to win.

"Supposedly, we couldn't have learned the loss of the Thick Soil Sector yet, in which case the few Sectors near it might've fallen too, but thankfully, some of the soldiers who belonged to a special reconnaissance team miraculously stole a high-speed shuttle and escaped from the Covenant Alliance's siege.

"Not only have they brought us the message that the Covenant Alliance has returned, but they also presented first-hand files on how the Covenant Alliance jumped to the main planet of the Thick Soil Sector so precisely. Please take a look."

Battle videos appeared on the light beam behind Lei Chenghu, displaying the Covenant Alliance's secret base as well as the super teleportation array that the soldiers of the reconnaissance team captured.

"This is…"

Everybody was attracted to the magic equipment that looked like a spire. They were also disgusted by the hibernation capsules of the Covenant Alliance.

As the best refiner here, Li Yao was obliged to speak first. He narrowed his eyes and studied for a long time before he said, "It seems like some sort of super teleportation array or a powerful signal launcher. It's in the vintage style of the primeval age.

"Judging from the runes and the dazzling electric arcs, it was trying to tear apart the three-dimension, create mini wormholes, and send special signals to somewhere else.

"In other words, it can partly replace the space gate. It's even an advanced and secret space gate itself that can summon the main forces of the Covenant Alliance from thousands of lightyears away!"

"How's it possible?"

Li Jialing—Li Yao still preferred his old name—found it hard to believe. "How can a space gate be constructed within the atmosphere? Its signals will be warped when they pass the atmosphere!"

"About that, the Covenant Alliance might have improved their space gate with the new technologies that they excavated. Or maybe they hid a space gate in the Thick Soil Sector, and this facility on the ground was only meant to activate it."

Li Yao opened his hands and said, "There are many ways to do that. The only problem is that the garrison should've discovered the intense space ripples and spiritual energy when the space gate was opened. Why was the garrison of the Thick Soil Sector so sluggish?"

"Because…"

Lei Chenghu paused for a moment, and he couldn't have looked more awful. "A while back, the garrison of the Thick Soil Sector initiated an operation codenamed 'Absolute Loyalty'. Many traitors from the four families who opposed the reform were found. Unfortunately, the commanders of the orbit troops were from the four families and were arrested, which resulted in the flaws in the defense."

Silently, Li Yao stared at Lei Chenghu with his eyes bulging.

Lei Chenghu could hardly confront his burning eyes. Out of his usual behavior, he turned his head aside.

"Seriously?"

Li Yao, however, did not let him go. He gnashed his teeth and asked, "You're doing a purge at the frontline at such a critical moment when the Covenant Alliance may attack anytime?"

"What choices do I have?"

Veins jutted out on Lei Chenghu's neck as he bashed the table and roared, "Since their failure in the Seven Seas Sector and the capital, the four families have been trying to win the expedition army over. They know that they're losing, so they're investing tremendous money to attract the soldiers in the expedition army who belong to the four families and instigate them to rebel!

"Nobody wants to treat their comrades so cruelly. I do not want the soldiers to be killed by their own men either. However, vetting and loyal education are necessary, or the whole expedition army might pledge loyalty to Emperor Chengwu overnight!

"Yes, the garrisons of different Sectors have different ways of investigation, and some of them are radical, but what can I do? Can I split myself into thousands of parts and supervise the investigation in every Sector like a kindergarten teacher?

"Don't say that it's cruel! How can we ensure the expedition army's loyalty to the new emperor, to the reform, and to the new Imperium? By asking a Cultivator to make a speech to them or to play porn videos for them again?"

"You!"

Li Yao was so angry that he did not know what to say. "How can you do that?"

"Alright, you two…"

Li Jialing was new to being an emperor. His performance during the coronation ceremony was good enough, but he lost the manners of an emperor under the emergency. He could only say, "What happened has happened. We better figure out a solution instead of fighting among ourselves! Lei Chenghu, how are things going on in other Sectors? Has the Covenant Alliance attacked them?"

"Not for now."

Taking a deep breath, Lei Chenghu managed to calm himself down. He displayed the map of the newly-recovered area on the light beam behind him before he said hoarsely, "However, the loss of the Thick Soil Sector is already very severe, if not critical! Please take a look. The Thick Soil Sector is an intersection of many sailing routes in the newly-recovered area. It's a transportation hub for different battle areas, so a lot of resources have been stacked there.

"The fall of the Thick Soil Sector has not only made us lose a lot of precious resources but also disrupted the supply to the frontline. Earlier, the troops from dozens of worlds rested and replenished in the Thick Soil Sector when they marched, and if they have to choose a different route, they will have to consume 20% more fuel and resources.

"More importantly, after occupying the Thick Soil Sector, it's like the Covenant Alliance has smashed a nail into the heart of our newly-recovered area. They can attack all the nearby Sectors with the Sector as their base."

Lei Chenghu clenched his fists as he talked.

"I tried to explain to the Council of Elders a year ago that our battle up until then had been going on too well. Our expedition army was projected to more planets and more sailing routes when the main forces of the Covenant Alliance were not destroyed yet, and we lost our mobility!"

Lei Chenghu said coldly, "Now, the main forces of the Covenant Alliance have been gathered into a fist that can punch anywhere they want, but we have to divide our army to defend a hundred Sectors simultaneously. It's possible that none of them can be secured in the end!"

"Then…"

Naturally, Li Yao asked, "Can you abandon part of the newly-recovered area and gather our forces to fight the main forces of the Covenant Alliance, or at least to defend the most critical locations?"

"It's very difficult. It was the best strategy a year ago, but it's not an option right now. It's not a military problem; it's a political one."

Lei Chenghu shook his head and said, "We're different from the Covenant Alliance. The people of the Covenant Alliance are a bunch of lunatics and machines who do not care about consequences. They can abandon dozens of Sectors without any hesitation and bury themselves for years if not decades for the best opportunity of a comeback, but we can't do the same. We're the army of the Imperium. We're the guardians of mankind that represent the glory of our civilization!

"Now, the new emperor has just been coronated, and the Imperium is about to be ushered into the most splendid age, but we're retreating from dozens of Sectors and making those worlds fall into darkness again?

"If so, the new emperor and the Imperium will be in too much of a disgrace. We can't explain to the people, and the rebels of the four families will definitely attack us with that excuse or even instigate the expedition army to take their side!"

Chapter 2697: The Covenant Alliance's Cutthroat Attack! 23 days ago

Li Yao was very angry, but there was nothing he could say as he realized that the newly-recovered area was indeed a headache for whoever controlled it.

The two emperors supported by the reformists and the four Kurfursten families respectively were fighting for the legitimacy of their right to the throne. For the people of the Imperium of True Human Beings who believed in survival of the fittest, strength was the best justification. If Li Jialing lost tremendous territory just after he claimed the crown, it was true that many people would be disappointed and many experts might change their sides.

The four Kurfursten families certainly wouldn't hesitate to mock either. Why was the newly-covered area impenetrable when it was under the control of the four families, yet it was immediately conquered when the reformists got it?

If resistance failed and the enemy claimed a few Sectors, it would be easier to explain. But if the reformists retreated from dozens of Sectors without fighting at all, wouldn't it be treason? The political consequences that it would cause might even collapse the regime of the reformists.

"We have to say that the Covenant Alliance chose the best timing."

Lei Chenghu said gloomily, "I thought that they would attack when the Battle of Seven Seas or the battle in the capital was most heated, but they waited patiently until two emperors were confronting one another in the Imperium.

"The confrontation of two emperors means that it's impossible for the reformists and the four families to negotiate. They have to destroy each other and will never work together to resist the Covenant Alliance!"

Li Yao was silent. He had to admit that Lei Chenghu was right.

Royal power was supreme in the Imperium of True Human Beings. Even the four Kurfursten had to exert their dominance through the royal power.

The Immortal Cultivators could compromise in their beliefs. For example, Lei Chenghu could cooperate with Li Yao even though Li Yao was a Cultivator.

After all, Li Yao represented the Star Glory Federation, which did not have an emperor but only a "Speaker", which was not competitive with the crown and would not jeopardize the brilliance of the royal power.

During the battle of the capital and the Battle of Seven Seas, the reformists and the four families hadn't elected their emperor yet, and there was still room for negotiation.

If the Covenant Alliance had attacked back then, chances were that the reformists and the four families would have reached a truce and fought the common foe first.

It was definitely the last thing that the Covenant Alliance would like to see.

But now that both parties had elected their emperors, they could only fight each other until they destroyed their enemies even though they knew that the Covenant Alliance was coveting them.

"The Covenant Alliance certainly knows the best timing because the whole Imperium has been compromised by them. The intelligence on the military, politics, and economy of the Imperium is delivered to the Covenant Alliance on a daily basis. There are too many spies for the Association of Demon Hunters to catch," said Long Yangjun.

Since she had helped the new emperor ascend the throne, her job as the president of the Association of Demon Hunters had been restored. Furthermore, as Yue Wushuang, the head of the other special agency of the Imperium, was involved in Wuying Qi's scheme and being secretly investigated, Long Yangjun's rival was gone, and she became the sole leader of spies of the Imperium.

She did not need her fake name, Dongfang Mingyue, anymore. After all, Dongfang was not a popular name among the reformists. She was described to be the reformists' agent that was sent to the Dongfang family for special missions, and now that her missions were done, she had picked up her old name.

Now, Long Yangjun's main job was to deal with the spies that the Covenant Alliance had sent to the Imperium. She was doing quite a good job.

Li Yao had seen the spies of the Covenant Alliance on Kunlun more than a hundred years ago. Those spies were emotionless but could simulate the feelings of human beings, and they managed to pass the Imperium's tests again and again.

The corruption and darkness of the Imperium created the best environment for the spies of the Covenant Alliance. For the people of the Imperium mired in blood at the bottom of the Imperium, it was not the worst option to join the Covenant Alliance!

Therefore, the spies of the Covenant Alliance had never been cleared, and they caused severe harm.

On the other hand, because of the social structure of the Covenant Alliance and the Path of Ultimate Benevolence, it was barely possible for the agents of the Imperium to sneak into the Covenant Alliance.

Previously, the military of the Imperium had sent many strong and determined agents into the Covenant Alliance, but few of them could stand the Covenant Alliance's repetitive brainwashing. They all ended up as real members of the Covenant Alliance and confessed a lot of secrets of the Imperium.

As a result, the Imperium did not know the Covenant Alliance much even though they had been at war for a thousand years. It was also an important reason as to why the Covenant Alliance remained undefeated after a thousand years when it was in remote worlds and had few resources.

"All in all, it's impossible for us to negotiate with the four families in this competition of legitimacy. No matter how aggressive the Covenant Alliance, we have to carry on our previous plan!"

Lei Chenghu bashed the table and declared, "His Majesty has personally announced the mobilization order against rebels. He would be a joke if it's never carried out. Also, our army has been making military preparations since half a year ago, and there is no way to stop right now!"

Li Yao was silent. He also knew that the war between the reformists and the four families was inevitable.

It was not just about the authority of the emperor but also about the implementation of the new policies.

On one hand, the four families were not dead yet, and they were trying to heal their wounds. They were getting stronger each day.

When they were healed, it would be very tricky to deal with them.

On the other hand, the pardon order, the abolition order, and the reformists' other policies had to be carried out with the fat from the bodies of the four families!

Abolition was more difficult than it sounded. Liberating and educating the people required money. The city that Li Yao, Xiaoming, and Wenwen established in the Great Iron Factory below the ground of the capital planet consumed astronomical investments, and there were too many hominoids and more Great Iron Factories all over the Imperium that were in dire need of help.

As a Cultivator, Li Yao definitely supported the reform of the Imperium. He certainly couldn't try to get money from the reformists like Lei Chenghu, Li Linghai, or Li Jialing. If they did not kill the four families and acquire their money, where could they find the investments for their reform?

Without money, the pardon order, the abolition order, and the subsequent reform policies would be jokes, and the foundation of the reformists would be changed. Eventually, even the national interests of the Star Glory Federation might be at risk.

So, Li Yao could only agree with Lei Chenghu that the battle against the four families had to be carried out even if the Covenant Alliance was turning the newly-recovered area upside down!

"So, we're going to fight on two sides at the same time?"

Looking at the map behind Lei Chenghu, Li Yao asked, "To be faced with the military pressure of the Covenant Alliance and the four families simultaneously?"

"Yes. In the best-case scenario…"

Lei Chenghu said, "If the expedition army can stall the Covenant Alliance in the newly-recovered area, and the army that I command can crush the last capable troop of the four families, we'll be able to take our time to deal with the Covenant Alliance."

"However, now that the Covenant Alliance has emerged at such a tricky moment, they certainly wouldn't watch us finish off the four families."

Li Linghai coughed and said, "I'm very suspicious that the Covenant Alliance's target is the newly-recovered area. If they really wanted those Sectors, they wouldn't have burnt those Sectors to the ground when they retreated a few years ago.

"Is the debris of any significance to them even if they can get it back?

"General Lei Chenghu said in his report to the Council of Elders that the Covenant Alliance was implementing a strategy, hoping to win the war once and for all. I agree with him. If it's true, the Covenant Alliance's target won't be the newly-recovered area or the expedition army; it might be the capital!

"That's right. Skipping the hundreds of Sectors in the middle and stabbing the heart of the Imperium sounds like a suicide strategy and will never work out at other times.

"However, now that most of our forces have to be deployed to eliminate the rebels of the four families, and the remaining forces are scattered in the dozens of Sectors in the newly-recovered area, the capital will be unprecedentedly undefended when the final battle between us and the rebels of the four families begins, and it will be the best moment for the Covenant Alliance to cut our throat!"

Chapter 2698: Super Jump Point! 23 days ago

Li Linghai was the empress dowager of the Imperium of True Human Beings right now, but in front of her allies, she considered herself the former leader of the reformists and did not cover her sharpness at all.

While talking, she displayed the defense of the whole Empyreal Terminus Sector at present.

Once upon a time, the Empyreal Terminus Sector was the most impenetrable stronghold. Every inch of it was armed to the teeth.

Pitifully, when the four families attacked the Empyreal Terminus Sector, all the defense lines apart from the capital planet were destroyed, and the solar storm that happened later further ruined them. In the following half a year, the reformists had been too busy reorganizing themselves to repair the defense lines.

The capital had never been more lightly defended in the past thousand years.

If Lei Chenghu was to mobilize the best fleets to launch an expedition at the four families' territory, the capital wouldn't have enough troops to resist the assault of the main forces of the Covenant Alliance.

"That's it?"

Looking at the map of the capital, Li Yao was deep in thought. "So, the Covenant Alliance's ultimate purpose is the capital? I remember that General Lei Chenghu told me that the Covenant Alliance had been an uncreative, abnormal country that lacked the vigor of a real society. They must be bordering on collapse after a thousand years and have to fight a final battle with the Imperium.

"Assuming that the Covenant Alliance's target is the capital, even if they can take it, so what? Most of the Immortal Cultivators would never surrender to the Covenant Alliance. They would keep on fighting in other Sectors until the last soldier on the last planet of the last Sector dies. Even the four families wouldn't surrender either. The war would last for at least decades.

"If the Covenant Alliance is bordering on collapse, occupying the capital won't help. Can they keep attacking for a hundred years?"

"So, as I've told you before, I'm suspecting that the Covenant Alliance's ultimate target is not the capital, but the entrance to the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum."

Li Linghai said, "The Covenant Alliance unexpectedly occupied the Thick Soil Sector to wreak chaos on the expedition army, which will create an opportunity for them to attack the capital. However, attacking the capital is just another distraction. Their real purpose is to occupy the space zone where the entrance to the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum is located. It's somewhere around here…"

Several strange maps appeared on the light beam. Among the sparse stars were several dot lines and blips.

"The entrance to the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum is like a ghost island that surfaces in the ocean regularly. It has been a year and a half since the mausoleum last showed up. If my calculation is correct, it will appear again three months to half a year later."

Li Linghai said, "Based on the previous experience, the entrance will be steady for a long time. If the Covenant Alliance are really planning to sneak into the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum, this will be their best opportunity over the past hundreds of years.

"I believe that you've all realized that the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum is not just the place where the Supreme Emperor is buried, but also the place that countless primeval experts are sleeping in. The Covenant Alliance, on the other hand, is a fanatic organization that worships the primeval experts rather than a normal country. Their goal is to wake up all the primeval experts!"

Everybody was shivering when they heard about that.

One could only infer what their enemy wanted to do when their enemy was normal, but who could tell what a bunch of inhumane lunatics would do?

"I'm still confused. How can the entrance of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum be so unpredictable?"

Frowning, Li Yao pointed at the map and said, "The possible location of the entrance crosses two Sectors. That's a wide range! Also, you said it would appear three months to half a year later. Is it going to be three months or half a year? Why is the entrance to the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum so mysterious? Can you explain it in a more reasonable way?"

"Of course. While the entrance to the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum is mysterious, it can be explained with natural laws, as long as you break the stereotype in your head and understand it from a higher dimension."

Li Linghai grabbed a piece of paper and two pens. She shook the paper and said, "Let's assume that this paper is a universe."

Then, she rolled the piece into a round paper ball.

"Now, it's a high-dimensional universe."

She then drew a black circle around the paper ball with one of the pens. "This is the trajectory of a certain object that is moving at an even speed in this high-dimensional universe. Look at the trajectory. It's clear and patterned. As long as you know the location of an object and its speed, you can calculate where it will be in the next second. For example, if I draw a red spot here, it will always move along with the black circle, right?"

"Yes."

Li Yao observed it carefully. The math was pretty easy even for elementary school students. But he still did not understand what Li Linghai meant. "So?"

"So…"

Li Linghai smiled and unfolded the paper ball. All the wrinkles were removed, and it was a piece of paper again.

On the piece of paper were countless black spots that used to be part of the black circle.

However, they were now sparse and disorganized, like the doodling of a little child.

The red spot, on the other hand, appeared on one of the black spots independently.

"What about now?"

Looking at Li Yao, Li Linghai asked, "Assuming that the moving pattern of the red spot does not change, can you still tell me where it's going to be next?"

Li Yao was lost for words.

Of course, he could not predict it.

But he more or less understood what Li Linghai meant now.

"The entrance to the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum is like this red spot. It has a fixed pattern of movement, but the pattern is not a three-dimensional one but comes from a higher dimension."

Li Linghai said, "When the pattern of the higher dimension is projected into our universe, it becomes the black spots on the paper that seem to be randomly distributed.

"At first look, the entrance to the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum is unpredictable and completely violates the laws of our universe, but actually, it has its own patterns, although we can barely conclude the patterns. After all, we're nothing more than ants on this piece of paper.

"Thankfully, the Blood God and the Supreme Emperor grasped part of the patterns with the files on the entrance to the mausoleum from the primeval age, so I can roughly estimate the range of the next black spot.

"That's right. Back in the primeval age, the ultimate experts of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan had grown interested in the entrance to the mausoleum. Of course, it was not a mausoleum yet, but more likely a super jump point."

"A super jump point?"

Li Yao asked, "What's that?"

"Normal space jumps are like this."

Li Linghai folded the piece of paper in halves.

"A super space jump, or a multiple space jump, is like this."

Li Linghai folded the paper again and again until it was only the size of her fingernail.

"You're saying that…"

Li Yao thought quickly and immediately understood it. "If we can fold the space again and again, we'll be able to jump tens of billions of lightyears away.

"Furthermore, with our current space jump technologies, we'll end up in the tiny pond that is the Pangu universe however we jump, but if we find the super jump point, we might be able to jump over the goddamn black wall into the multiverse outside?"

Everybody was shocked to hear that.

"Maybe."

Li Linghai said, "All in all, the mausoleum must've been unimaginably mysterious even for the primeval civilizations. That's why the primeval experts chose to bury themselves there. If your guess is correct, their decision would be reasonable. Because the mausoleum is a super jump point to the multiverse outside of the black wall, the ultimate experts of the primeval era had to defend it and would never allow anyone to jump out from there!"

Everybody was frightened as they listened to Li Yao and Li Linghai's conversation.

"Everything so far is just your speculation. It would be best if your theory could be confirmed by the intelligence from the Covenant Alliance."

Frowning deeply, Lei Chenghu sighed and asked, "We'll still have to fight in a dark fog if there's no detailed intelligence but only questionable speculations. Right, Black Wind King, what's the response of the Star Glory Federation? Will you send reinforcements to help us eliminate the rebels of the four families?"

"About that…"

Li Yao coughed and said, "You all know that the Star Glory Federation is a democratic country. It's an important decision whether or not to intervene in the civil war of the Immortal Cultivators, and the parliament is still discussing it. However influential I am back in the federation, I cannot stop the parliament from making a democratic decision, can I?"

Chapter 2699: Research Institute of Severe Mental Diseases 22 days ago

"Brother Yao, say no more. We all know that it's an outrageous request to ask the Star Glory Federation to assist the Imperium of True Human Beings."

Biting his teeth, Li Jialing said, "If any of us were the leader of the federation, we would barely be able to persuade the people to make such a risky decision.

"If the federation decides to ignore the conflicts at the center of the cosmos, that will be completely understandable.

"I can only say that if the federation does send their troops to the center of the cosmos, I'll always remember the friendship, try my best to protect their safety, and meet your demands. Anything will be negotiable.

"Besides, the reformists and the Star Glory Federation are on the same boat at this moment. Once the reformists collapse, whether the four families or the Covenant Alliance wins, they will definitely consider the federation their next target. Or even worse, if the Covenant Alliance finds the mausoleum and wakes up hundreds of primeval experts, it will be the doom of mankind. Can the Star Glory Federation survive it?

"The federation is too weak to impose influence on the center of the cosmos except right now! Now, you can play a critical role. It's a great opportunity for you. So—"

"Your Majesty, you do not have to be so yearning."

Lei Chenghu coldly interrupted Li Jialing and said, "Do the Immortal Cultivators really have to beg for the Cultivators for help? We can still defeat the rebels of the four families and the Cultivators without the Star Glory Federation! Even if the primeval experts are woken up, so what? We'll teach those people who should've died a lesson that we're no longer the slaves we used to be hundreds of thousands of years ago!"

"General Lei Chenghu, we should stop arguing between ourselves."

Li Yao clenched his fists and made a decision. "I cannot make a decision for the parliament of the federation. To be honest, I'm wavering too whether or not the soldiers of the federation should shed their blood at the center of the cosmos.

"However, I do have a solution about sneaking into the Covenant Alliance and acquiring critical intelligence.

"General Lei Chenghu, if I can get intelligence from the Covenant Alliance or even disrupt their pace of attack so that they will be slowed down for a couple of months, will you be able to finish off the four families in the meantime?"

"Why, of course!"

With dazzling brilliance in his eyes, Lei Chenghu snorted. "The main forces and the elites of the four families have been destroyed in the Battle of Seven Seas and the battle in the capital. The remainder of them is absolutely worthless. I can finish them easily as long as I have enough time!"

"But Brother Yao, how are you going to do it?"

Li Jialing asked earnestly, "Are you trying to resort to the Sunspot Plan that Wuying Qi 3.0 left?"

"Exactly."

Li Yao murmured, "It's highly risky, but we don't have a choice. Only if we get tremendous intelligence on the leadership of the Covenant Alliance, find out their plan, and even raise a riot within the Covenant Alliance, can we save the precious lives of the soldiers of the Imperium and the federation.

"To prevent the soldiers from dying miserably like the garrison of the Thick Soil Sector did, the plan is worth a try however risky it is!"

Li Yao smashed his fist on the table, leaving a deep dent on it.

The special meeting lasted a whole night. Lei Chenghu, Boss Bai, and other leaders of the military discussed the details of their march. Li Linghai and Li Yao exchanged more secrets on the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum, whereas the Fist King offered his input to the automatic defense system of the capital. When the meeting was over, the sun was already rising in the east.

Everybody left the royal palace with a heavy heart. Long Yangjun, however, invited Li Yao to her shuttle, claiming that she would take him to somewhere special.

"To the Association of Demon Hunters?"

Frowning, Li Yao said in the cold wind, "Forget it. I prefer to explore the secrets of the Gold Crystal Pyramid and figure out the details of the Sunspot Plan in the next couple of days.

"The sooner I get the intelligence on the leadership of the Covenant Alliance, the sooner I will grasp the pace of the Covenant Alliance's attack. Countless people will be saved!"

"If you're going to sneak into the Covenant Alliance to obtain intelligence and stir trouble, shouldn't you get a deeper understanding of the Covenant Alliance first?"

Long Yangjun said with a smile, "Come on. It won't be a waste of your time."

Li Yao knew that Long Yangjun was fond of making jokes, but she was trustworthy on critical matters. He pondered for a moment and got into Long Yangjun's shuttle.

The shuttle flew agilely in the early morning mist. Soon, it left the downtown area that was full of skyscrapers and moved all the way to the ocean, before it slowly landed on an island that was not far away from the shore.

Looking down at the island, Li Yao realized that the island was quite huge with many white buildings on it.

Li Yao had thought that it was some sort of oceanography institute, but when he noticed the high-voltage defense towers and the barbed wire fences, he thought it was a prison. However, when the shuttle finally landed, he saw "The Third Research Institute of the No. 7 Hospital of Imperial Medical University".

Li Yao knew that the Third Research Institute of the No. 7 Hospital of Imperial Medical University was one of the best hospitals in the country specializing in mental diseases.

Why had Long Yangjun brought him here?

"You know the No. 7 Hospital, right?"

Noticing his confusion, Long Yangjun smiled and said, "The Third Research Institute is a place that studies violent patients, but since a hundred years ago, it has also been studying the people of the Covenant Alliance. This is one of the best research centers on Covenant Alliance.

"You've seen a lot of people in Godly Might Prison, but they were all common people who were not worth studying. The senior commanders of the Covenant Alliance, as well as the captives whose brains are abnormal, have all been sent to places like the Third Research Institute.

"Actually, the Association of Demon Hunters has a similar research center, but our research level is not high, and we do not have the best specimen. So, I often come to the Third Research Institute to discuss with the specialists here and to elicit intelligence from the Covenant Alliance captives.

"Zhuge Jinglun, the director of this place, is my old friend. He's not only one of the best specialists on mental diseases in the Imperium but also an awesome Meditation Healer. Besides, he's a legitimate heir of the Ring-Tu School. You can ask him anything about the Covenant Alliance."

"An awesome Meditation Healer?"

Li Yao was briefly stunned. He knew about the Ring-Tu School, which was an organization of scholars who came up with a test to distinguish the people of the Covenant Alliance from normal people. "He still lives in the capital? Why was he not captured by Wuying Qi as fuel for the Next Sun Plan?"

"Hahahaha. Wuying Qi was not crazy. He did not catch all the Meditation Healers!"

Long Yangjun said with a smile, "Director Zhuge is a pure scholar, and his research is of paramount importance. Think about it, whoever assumes the power—Wuying Qi, the four families, the reformists, or even the Cultivators—has to deal with the Cultivators next, don't they? Specialists like Director Zhuge are indispensable in the war against the Cultivator, so no matter what happens in the capital, he's always safe. Even Wuying Qi never thought to harm him."

"It does make sense."

Li Yao thought for a moment and remarked, "You do have a lot of connections. Such a specialist is your 'old friend'?"

"Well, Director Zhuge's research institute has been half official and half private. It couldn't have been sustained without the donations from the four families."

Long Yangjun explained, "Dongfang Renxin, the former prime minister's elder brother, was interested in Director Zhuge's research. He even became a great sponsor for Director Zhuge after their academic discussion.

"At that time, I was appointed as the president of the Association of Demon Hunters, and I got to know Director Zhuge.

"A lot of violent patients were kept in Director Zhuge's place. Many of them had strong desires to kill because of their active demonic genes. In other words, the targets of the Association of Demon Hunters were the objects that Director Zhuge studied. Therefore, we communicated a lot.

"Also, I was personally interested in Director Zhuge's studies too. You know that I'm from the primeval age. Maybe Director Zhuge's studies on the Covenant Alliance will unravel more secrets in my brain? For that purpose, I maintained my contact with him and even advised him on the side he should take in the political conflicts in the capital. Otherwise, an unworldly scholar like him could've been killed!

"As time went on, Director Zhuge and I became good friends, and he always shared with me the results of his latest studies. Of course, most of them are purely academic and cannot be applied to real battles. But I think they might come in handy for your Sunspot Plan.

"So, let's go. I'll introduce you to Director Zhuge."

Chapter 2700: The Ring-Tu Test 22 days ago

They passed a square that was blocked by barbed wires. In the square, many patients wearing gowns of blue stripes were enjoying the sunlight. They all had metal rings on their ankles and their necks. There were also sensors on their heads that outputted the data on their brainwaves all the time.

Most of the patients were calm. Basked in the warm sunlight, they stared at the ocean far away.

But some of the patients, whose eyes were hollow and bloodshot, paced back and forth and looked at the people around them maliciously. Such patients were all separated by barbed wires so that they would be alone in their space.

When Li Yao and Long Yangjun passed by, some patients kept watching the ocean, some mumbled something to the strangers, and some were laughing and howling at them while crouching next to the barbed wires like beasts.

Li Yao and Long Yangjun seemed to be passing through a discredited zoo that was full of hungry beasts.

"Are they all from the Covenant Alliance?" Li Yao asked Long Yangjun in a low voice.

"Not really. Those are regular psychopaths."

Long Yangjun was amused by what she said. "However, the psychopaths that have been sent to Director Zhuge are hardly 'regular'. They are mostly nefarious criminals and murderers. Only those who murdered dozens of people consecutively, killed their whole families, or poisoned all the children in a school without feeling guilty are qualified to be caged here. They are the real scumbags, demons in human skin, and more like apes than humans. All in all, don't feel sorry for them at all. If you have read the files on the crimes that they committed, you would treat them ten times more cruelly than Director Zhuge does.

"Speaking of which, I kind of like those psychopaths. Normal people, bound by morals and society, often have to hide and even twist their real selves. The Cultivators have the Cultivators' morals, and the Immortal Cultivators have their own. Everybody's heart wears a mask, although the masks might have different appearances.

"Only those psychopaths, whose brains have been corrupted by extraterritorial devils and whose hearts have been liberated, can unleash their purest side without caring about anything. If Cultivation means finding out one's real self, those people would probably be great Cultivators.

"Did you know that when the solar storm Wuying Qi raised swept across the whole Empyreal Terminus Sector, few people in the Third Research Institute—both the patients and the doctors—were hardly affected? Some of the severest patients had no reaction even though they were directly exposed to the brainwashing waves.

"A person cannot die twice, and a collapsed building cannot collapse again. A patient whose mind has completely collapsed can't be brainwashed, can he?

"Comparing those psychopaths with the people of the Covenant Alliance is the greatest feature of the Third Research Institute. We always say that the people of the Covenant Alliance are inhumane lunatics, but we can hardly study them directly. We can only find another bunch of inhumane lunatics who are easier to find and study. By juxtaposing the two groups of lunatics and studying their similarities and differences, we can explore the mysteries of the Covenant Alliance with the psychopaths as a frame of reference. That's Director Zhuge's approach."

Led by Long Yangjun, Li Yao finally found Zhuge Jinglun, the mental specialist who had been staying away from politics.

Zhuge Jinglun was a typical devoted researcher. His hair was even messier than Li Yao's, and his filthy gown was stained with suspicious stains and fluids. There was no telling when he last washed his face because the wrinkles on his face were laden with dirt. He looked absolutely no different from the targets that he researched on outside.

Apparently, he was one of the researchers who were interested in nothing except their studies. No matter what happened outside or who became their boss, they wouldn't even bother to bat an eye.

Vulture Li Yao, the Black Wind King, was one of the most awe-inspiring names in the Imperium, but when Long Yangjun introduced Li Yao to him, he greeted Li Yao simply and rubbed his hands, not knowing what to say.

He was closer to Long Yangjun, probably because Long Yangjun had been offering him funding and specimens since she held the power of the Association of Demon Hunters again. His eyes glowed the moment he saw Long Yangjun. Ignoring Li Yao, he asked, "President Dongfang, you've come at the best moment. We've just improved the Ring-Tu Test, and the accuracy has been increased by more than 2.5%. Would you like to take a look?"

He talked so proudly as if he were a big boy who just invented a new game.

"A new-generation Ring-Tu Test? Of course I would."

Long Yangjun coughed and looked at Li Yao. "However, Director Zhuge, as I told you last time, 'Dongfang Mingyue' was a fake name that I used when I worked as a spy. My real name is Long Yangjun. You'd better call me 'President Long'."

"Of course, President Dongfang. Your name is Long Yangjun, right? Got it, President Dongfang!"

Zhuge Jinglun rubbed his hands in excitement. "This way please, President Dongfang and Black Wind King!"

Li Yao and Long Yangjun looked at each other before they followed Zhuge Jinglun into the test area on the third floor underground.

Bound by the Path of Ultimate Benevolence, the people of the Covenant Alliance did not have feelings, but to sneak into the Imperium, they could simulate feelings vividly.

The Ring-Tu Test was exactly a method in mental studies to distinguish real feelings from simulated feelings. It was also the last defense line for the Imperium to resist the spies of the Covenant Alliance.

For hundreds of years, the Covenant Alliance had been revising their simulation techniques and deceiving the Ring-Tu Test for generations.

The Imperium, on the other hand, had been upgrading the Ring-Tu Test too, trying to make it keener and more accurate, so that it could distinguish the spies of the Covenant Alliance that were hiding among the normal people.

Zhuge Jinglun brought Li Yao and Long Yangjun to a mirror whose back was transparent. On the other side of the mirror was a test room where the researcher and the test-taker were seated.

The researcher was a young man who was wearing a gentle smile.

The test-taker, on the other hand, was a ferocious middle-aged man who had a beer belly.

The researcher typed on his mini crystal processor and smiled at the test-taker now and then, indicating that he was well-trained.

The test-taker, however, was bound to his seat by metal ropes, agitated and uneasy. His hollow eyes emitted beastly light now and then while he growled.

The desk between the two of them was an enormous tablet where abstract patterns that looked like colorful swirls were changing splendidly all the time.

Illuminated by the colorful tablet, the cold wall and the white ceiling looked like the brain of a psychopath.

Those colorful patterns were exactly Ring-Tu.

"Sorry, but I cannot cancel those messy spots of light."

The researcher finally finished typing the last rune on his mini crystal processor. He rubbed his neck and smiled at the test-taker apologetically. "You're not dazzled, are you? Tell me, what do they look like?"

"What the f*ck is the meaning of this?"

Like a trapped beast, the test-taker asked warily, "Has the test begun? Is this part of the test and I have to say it? Huh?"

"No, no, no. Don't be anxious. Relax. Just relax. This is—"

The researcher opened his hands and said, "This is just a regular examination. You know that this is the best asylum of the Imperium, and you're lucky to be sent here with all the crimes you committed. However, we have to ensure that you do not harm other people. Therefore…

"Look, I don't know what those light spots and patterns are either. It's the work of the obnoxious people above me who think they're smart. My job is to turn on the switch and ask whoever sits before me questions every day. Then, you'll enjoy the sunlight and the ocean, and I'll go home after work. Simple as that. This is just my duty. Let's just get it over with, alright? Relax. Take a deep breath and be ready to answer questions. Okay?"

The test-taker murmured again and nodded unwillingly.

The researcher smiled and turned on another piece of mini magic equipment on the desk that looked like a combination of a magnifier and a telescopic sight. Through the device, the test-taker's eyeballs, as well as the reflections in them, could be clearly seen.

"What's that? What the f*ck is that?"

The test-taker grew anxious again.

"Relax. Relax. It's just an… unimportant gadget. I've been working in this room for three years, and I never care what it is exactly. Let's just ignore it and get going, shall we?"

Clearing his throat, the researcher asked, "The first question. Imagine that you see a green wasp on the back of your hand when you unintentionally raise your arm, what will you do?"
